- Files matching patterns in .gitignore are excluded
- Files matching default ignore patterns are excluded
- Files are sorted by Git change count (files with more changes are at the bottom)

/**
 * audit_era_purity.js
 * 华夏世家大系 · 第一赛季（汉魏·公元100—300年）时代纯净性自动化审计工具 (A3)
 * 依据规划附录 B 审计规则：
 * 1. 现代科技（电、手机、电脑、芯片、机床、软件、程序等）
 * 2. 现代金融与组织（公司、集团、银行、贷款、股票、证券、融资等）
 * 3. 现代交通（火车、飞机、地铁、高铁、公交等）
 * 4. 现代媒体（电视、报纸、广播、直播、互联网等）
 * 5. 现代制度（高考、职称、公务员、考公等）
 * 6. 现代货币与单位（元、块、¥、公里、米等）
 * 7. 越界年份（世界年 > 300）
 */

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// 允许在古汉语/历史语境下出现的词汇白名单（经史料复核备案）
const APPROVED_WHITELIST = new Set([
  '地动仪',   // 张衡候风地动仪
  '机巧',     // 百工机巧、心机
  '机杼',     // 织造机杼
  '枢机',     // 朝省枢机
  '机枢',     // 机枢重地
  '先机',     // 洞察先机
  '契机',     // 转变契机
  '机宜',     // 相机宜行
  '生机',     // 一线生机
  '良机',     // 错失良机
  '伺机',     // 伺机而动
  '机要',     // 军机机要
  '危机',     // 宗门危机
  '玄机',     // 暗藏玄机
  '相机',     // 相机行事
  '战机',     // 延误战机
  '气机',     // 调摄气机
  '神机',     // 神机妙算
  '机警',     // 敏锐机警
  '风电',     // 疾如风电、风驰电掣
  '雷电',     // 疾风雷电
  '电掣',     // 风驰电掣
  '电光',     // 电光石火
  '闪电',     // 掣同闪电
  '天网',     // 天网恢恢
  '罗网',     // 坠入罗网
  '网罗',     // 网罗英杰
  '渔网',     // 结渔网
  '蛛网',     // 尘封蛛网
  '元老',     // 开国元老、太勋元老
  '元年',     // 永汉元年、建安元年等
  '元勋',     // 开国元勋
  '元气',     // 固本培元
  '元初',     // 年号
  '元康',     // 年号
  '元嘉',     // 年号
  '元和',     // 年号
  '元帝',     // 汉元帝
  '元服',     // 加冠元服
  '米粮',     // 米粮、斗米
  '粟米',     // 粟米、米粟
  '五斗米',   // 五斗米道
  '米盐',     // 细如米盐
  '糙米',     // 糙米
  '粳米',     // 粳米
  '稻米',     // 稻米
  '块磊',     // 胸中块磊
  '土块',     // 聚土成块
  '石块',     // 投石击块
  '板块',     // 地理山川板块
  '公元100',  // 赛季年份
  '公元1',    // 100~300 年代
  '公元2',    // 200~300 年代
  '公元300'   // 赛季结卷年份
]);

// 违禁词表分类模式
const FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS = [
  // 现代货币与符号
  { category: '货币与符号', regex: /¥/g, desc: '现代人民币符号 ¥' },
  { category: '货币与符号', regex: /\d+\s*元(?!老|年|勋|气|初|康|嘉|和|帝|服)/g, desc: '现代货币单位 元' },
  { category: '货币与符号', regex: /\d+\s*块(?!磊)/g, desc: '现代口语货币 块' },

  // 现代度量衡
  { category: '度量衡', regex: /\d+\s*公里/g, desc: '现代公制单位 公里' },
  { category: '度量衡', regex: /\d+\s*千米/g, desc: '现代公制单位 千米' },
  { category: '度量衡', regex: /(?<!粟|米粮|糙|粳|稻|斗)米(?![粮粟])/g, filter: (match, fullText, idx) => {
    // 检查前后文，排除五斗米、米粮、糙米等
    const window = fullText.slice(Math.max(0, idx - 4), Math.min(fullText.length, idx + 5));
    for (const w of APPROVED_WHITELIST) {
      if (window.includes(w)) return false;
    }
    return /\d+\s*米(?!\s*粮|\s*粟)/.test(window);
  }, desc: '现代公制度量单位 米' },

  // 现代科技
  { category: '现代科技', regex: /(电脑|手机|互联网|软件|算法|芯片|人工智能|半导体|航模|微机|代码|程序猿|网购|充电|电池)/g, desc: '现代电子与信息技术' },
  { category: '现代科技', regex: /电(?!掣|光|气|雷|冰|石火|母|神)/g, filter: (match, fullText, idx) => {
    const window = fullText.slice(Math.max(0, idx - 2), Math.min(fullText.length, idx + 4));
    for (const w of APPROVED_WHITELIST) {
      if (window.includes(w)) return false;
    }
    return /(电话|电报|电子|电视|电影|电费|电厂|电机|电力|电梯|网)/.test(window);
  }, desc: '现代电力与电信' },

  // 现代金融与组织
  { category: '金融组织', regex: /(公司|集团|银行|证券|期货|股票|基金会|合伙人|破产清算|融资|天使轮|风投|外汇)/g, desc: '现代金融与企业组织' },

  // 现代交通
  { category: '现代交通', regex: /(火车|飞机|高铁|地铁|公交车|出租车|乘用车|大巴|客运站|飞机场)/g, desc: '现代交通工具' },

  // 现代媒体
  { category: '现代媒体', regex: /(电视|报纸|广播|直播|短视频|自媒体|博主|网易|央视|新华社)/g, desc: '现代传媒机构' },

  // 现代制度
  { category: '现代制度', regex: /(高考|考研|公务员|考公|职称评定|大专|本科生|少先队|少年宫|工会)/g, desc: '现代人事教育制度' },

  // 现代职场人称
  { category: '职场人称', regex: /(同事|老板|领导(?!\s*班子)|客户|销售额|KPI|PPT|汇报材料)/g, desc: '现代企业职场称谓与黑话' }
];

// 年份违规检测：文中出现 > 300 且不是已登记特殊ID的年份
function checkYearOutOfBounds(text) {
  const violations = [];
  const yrRegex = /(?:公元|西元)?([1-9]\d{2,3})年?/g;
  let match;
  while ((match = yrRegex.exec(text)) !== null) {
    const yr = parseInt(match[1], 10);
    // 忽略白名单与合理范围（100~300年）
    if (yr > 300 && yr < 1000) {
      violations.push({ text: match[0], year: yr });
    } else if (yr >= 1800 && yr <= 2100) {
      // 现代年份越界
      violations.push({ text: match[0], year: yr });
    }
  }
  return violations;
}

// 深度提取对象中的所有中文文本字符串
function extractAllStrings(obj, pathPrefix = '', bucket = []) {
  if (!obj) return bucket;
  if (typeof obj === 'string') {
    bucket.push({ path: pathPrefix, text: obj });
  } else if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
    obj.forEach((item, idx) => {
      extractAllStrings(item, `${pathPrefix}[${idx}]`, bucket);
    });
  } else if (typeof obj === 'object') {
    for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(obj)) {
      // 忽略不需要审计的代码内逻辑键名
      if (key === 'id' || key === 'statReq' || key === 'tag' && typeof val !== 'string') continue;
      extractAllStrings(val, `${pathPrefix}.${key}`, bucket);
    }
  }
  return bucket;
}

function runAudit() {
  console.log('====================================================');
  console.log('🏛️  华夏世家大系 · 第一赛季（汉魏·公元100—300年）时代纯净性审计');
  console.log('====================================================\n');

  const filesToAudit = [
    { name: 'historicalMilieuHanWei.js', path: path.join(__dirname, 'wechat-minigame/src/data/historicalMilieuHanWei.js') },
    { name: 'epochOneEngine.js', path: path.join(__dirname, 'wechat-minigame/src/data/epochOneEngine.js') },
    { name: 'childhood.js (Han-Wei packages)', path: path.join(__dirname, 'wechat-minigame/src/data/childhood.js'), filterS1Only: true },
    { name: 'familySystem.js', path: path.join(__dirname, 'wechat-minigame/src/data/familySystem.js') }
  ];

  let totalViolations = 0;
  let auditedItemsCount = 0;

  filesToAudit.forEach(fileInfo => {
    if (!fs.existsSync(fileInfo.path)) {
      console.warn(`[WARN] 找不到待审计文件: ${fileInfo.path}`);
      return;
    }

    console.log(`🔍 正在审计: ${fileInfo.name} ...`);
    let mod;
    try {
      mod = require(fileInfo.path);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`[ERROR] 载入模块失败: ${fileInfo.path}`, e);
      return;
    }

    // 针对 childhood.js 只审计汉魏序章包
    let dataToAudit = mod;
    if (fileInfo.filterS1Only) {
      dataToAudit = {
        EPOCH_ONE_CHILDHOOD_100: mod.EPOCH_ONE_CHILDHOOD_100,
        EPOCH_ONE_CHILDHOOD_168: mod.EPOCH_ONE_CHILDHOOD_168,
        EPOCH_ONE_CHILDHOOD_220: mod.EPOCH_ONE_CHILDHOOD_220
      };
    }

    const allStrings = extractAllStrings(dataToAudit, fileInfo.name);
    auditedItemsCount += allStrings.length;

    let fileViolations = 0;

    allStrings.forEach(item => {
      // 1. 违禁词检测
      FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS.forEach(rule => {
        let match;
        const re = new RegExp(rule.regex);
        while ((match = re.exec(item.text)) !== null) {
          const matchedWord = match[0];
          if (APPROVED_WHITELIST.has(matchedWord)) continue;
          if (rule.filter && !rule.filter(matchedWord, item.text, match.index)) continue;

          console.error(`  ❌ [${rule.category}] 违规词 "${matchedWord}" (${rule.desc})`);
          console.error(`     位置: ${item.path}`);
          console.error(`     文案: "${item.text.trim()}"\n`);
          fileViolations++;
          totalViolations++;
        }
      });

      // 2. 年份越界检测
      const yrViolations = checkYearOutOfBounds(item.text);
      yrViolations.forEach(v => {
        console.error(`  ❌ [年份越界] 文中出现越界年份 "${v.text}" (第一赛季边界为公元 100~300)`);
        console.error(`     位置: ${item.path}`);
        console.error(`     文案: "${item.text.trim()}"\n`);
        fileViolations++;
        totalViolations++;
      });
    });

    if (fileViolations === 0) {
      console.log(`  ✅ ${fileInfo.name} 审计通过：0 处违规词，0 处越界年份。\n`);
    } else {
      console.log(`  ⚠️  ${fileInfo.name} 发现 ${fileViolations} 处违规。\n`);
    }
  });

  console.log('----------------------------------------------------');
  console.log(`📊 审计完成汇总: 扫描字符串 ${auditedItemsCount} 处, 违规总数: ${totalViolations}`);
  console.log('----------------------------------------------------');

  if (totalViolations === 0) {
    console.log('🎉 时代纯净性审计全部通过 (0 违例)！符合附录 B 与质量门标准！\n');
    process.exit(0);
  } else {
    console.error(`💥 时代纯净性审计未通过，存在 ${totalViolations} 处违例，请整改！\n`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

runAudit();
</file>

<file path="balance_sim.js">
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * balance_sim.js — 《风华年代：沧海沉浮录》整局无头模拟 / 验收脚本
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node balance_sim.js <game.js 路径> [每赛道局数=3] [策略=strategic|random|skip|all] [--json out.json]
 *
 * 原理:
 *   1. 在 Node 里 mock wx / Canvas，把 game.js 复制到临时文件，末尾追加一段导出（不改原文件）。
 *   2. 每局: 创角 -> 逐年(或逐回合)推进 -> 遇到弹窗就用游戏自带的 resolveCurrentModalByPolicy 处理 -> 直到结局。
 *   3. 统计: 结局分布、S/A/B/C、抱负达成率、交互次数、估算时长、固定剧本占比、属性曲线、报错。
 *
 * 与游戏的契约(改造后请保持):
 *   - 必须存在全局函数: tickOneYear, resolveCurrentModalByPolicy, setRandomSeed,
 *     startCharacterCreation, evaluateEndgameArchetype, ensureLifeAmbition, 变量 state。
 *   - 若实现了回合包装器 runTurn(): 本脚本会优先调用它(见方案文档 §4.P1)。
 *     约定: runTurn() 返回后，若 state.activeModal 非空，表示需要玩家介入;
 *           否则表示本回合已跑完。被代办的事件记录在 state.turnDigest。
 *
 * 注意: 估算时长 = 玩家交互数 × 12s + 回合数 × 1.8s + 70s(开局+结局)。12s/次是假设值，请用真机计时校准。
 */
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');

const GAME = process.argv[2];
if (!GAME) { console.error('用法: node balance_sim.js <game.js> [每赛道局数] [策略] [--json out.json]'); process.exit(1); }
const PER_TRACK = parseInt(process.argv[3], 10) || 3;
const POLICY_ARG = (process.argv[4] && !process.argv[4].startsWith('--')) ? process.argv[4] : 'strategic';
const jIdx = process.argv.indexOf('--json');
const JSON_OUT = jIdx > -1 ? process.argv[jIdx + 1] : null;
const SECONDS_PER_INTERACTION = 12, SECONDS_PER_TURN = 1.8, SECONDS_FIXED = 70;

// ---------- mock 运行环境 ----------
const noop = () => {};
const ctx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => k === 'measureText' ? () => ({ width: 10 })
    : (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') ? () => ({ addColorStop: noop })
    : k === 'createPattern' ? () => ({})
    : (t[k] !== undefined ? t[k] : noop),
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});
const canvas = { width: 750, height: 1624, getContext: () => ctx };
let store = {};
global.wx = {
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 812, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 44, bottom: 778 } }),
  createCanvas: () => canvas, createOffscreenCanvas: () => canvas, createWebAudioContext: () => null,
  getStorageSync: k => store[k], setStorageSync: (k, v) => { store[k] = v }, removeStorageSync: k => delete store[k],
  vibrateShort: noop, vibrateLong: noop, onTouchStart: noop, onTouchMove: noop, onTouchEnd: noop, onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop, onHide: noop, onWindowResize: noop, onAudioInterruptionBegin: noop, onAudioInterruptionEnd: noop,
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 48, right: 365, bottom: 80, left: 278, width: 87, height: 32 }),
  getSystemInfo: noop, getAccountInfoSync: () => ({ miniProgram: { envVersion: 'develop' } }),
  reportEvent: noop, showShareMenu: noop, onShareAppMessage: noop, onShareTimeline: noop,
  getLaunchOptionsSync: () => ({ query: {} }), getUpdateManager: () => ({ onCheckForUpdate: noop, onUpdateReady: noop, onUpdateFailed: noop })
};
global.requestAnimationFrame = noop; global.canvas = canvas;
global.Image = function () {}; global.document = undefined;

// ---------- 生成带导出的临时副本 ----------
const src = fs.readFileSync(GAME, 'utf8');
const EXPORT = `
;global.__t = {
  get state(){ return state; },
  tickOneYear, resolveCurrentModalByPolicy, setRandomSeed,
  startCharacterCreation, finishCharacterCreation, evaluateEndgameArchetype, ensureLifeAmbition,
  triggerWellnessAction: (typeof triggerWellnessAction==='function'?triggerWellnessAction:null),
  openFrontlineEvent: (typeof openFrontlineEvent==='function'?openFrontlineEvent:null),
  openFamilyEvent: (typeof openFamilyEvent==='function'?openFamilyEvent:null),
  generateProceduralBirth: (typeof generateProceduralBirth==='function'?generateProceduralBirth:null),
  runTurn: (typeof runTurn==='function'?runTurn:null)
};
`;
const TMP = path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'balance_sim_game_' + process.pid + '.js');
fs.writeFileSync(TMP, src + EXPORT, 'utf8');
function fresh() { delete require.cache[require.resolve(TMP)]; store = {}; require(TMP); return global.__t; }

// ---------- 单局 ----------
const DECISION_FAMILY = id => id ? String(id) : '-';
function playLife(seed, policy, track) {
  const T = fresh(); const st = T.state; T.setRandomSeed(seed);
  let err = null;
  try { T.startCharacterCreation(false); } catch (e) { err = 'start:' + e.message.slice(0, 60); }
  try { T.generateProceduralBirth && T.generateProceduralBirth(); } catch (e) {}
  st.preferredTrack = track; st.isStarted = true; st.gameScene = 'game';
  let g = 0; while (st.activeModal && g++ < 5) { try { T.resolveCurrentModalByPolicy(policy); } catch (e) { err = err || 'fate:' + e.message.slice(0, 60); break; } }

  const decisions = []; const modalCount = {}; const samples = {};
  let interactions = 0, turns = 0, years = 0, stuck = 0, guard = 0;
  const useTurn = !!T.runTurn;
  while (guard++ < 400) {
    const a = st.age;
    if (st.activeModal === 'endgame') break;

    // 生命周期越权与数值边界断言
    if (st.age < 18) {
      if (st.family && st.family.spouseName) err = err || `minor_marriage_at_${st.age}`;
      if (st.investments && st.investments.realEstate > 0) err = err || `minor_realestate_at_${st.age}`;
      if (st.govLevel !== 0 || st.rankIndex !== 0) err = err || `minor_rank_${st.govLevel}_${st.rankIndex}`;
    } else if (st.govLevel !== st.rankIndex || st.govLevel < 1 || st.govLevel > 5 || st.lifetimeMaxRank < st.govLevel) {
      err = err || `rank_desync_${st.govLevel}_${st.rankIndex}_${st.lifetimeMaxRank}`;
    }

    let k = 0;
    while ((st.activeModal || st.pendingPhoneCrisis) && st.activeModal !== 'endgame' && k++ < 60) {
      const m = st.activeModal || 'phone';
      modalCount[m] = (modalCount[m] || 0) + 1; interactions++;
      if (m === 'decision' && st.activeDecisionEvent) decisions.push({ age: st.age, id: DECISION_FAMILY(st.activeDecisionEvent.id) });
      try { T.resolveCurrentModalByPolicy(policy); } catch (e) { err = err || ('modal ' + m + ': ' + e.message.slice(0, 70)); st.activeModal = null; st.pendingPhoneCrisis = null; }
    }
    if (st.activeModal === 'endgame') break;

    // 成年后玩家主动消耗 AP 执行日常履职与按需调养（skip 策略不执行）
    if (st.age >= 18 && policy !== 'skip') {
      if (policy === 'strategic' && (st.health < 50 || st.clean < 61) && st.ap > 0 && T.triggerWellnessAction) {
        T.triggerWellnessAction();
      }
      if (st.ap > 0 && (policy === 'strategic' || (policy === 'random' && st.age % 3 === 0))) {
        if (st.age % 2 === 0 && T.openFrontlineEvent) T.openFrontlineEvent();
        else if (T.openFamilyEvent) T.openFamilyEvent();
        let innerK = 0;
        while ((st.activeModal || st.pendingPhoneCrisis) && st.activeModal !== 'endgame' && innerK++ < 15) {
          const m = st.activeModal || 'phone';
          modalCount[m] = (modalCount[m] || 0) + 1; interactions++;
          if (m === 'decision' && st.activeDecisionEvent) decisions.push({ age: st.age, id: DECISION_FAMILY(st.activeDecisionEvent.id) });
          try { T.resolveCurrentModalByPolicy(policy); } catch (e) { err = err || ('modal ' + m + ': ' + e.message.slice(0, 70)); st.activeModal = null; st.pendingPhoneCrisis = null; }
        }
      }
    }
    if (st.activeModal === 'endgame') break;

    try { useTurn ? T.runTurn() : T.tickOneYear(); } catch (e) { err = err || ('tick: ' + e.message.slice(0, 80)); }
    turns++; years += Math.max(0, st.age - a);
    if (st.age === a) { stuck++; if (stuck > 12) break; } else stuck = 0;
    [20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70].forEach(x => { if (st.age >= x && !samples[x]) samples[x] = { cap: Math.round(st.capital || 0), rep: Math.round(st.rep || 0), clean: Math.round(st.clean || 0), hp: Math.round(st.health || 0) }; });
  }
  let arch = null, amb = null;
  try { arch = T.evaluateEndgameArchetype(st); amb = T.ensureLifeAmbition(st); } catch (e) {}
  // 非回合模式下,"回合数"按每 5 年折算,便于与回合版对比
  const turnsForTime = useTurn ? turns : Math.max(1, Math.round(years / 5));
  const secs = interactions * SECONDS_PER_INTERACTION + turnsForTime * SECONDS_PER_TURN + SECONDS_FIXED;
  return {
    req: track, track: st.track, endAge: st.age, cap: Math.round(st.capital || 0),
    iq: Math.round(st.iq), eq: Math.round(st.eq), cou: Math.round(st.courage), clean: Math.round(st.clean),
    rep: Math.round(st.rep), hp: Math.round(st.health),
    gaokaoScore: st.gaokaoScore || null, gaokaoTier: st.gaokaoTier || 'non-gaokao',
    gaokaoSchool: st.gaokaoSchool || '-', finalJob: st.jobTitle || '-',
    spouse: (st.family && st.family.spouseName) || '未婚',
    contactsCount: Array.isArray(st.contacts) ? st.contacts.length : 0,
    end: arch && arch.id, grade: arch && arch.grade, amb: !!(amb && amb.achieved),
    interactions, decisions: decisions.length, decisionIds: decisions, turns: turnsForTime,
    estMinutes: +(secs / 60).toFixed(1), err, samples, modalCount, delegated: (st.turnDigest || []).length
  };
}

// ---------- 汇总 ----------
const TRACKS = ['gov', 'tech', 'biz', 'trade', 'industry', 'military', 'agri', 'grassroots', 'art', 'med', 'law', 'education'];
const avg = (a, k) => a.length ? Math.round(a.reduce((s, r) => s + (+r[k] || 0), 0) / a.length) : 0;
const avg1 = (a, k) => a.length ? +(a.reduce((s, r) => s + (+r[k] || 0), 0) / a.length).toFixed(1) : 0;
const pct = (n, d) => d ? Math.round(n * 100 / d) + '%' : '-';
function hashTrackSeed(tr) {
  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < tr.length; i++) h = Math.imul(h ^ tr.charCodeAt(i), 16777619) >>> 0;
  return (h % 99991) + 17;
}

function runPolicy(policy) {
  const all = [];
  TRACKS.forEach(tr => { for (let i = 1; i <= PER_TRACK; i++) all.push(playLife(i * 9173 + hashTrackSeed(tr), policy, tr)); });
  const N = all.length;
  const grades = { S: 0, A: 0, B: 0, C: 0 }; all.forEach(r => { grades[r.grade] = (grades[r.grade] || 0) + 1; });
  const endings = {}; all.forEach(r => { endings[r.end] = (endings[r.end] || 0) + 1; });
  // 高考分数与分档分布统计
  const gaokaoScores = all.map(r => r.gaokaoScore).filter(x => typeof x === 'number');
  const gaokaoTiers = {}; all.forEach(r => { gaokaoTiers[r.gaokaoTier] = (gaokaoTiers[r.gaokaoTier] || 0) + 1; });
  // 固定剧本占比: 在 >=85% 的局里都出现的决策 id
  const idLife = all.map(r => new Set(r.decisionIds.map(d => d.id)));
  const idCnt = {}; idLife.forEach(s => s.forEach(i => { idCnt[i] = (idCnt[i] || 0) + 1; }));
  const fixed = new Set(Object.keys(idCnt).filter(i => idCnt[i] >= 0.85 * N));
  const totalDec = all.reduce((s, r) => s + r.decisionIds.length, 0);
  const fixedDec = all.reduce((s, r) => s + r.decisionIds.filter(d => fixed.has(d.id)).length, 0);
  const errs = {}; all.forEach(r => { if (r.err) errs[r.err] = (errs[r.err] || 0) + 1; });
  const curve = {}; [20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70].forEach(a => {
    const v = all.map(r => r.samples[a]).filter(Boolean);
    curve[a] = v.length ? { cap: Math.round(v.reduce((s, x) => s + x.cap, 0) / v.length), rep: Math.round(v.reduce((s, x) => s + x.rep, 0) / v.length) } : null;
  });
  const perTrack = TRACKS.map(tr => {
    const a = all.filter(r => r.req === tr); const g = { S: 0, A: 0, B: 0, C: 0 }; a.forEach(r => { g[r.grade] = (g[r.grade] || 0) + 1; });
    const gk = a.map(r => r.gaokaoScore).filter(Boolean);
    return {
      track: tr, real: [...new Set(a.map(r => r.track))].join(','), endAge: avg(a, 'endAge'),
      cap: avg(a, 'cap'), rep: avg(a, 'rep'), clean: avg(a, 'clean'), hp: avg(a, 'hp'),
      grades: `${g.S}/${g.A}/${g.B}/${g.C}`, amb: pct(a.filter(r => r.amb).length, a.length),
      gaokaoSamples: gk.length ? gk.join('/') : '职教/入伍/闯海/务农',
      endingSamples: [...new Set(a.map(r => r.end))].join(',')
    };
  });
  return {
    policy, lives: N,
    summary: {
      avgEndAge: avg(all, 'endAge'), interactionsPerLife: avg1(all, 'interactions'), decisionsPerLife: avg1(all, 'decisions'),
      turnsPerLife: avg1(all, 'turns'), estMinutesPerLife: avg1(all, 'estMinutes'), delegatedPerLife: avg1(all, 'delegated'),
      S_or_A_share: pct(grades.S + grades.A, N), grades, ambitionAchieved: pct(all.filter(r => r.amb).length, N),
      gaokaoScoreRange: gaokaoScores.length ? `${Math.min(...gaokaoScores)}~${Math.max(...gaokaoScores)} (均值 ${Math.round(gaokaoScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / gaokaoScores.length)})` : '-',
      gaokaoTiers,
      distinctDecisionIds: Object.keys(idCnt).length, fixedScriptIds: fixed.size, fixedScriptShare: pct(fixedDec, totalDec),
      variableDecisionsPerLife: +((totalDec - fixedDec) / N).toFixed(1), distinctEndings: Object.keys(endings).length, endings, errors: errs
    },
    curve, perTrack, fixedIds: [...fixed].sort()
  };
}

// ---------- 同条件同策略分叉度测试 (Divergence Test) ----------
function runDivergenceTest(runs = 20) {
  const results = [];
  for (let i = 1; i <= runs; i++) {
    results.push(playLife(880000 + i * 104729, 'strategic', 'gov'));
  }
  const gkScores = new Set(results.map(r => r.gaokaoScore).filter(Boolean));
  const endIds = new Set(results.map(r => r.end));
  const endAges = new Set(results.map(r => r.endAge));
  const grades = { S: 0, A: 0, B: 0, C: 0 };
  results.forEach(r => { grades[r.grade] = (grades[r.grade] || 0) + 1; });
  return {
    runs,
    distinctGaokaoScores: gkScores.size,
    distinctEndAges: endAges.size,
    distinctEndings: endIds.size,
    grades
  };
}

const policies = POLICY_ARG === 'all' ? ['skip', 'random', 'strategic'] : [POLICY_ARG];
const reports = policies.map(runPolicy);
reports.forEach(r => {
  console.log(`\n===== 策略 ${r.policy} | ${r.lives} 局 | runTurn=${!!global.__t.runTurn} =====`);
  console.log(JSON.stringify(r.summary, null, 1));
  console.log('赛道       实际赛道        终龄  资金       声望 清廉 健康  S/A/B/C  抱负  高考抽样分数');
  r.perTrack.forEach(t => console.log(t.track.padEnd(11), t.real.padEnd(14), String(t.endAge).padStart(3), String(t.cap).padStart(9), String(t.rep).padStart(5), String(t.clean).padStart(4), String(t.hp).padStart(4), ' ', t.grades.padEnd(8), t.amb.padEnd(5), t.gaokaoSamples));
  console.log('属性曲线(平均):', Object.entries(r.curve).map(([a, v]) => v ? `${a}岁 资金${v.cap}/声望${v.rep}` : `${a}岁 -`).join(' | '));
});
const divReport = runDivergenceTest(20);
console.log('\n===== 同条件同策略分叉度测试 (Divergence Test, 20 局 gov) =====');
console.log(JSON.stringify(divReport, null, 1));
if (JSON_OUT) { fs.writeFileSync(JSON_OUT, JSON.stringify({ reports, divReport }, null, 1)); console.log('\n已写入', JSON_OUT); }
try { fs.unlinkSync(TMP); } catch (e) {}

<file path="big_shot_sim.py">
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
大人物人生模拟器 (Big Shot Life Simulator) - 核心逻辑原型
"""
import random

class BigShotSimulator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.name = "林远舟"
        self.age = 0
        self.max_age = 83
        
        # 大人物核心维度
        self.vision = 50       # 战略眼界 (认知与时代嗅觉)
        self.courage = 50      # 枭雄魄力 (决断力、敢下重注的胆识)
        self.scheming = 50     # 权谋心术 (统御、制衡、人际政治)
        self.reputation = 20   # 时代声望 (社会公信力、历史地位)
        self.health = 95       # 生理健康
        self.capital = 0       # 资本积累 (人民币)
        
        # 人生阶位
        self.power_title = "草莽学子"
        self.family_background = ""
        self.talents = []
        self.chronicle = []    # 纪传体编年史
        self.family_status = {
            "spouse": None,
            "children": [],
            "dynasty_influence": 0
        }

    def init_birth(self):
        """随机出身与命格天赋"""
        origins = [
            ("三线小城工薪家庭", "虽无权财，但父母刚正坚毅，赋予了坚不可摧的底层逆袭决心", {"courage": +15, "health": +10, "capital": 5000}),
            ("江南书香望族余荫", "藏书万卷，自幼受教于政经名宿，博弈心术极深", {"vision": +20, "scheming": +15, "capital": 50000}),
            ("戎马军旅大院子弟", "作风铁血，说一不二，天生自带令人臣服的将帅威严", {"courage": +25, "reputation": +15, "capital": 30000}),
            ("沿海破产商人后代", "早历世态炎凉与催债屈辱，对金钱与危机有野兽般的直觉", {"vision": +15, "scheming": +20, "capital": 2000}),
        ]
        chosen = random.choice(origins)
        self.family_background = chosen[0]
        self.record_event(0, f"你降生于【{self.family_background}】。{chosen[1]}。")
        for k, v in chosen[2].items():
            setattr(self, k, getattr(self, k) + v)

        talent_pool = [
            ("【过目不忘】", "心智绝伦，对复杂信息与商业财报一眼洞穿", {"vision": +15}),
            ("【枭雄气场】", "天生具有令人信服的统御威严，下属誓死追随", {"scheming": +15, "courage": +10}),
            ("【时代弄潮儿】", "对历史大势的感知超越常人，总能押中关键风口", {"vision": +20}),
            ("【钢铁意志】", "哪怕跌入万丈深渊亦能绝地反击，心智坚不可摧", {"courage": +15, "health": +10}),
        ]
        self.talents = random.sample(talent_pool, 2)
        for t_name, t_desc, t_buff in self.talents:
            for k, v in t_buff.items():
                setattr(self, k, getattr(self, k) + v)

        t_str = "、".join([f"{t[0]}({t[1]})" for t in self.talents])
        self.record_event(0, f"觉醒命格天命：{t_str}。")

    def record_event(self, age, text):
        self.chronicle.append((age, text))

    def format_money(self, amount):
        if amount >= 100000000000:
            return f"{amount / 100000000000:.1f}千亿"
        elif amount >= 100000000:
            return f"{amount / 100000000:.2f}亿"
        elif amount >= 10000:
            return f"{amount / 10000:.1f}万"
        else:
            return f"{amount}元"

    def run_simulation(self):
        self.init_birth()
        
        # 历史宏大事件链 (涵盖时代破局、资本博弈、执掌产业、门阀布局)
        events_timeline = {
            7: ("启蒙与心性", [
                ("在私塾与少年宫通读《资治通鉴》与近现代战争史，早慧惊人，立下'大丈夫当如是'之宏愿", {"vision": +10, "scheming": +5}),
                ("在学校与街巷聚拢一批生死死党，小小年纪令众人心悦诚服，初显枭雄领袖气质", {"courage": +15, "reputation": +5})
            ]),
            18: ("青年立世：大学分流", [
                ("以全省理科前十考入最高学府微电子与理论物理系，立志'造国之重器以定乾坤'", {"vision": +15, "reputation": +10}),
                ("考入顶级法政与经管学院，结识未来一批核心部委与政商门阀的同窗挚友", {"scheming": +15, "vision": +10})
            ]),
            23: ("时代风口：第一桶金", [
                ("精准捕捉移动互联网与半导体浪潮，创立高端硬科技企业，连熬三个月研发出核心专利，一举破局", {"courage": +20, "health": -10, "capital": 25000000, "title": "科技新锐黑马"}),
                ("投身产业资本并购，利用高超对赌杠杆在初级市场连续阻击四起并购案，斩获数千万原始积累", {"scheming": +20, "courage": +15, "capital": 50000000, "title": "资本操盘新星"})
            ]),
            29: ("资本围剿与绝境反杀", [
                ("老牌跨国巨头联手本土资本发起恶意围剿，企图以三倍低价强制收购你的心血。你断然拒绝，抵押全副身家誓死对赌研发自主架构！", {"courage": +25, "vision": +20, "capital": 350000000, "reputation": +25, "title": "行业领军先锋"}),
                ("在商界死局中长袖善舞，引入国家级战略新兴产业基金与顶级央企背书，借力打力反向吞并对手，一战成名！", {"scheming": +25, "reputation": +30, "capital": 800000000, "title": "产融战略巨头"})
            ]),
            36: ("问鼎行业龙头 & 门阀联姻", [
                ("集团于港交所与纳斯达克双重敲钟上市，市值突破800亿！迎娶世家名门知性千金，强强联合奠定在京圈与沪上的核心人脉", {"reputation": +30, "capital": 12000000000, "spouse": "世家名门后裔·海外智库理事", "title": "商界巨擘"}),
                ("拒绝外资天价收购，果断斥巨资横向整合整条上游稀土与核心元器件产业链。与相濡以沫的创业并肩合伙人缔结婚盟", {"vision": +30, "courage": +25, "capital": 9500000000, "spouse": "实业财团联合创始人", "title": "民族工业脊梁"})
            ]),
            43: ("大国博弈：卡脖子攻坚战", [
                ("海外大国宣布实施最高级别单边技术制裁。你召开全集团两万人誓师大会，当众宣布：每年全额投入80%利润，哪怕连亏十年，也要砸穿高端光刻与核心芯片封锁！", {"vision": +35, "courage": +30, "reputation": +45, "capital": -3000000000, "title": "国之栋梁"}),
                ("在全球离岸金融中心暗中布设暗网影子供应链与海外离岸基金，通过穿透式股权在海外暗度陈仓，牢牢掌控全球产业命脉", {"scheming": +35, "capital": 38000000000, "reputation": +20, "title": "暗影财阀教父"})
            ]),
            51: ("登临绝顶：突破封锁与时代加冕", [
                ("历经九年卧薪尝胆，千亿研发终见奇迹！全国产自主制程超级芯片正式量产！打破西方三十年垄断！最高领袖亲自授予国家最高科学技术奖特等勋章！", {"reputation": +50, "capital": 88000000000, "title": "时代教父"}),
                ("重组跨国超级财团，横跨半导体、商业航天、深海探测与新能源，位列《财富》全球最具权势领袖前十位", {"vision": +30, "capital": 180000000000, "scheming": +25, "title": "全球产业寡头"})
            ]),
            59: ("世家开宗：九子夺嫡与权杖筹谋", [

                ("推行极其严苛的《家族百年宪章》，引入外部贤能共治模式与淘汰赛马制，彻底奠定跨越周期的世家统治力", {"dynasty_influence": 98, "scheming": +30, "title": "铁血家督"})
            ]),
            69: ("英雄暮年：健康报警与退居幕后", [
                ("因年轻时数十次危机攻坚过度透支心力，突发心脑血管危机，私人医疗专机与国医大师联手施救脱险，正式退居幕后，将帅印交接下一代", {"health": -35, "reputation": +15, "title": "隐世元老"}),
                ("在西湖侧隐居别苑静养，门生故吏遍布各部委高层与万亿企业CEO，重大国策出台前高层常有特使登门问策", {"health": -25, "scheming": +15, "reputation": +20, "title": "当代帝师"})
            ]),
            79: ("世纪回响：散尽千金与训诫子孙", [
                ("宣布将个人名下绝大部分股权无偿划归国家实验室与民生慈善信托，资助百万学子寒门出贵子，举国赞颂其'公忠体国，一代完人'", {"reputation": +40, "capital": -50000000000, "dynasty_influence": +20}),
                ("在家族宗祠亲手题写家训：'天下之本在国，国之本在家。不与民争微利，当为天下开太平'", {"reputation": +25, "dynasty_influence": +35})
            ]),
            83: ("巨星陨落：大风落幕", [
                ("于祖宅安详离世。灵车过处十里相送，多国政要致电吊唁。主流媒体头版通栏长篇生平：'深切缅怀这位改变了国家工业命运的时代伟人'", {})
            ])
        }

        while self.age <= 83:
            # 基础衰老机制
            if self.age > 45:
                self.health -= random.randint(1, 2)
            
            # 资本自然增长与分红
            if self.capital > 0:
                roi = random.uniform(0.04, 0.10)
                self.capital = int(self.capital * (1 + roi))

            if self.age in events_timeline:
                title, choices = events_timeline[self.age]
                # 随机或根据性格抉择
                chosen_option, buffs = random.choice(choices)
                
                self.record_event(self.age, f"【{title}】\n  决断：{chosen_option}")
                for k, v in buffs.items():
                    if k == "title":
                        self.power_title = v
                    elif k == "spouse":
                        self.family_status["spouse"] = v
                    elif k == "dynasty_influence":
                        self.family_status["dynasty_influence"] += v
                    elif hasattr(self, k):
                        setattr(self, k, getattr(self, k) + v)

            self.age += 1

    def generate_report(self):
        """生成大人物盖棺定论报告"""
        res = []
        res.append("*" * 64)
        res.append(f"  ★ 时代人物志：【{self.power_title}】{self.name} 之生平大传 ★")
        res.append("*" * 64)
        res.append(f"【出身背景】：{self.family_background}")
        res.append(f"【享年岁数】：83 岁 (寿终正寝，国士待之)")
        res.append(f"【巅峰身家】：约 {self.format_money(self.capital)}")
        res.append(f"【历史声望】：{self.reputation} / 100 (名列当代史册，青史留名)")
        res.append(f"【门阀底蕴】：{self.family_status['dynasty_influence']} / 100 (奠定家族百年基业)")
        res.append(f"【联姻伴侣】：{self.family_status['spouse']}")
        res.append("-" * 64)
        res.append("【一生波澜壮阔之编年史】:")
        for age, ev in self.chronicle:
            res.append(f"\n[岁在 {age:02d}] {ev}")
        res.append("\n" + "=" * 64)
        res.append("【太史公 / 时代盖棺定论】:")
        res.append(f"  赞曰：")
        res.append(f"  '{self.name}起于微末，而怀吞吐天地之志。在国之危难、技术封锁之秋，")
        res.append(f"   孤注一掷砸下千亿身家，破西方数十年垄断之局，成国之脊梁。")
        res.append(f"   对内整饬家风，创立百年门阀；对外散尽千金，福泽万千寒门。")
        res.append(f"   生执天下牛耳，没享万民敬仰。真当代国士，不世出之枭雄也！'")
        res.append("*" * 64)
        return "\n".join(res)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sim = BigShotSimulator()
    sim.run_simulation()
    print(sim.generate_report())

<file path="export_editions.js">
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

console.log('=== 《浮生万象》双版本自动化独立导出构建 ===');

const projectRoot = '/Users/leonour/development/人生模拟器';
const srcHtmlPath = path.join(projectRoot, 'index.html');
const rawHtml = fs.readFileSync(srcHtmlPath, 'utf8');

const distDir = path.join(projectRoot, 'dist');
const domesticDir = path.join(distDir, 'domestic');
const globalDir = path.join(distDir, 'global');

fs.mkdirSync(domesticDir, { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(globalDir, { recursive: true });

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. 构建国内合规版 (微信小游戏 / 抖音小游戏提审专属包)
// -------------------------------------------------------------
let domesticHtml = rawHtml;
// 固化默认版本为 domestic，并调整顶部展示
domesticHtml = domesticHtml.replace(
  'let currentEdition = localStorage.getItem("lifesim_edition") || "domestic";',
  'let currentEdition = "domestic";'
);
// 移除版本切换按钮，直接显示国内合规标志
domesticHtml = domesticHtml.replace(
  /<button id="btn-edition-toggle"[\s\S]*?<\/button>/,
  '<div class="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-700 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm">🛡️ 国内合规正式版</div>'
);
// 彻底清除国内包中残存的 global 写实配置字典，确保国内代码无任何敏感词残留
domesticHtml = domesticHtml.replace(
  /global:\s*\{[\s\S]*?phoneTitle:\s*"保密红机电话内线"\s*\}/,
  'global: null'
);

const domesticOutputPath = path.join(domesticDir, 'index.html');
fs.writeFileSync(domesticOutputPath, domesticHtml, 'utf8');
console.log(`✓ [国内合规版] 导出成功 -> ${domesticOutputPath} (${domesticHtml.length} bytes)`);

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. 构建海外写实硬核版 (Steam / 海外发布专属包)
// -------------------------------------------------------------
let globalHtml = rawHtml;
// 固化默认版本为 global，注入真实九级官阶天梯
globalHtml = globalHtml.replace(
  'let currentEdition = localStorage.getItem("lifesim_edition") || "domestic";',
  'let currentEdition = "global";'
);
// 替换为 Steam / 海外写实专版标志
globalHtml = globalHtml.replace(
  /<button id="btn-edition-toggle"[\s\S]*?<\/button>/,
  '<div class="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-700 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm">🌍 Steam / 海外硬核写实版</div>'
);

const globalOutputPath = path.join(globalDir, 'index.html');
fs.writeFileSync(globalOutputPath, globalHtml, 'utf8');
console.log(`✓ [海外写实版] 导出成功 -> ${globalOutputPath} (${globalHtml.length} bytes)`);

console.log('=== 双版本独立产物导出全部完成！===');
</file>

          <div class="text-right text-[11px] text-amber-300/80 pt-1">
            —— <span id="marriage-from-clan">河南·陈氏·颍川堂</span> 敬呈
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 space-y-1 mb-4 font-mono">

  <script>
    // =========================================================================
    // 浮生万象 (All Walks of Life) - 核心引擎与数据结构
    // =========================================================================

    // 0. 纯前端 Web Audio 拟真音效引擎 (无外部音频依赖，秒级合成)
    let audioEnabled = true;
    let audioCtx = null;

    function getAudioCtx() {
      if (!audioCtx) {
        const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
        if (AudioContext) {
          audioCtx = new AudioContext();
        }
      }
      if (audioCtx && audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
        audioCtx.resume();
      }
      return audioCtx;
    }


    // =========================================================================
    // 0.1 双版本发行架构核心引擎 (Dual-Edition Architecture)
    // =========================================================================

    
    // =========================================================================
    // 0.3 局内主动技能与决策韬略值引擎 (Active Skill & SP Engine)
    // =========================================================================
    function updateSkillsUI() {
      if (!state) return;
      if (typeof state.sp === 'undefined') state.sp = 35;
      state.sp = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, state.sp));

      const spValEl = document.getElementById("ui-sp-val");
      const spBarEl = document.getElementById("ui-sp-bar");
      if (spValEl) spValEl.innerText = state.sp;
      if (spBarEl) spBarEl.style.width = `${state.sp}%`;

      const skillCosts = {
        insight: 20,
        shield: 25,
        resolve: 30,
        network: 25,
        rest: 20
      };

      Object.keys(skillCosts).forEach(k => {
        const btn = document.getElementById(`skill-btn-${k}`);
        if (!btn) return;
        const cost = skillCosts[k];
        if (state.sp >= cost) {
          btn.classList.remove("opacity-50", "cursor-not-allowed");
        } else {
          btn.classList.add("opacity-50", "cursor-not-allowed");
        }
      });
    }

    function castSkill(type) {
      if (!state) return;
      if (typeof state.sp === 'undefined') state.sp = 35;

      const costs = { insight: 20, shield: 25, resolve: 30, network: 25, rest: 20 };
      const cost = costs[type] || 20;

      if (state.sp < cost) {
        playSoundEffect('alert');
        showStatToast(`韬略点不足！释放该技能需要 ${cost} SP，当前仅有 ${state.sp} SP！`, 'bad');
        return;
      }

      state.sp -= cost;

      if (type === 'insight') {
        state.insightBuff = true;
        playSoundEffect('fanfare');
        showStatToast("🔍【深度洞察】已激活！下次重大决策事件成功率直接提升 +30%！", "gold");
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【深度洞察】动用高维博弈韬略，全面透视当前全局因果，决策把握显著跃升！", "韬略洞察", "history");
      } else if (type === 'shield') {
        state.activeShield = true;
        playSoundEffect('fanfare');
        showStatToast("🛡️【铁壁防线】已展开！本年度全面免疫一次突发黑天鹅或负面冲击！", "gold");
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【防线坚固】构筑严密制度与业务防线，彻底杜绝违规穿透与暗箭中伤！", "未雨绸缪", "history");
      } else if (type === 'resolve') {
        state.rep += 25;
        state.courage += 15;
        playSoundEffect('stamp');
        showStatToast("⚡【雷厉风行】强力破局！声誉 +25，魄力 +15！", "gold");
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【雷厉风行】以排山倒海之势铁腕攻坚克难，强推关键项目落地！", "攻坚破局", "history");
      } else if (type === 'network') {
        ENSEMBLE_CHARACTERS.forEach(c => {
          c.trust = Math.min(100, c.trust + 10);
        });
        state.capital += 50000;
        playSoundEffect('fanfare');
        showStatToast("🤝【八面来风】动员人脉网络！全体群像好感+10，筹措应急资金5万元！", "gold");
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【八面来风】统筹各方资源朋友圈，各界友人纷纷伸出援手共襄盛举！", "人脉借势", "social");
        renderContacts();
      } else if (type === 'rest') {
        state.health = Math.min(100, state.health + 30);
        playSoundEffect('fanfare');
        showStatToast("🌿【闭门调息】身心修整！生理健康精力立即 +30，消除过度透支！", "good");
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【闭门调息】静坐深思调养生息，身心重归从容淡泊，精力焕然一新！", "修身养性", "social");
      }

      updateSkillsUI();
      updateHeaderUI();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 0.2 游戏可玩性与精通曲线引擎 (Playability & Mastery Engine)
    // =========================================================================

    // 1. 三大一键极速开局预设 (10秒快速上手)
    function quickStartArchetype(type) {
      if (type === 'pioneer') {
        selectCareerTrack('gov');
        selectFamilyOrigin('peasant');
        selectRegionalSetting('central');
        activeTalents = [
          { id: "iron", name: "铁面清正", desc: "清廉底线坚若磐石，防暗箭能力极高", rarity: "SSR", color: "bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-700" },
          { id: "clean_heart", name: "赤子初心", desc: "心怀人民，在群众评价与大考中深受拥护", rarity: "SR", color: "bg-sky-950 text-sky-300 border-sky-700" },
          { id: "popular", name: "深得民心", desc: "群众公信力极高，关键期总有百姓挺身支持", rarity: "SR", color: "bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border-amber-700" }
        ];
      } else if (type === 'tycoon') {
        selectCareerTrack('biz');
        selectFamilyOrigin('merchant');
        selectRegionalSetting('coastal');
        activeTalents = [
          { id: "writer", name: "时代弄潮", desc: "眼光极其毒辣，洞察全球经贸风口", rarity: "SSR", color: "bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border-amber-700" },
          { id: "iron", name: "经商奇才", desc: "资本运作敏锐，百折不挠敢为人先", rarity: "SSR", color: "bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-700" },
          { id: "family", name: "八面玲珑", desc: "商界朋友圈遍布天下，人脉资源极广", rarity: "SR", color: "bg-purple-950 text-purple-300 border-purple-700" }
        ];
      } else {
        selectCareerTrack('med');
        selectFamilyOrigin('scholar');
        selectRegionalSetting('capital');
        activeTalents = [
          { id: "writer", name: "妙手神针", desc: "临床直觉惊人，专克疑难罕见杂症", rarity: "SSR", color: "bg-sky-950 text-sky-300 border-sky-700" },
          { id: "scholar", name: "严谨求实", desc: "国际顶级期刊常客，科研实力名震学界", rarity: "SSR", color: "bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-700" },
          { id: "clean_heart", name: "大医精诚", desc: "医德高尚，深得患者及全院爱戴信任", rarity: "SR", color: "bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-700" }
        ];
      }

      document.getElementById("fate-wheel-modal").classList.add("hidden");
      confirmTalentsAndStart();
      playSoundEffect('fanfare');
      showStatToast("已极速生成天命开局，扬帆起航！", "gold");
    }

    // 2. 新官上任三板斧：新手引导锦囊任务体系
    function initOnboardingTasks() {
      state.onboardingTasks = [
        { id: "doc", title: "【初试锋芒】在案头审阅批复完成第一份公文签批", done: false, rewardText: "声誉+15, 魄力+10", applyReward: (st) => { st.rep += 15; st.courage += 10; } },
        { id: "social", title: "【广结善缘】与任意一位群像人物密谈交心或送礼", done: false, rewardText: "情商+15, 储蓄+1万", applyReward: (st) => { st.eq += 15; st.capital += 10000; } },
        { id: "eval", title: "【经受初考】在首个考核期保持清廉≥65且声誉≥45", done: false, rewardText: "大局+20, 组织特别表彰", applyReward: (st) => { st.iq += 15; st.rep += 20; } }
      ];
      renderOnboardingWidget();
    }

    function checkOnboardingTask(taskId) {
      if (!state.onboardingTasks) return;
      const task = state.onboardingTasks.find(t => t.id === taskId);
      if (task && !task.done) {
        task.done = true;
        task.applyReward(state);
        playSoundEffect('fanfare');
        showStatToast(`🎉 完成起步任务：${task.title}！获赠【${task.rewardText}】！`, 'gold');
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【履职新星】圆满达成初任起步目标：${task.title}！获组织特别奖励【${task.rewardText}】！`, "新官上任", "history");
        renderOnboardingWidget();
        updateHeaderUI();
      }
    }

    function renderOnboardingWidget() {
      const widget = document.getElementById("onboarding-widget");
      if (!widget || !state.onboardingTasks) return;

      const completedCount = state.onboardingTasks.filter(t => t.done).length;
      const tag = document.getElementById("onboarding-progress-tag");
      if (tag) tag.innerText = `进度: ${completedCount}/3`;

      // 全部完成且已过 26 岁，可淡出折叠
      if (completedCount === 3 && state.age >= 27) {
        widget.classList.add("hidden");
        return;
      }
      widget.classList.remove("hidden");

      const list = document.getElementById("onboarding-tasks-list");
      if (!list) return;
      list.innerHTML = "";

      state.onboardingTasks.forEach(task => {
        const item = document.createElement("div");
        item.className = task.done
          ? "p-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-700/60 text-emerald-300 flex items-center justify-between transition-all"
          : "p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 text-slate-300 flex items-center justify-between hover:border-amber-600/50 transition-all";
        item.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate">
            <span>${task.done ? '✅' : '⏳'}</span>
            <span class="truncate text-xs font-medium">${task.title}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="text-xs px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded shrink-0 font-mono font-bold ${task.done ? 'bg-emerald-900 text-emerald-200' : 'bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800'}">
            ${task.done ? '已领奖' : task.rewardText}
          </span>
        `;
        list.appendChild(item);
      });
    }

    // 3. 深度策略流派共鸣光环系统 (Build Synergies)
    function updateArchetypeAura() {
      const banner = document.getElementById("archetype-aura-banner");
      if (!banner) return;

      let aura = null;
      if (state.courage >= 80 && state.strategy === 'aggressive') {
        aura = {
          icon: "🚀",
          title: "【改革闯将流】共鸣生效",
          desc: "魄力超群！年度业绩与声望暴击率+50%，急难险重事件考核破格加分！",
          buff: () => { state.rep += 2; state.courage += 1; }
        };
      } else if (state.eq >= 80 && state.clean >= 80) {
        aura = {
          icon: "🤝",
          title: "【长袖清风流】共鸣生效",
          desc: "德才兼备！跨部门协同阻力清零，巡检质效考核必定全优通过！",
          buff: () => { state.clean += 2; state.eq += 1; }
        };
      } else if (state.iq >= 80 && state.capital >= 150000) {
        aura = {
          icon: "💡",
          title: "【实业资本流】共鸣生效",
          desc: "智谋兼备！产业自研转化收益翻倍，对冲宏观黑天鹅抗风险能力极强！",
          buff: () => { state.capital += 15000; state.iq += 1; }
        };
      }

      state.activeAura = aura;
      if (aura) {
        banner.classList.remove("hidden");
        const iconEl = document.getElementById("aura-icon");
        if (iconEl) iconEl.innerText = aura.icon;
        const titleEl = document.getElementById("aura-title");
        if (titleEl) titleEl.innerText = aura.title;
        const descEl = document.getElementById("aura-desc");
        if (descEl) descEl.innerText = aura.desc;
        aura.buff();
      } else {
        banner.classList.add("hidden");
      }
    }

    // 4. 年度履职组织考评单 (S/A/B/C 结构化评估反馈)
    function evaluateYearlyAppraisal() {
      let score = 50;
      score += Math.min(25, (state.clean - 50) * 0.5);
      score += Math.min(25, (state.rep - 40) * 0.4);
      score += (state.health >= 70 ? 10 : (state.health < 40 ? -15 : 0));
      if (state.activeAura) score += 10;

      let grade = "A";
      let gradeBadgeClass = "bg-sky-950 text-sky-300 border-sky-700";
      let feedback = "";

      if (score >= 80) {
        grade = "S 卓越";
        gradeBadgeClass = "bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border-amber-500 shadow-sm";
        state.rep += 8;
        state.courage += 3;
        feedback = "🌟 本年度攻坚克难成绩斐然，获全区通报表扬！组织重点关注，声誉+8、魄力+3！";
      } else if (score >= 60) {
        grade = "A 称职";
        gradeBadgeClass = "bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-700";
        feedback = "✓ 扎实稳健履职，各分管线条均按期保质达标，保持良好的成长梯队排位。";
      } else if (score >= 45) {
        grade = "B 基本称职";
        gradeBadgeClass = "bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-700";
        feedback = "⚠️ 履职平稳，但需注意补齐短板指标（建议优化公文审阅与强化廉洁清廉底线）。";
      } else {
        grade = "C 待改进";
        gradeBadgeClass = "bg-rose-950 text-rose-300 border-rose-800";
        feedback = "🚨 身心健康或作风指标亮红灯，务必及时调整策略，防范透支与违规风险！";
      }

      // 记录最新年度考评结果并内嵌呈现在主界面（绝不弹窗打断）
      state.lastAppraisal = { grade, gradeBadgeClass, feedback };
      const appraisalPill = document.getElementById("ui-appraisal-pill");
      if (appraisalPill) {
        appraisalPill.innerHTML = `<span>📋 考评:</span> <span class="${gradeBadgeClass} px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-mono font-bold">${grade.split(' ')[0]}</span>`;
        appraisalPill.classList.remove("hidden");
      }
      if (state.age % 3 === 0) {
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【年度考评】综合评定为【${grade}】。详情：${feedback}`, "绩效考评", "promo");
        showStatToast(`📋 年度考评结果出炉：【${grade}】！`, grade.startsWith('S') ? 'gold' : 'good');
      }
    }

    function openYearlyAppraisalModal() {
      if (!state.lastAppraisal) return;
      const { grade, gradeBadgeClass, feedback } = state.lastAppraisal;
      pauseTimeline();
      const modal = document.getElementById("yearly-appraisal-modal");
      if (!modal) return;
      const tEl = document.getElementById("appraisal-modal-title");
      if (tEl) tEl.innerText = `【${state.year}年度 · ${state.age}岁】组织履职考评报告`;
      const badge = document.getElementById("appraisal-grade-badge");
      if (badge) {
        badge.innerText = grade;
        badge.className = `text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold font-mono border ${gradeBadgeClass}`;
      }
      const cEl = document.getElementById("appraisal-modal-content");
      if (cEl) cEl.innerText = `在过去一年中，你在【${state.jobTitle}】岗位上统筹推进重点工作，综合治理考评得分已封存归档。`;
      const fBox = document.getElementById("appraisal-feedback-box");
      if (fBox) {
        fBox.innerHTML = `
          <div class="font-bold text-slate-200">综合督办考评鉴定结论：</div>
          <div class="leading-relaxed text-amber-200/90">${feedback}</div>
          <div class="pt-1.5 border-t border-slate-800 text-xs text-slate-400 flex justify-between">
            <span>当前清廉指数: ${state.clean}/100</span>
            <span>群众声誉: ${state.rep}</span>
            <span>生理精力: ${state.health}/100</span>
          </div>
        `;
      }
      modal.classList.remove("hidden");
      playSoundEffect('fanfare');
    }

    function showYearlyAppraisalModal_DEPRECATED(grade, badgeClass, feedback) {
      pauseTimeline();
      const modal = document.getElementById("yearly-appraisal-modal");
      if (!modal) return;
      document.getElementById("appraisal-modal-title").innerText = `【${state.year}年度 · ${state.age}岁】组织履职考评报告`;
      const badge = document.getElementById("appraisal-grade-badge");
      badge.innerText = grade;
      badge.className = `text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold font-mono border ${badgeClass}`;
      document.getElementById("appraisal-modal-content").innerText = `在过去一年中，你在【${state.jobTitle}】岗位上统筹推进重点工作，综合治理考评得分已封存归档。`;
      document.getElementById("appraisal-feedback-box").innerHTML = `
        <div class="font-bold text-slate-200">综合督办考评鉴定结论：</div>
        <div class="leading-relaxed text-amber-200/90">${feedback}</div>
        <div class="pt-1.5 border-t border-slate-800 text-xs text-slate-400 flex justify-between">
          <span>当前清廉指数: ${state.clean}/100</span>
          <span>群众声誉: ${state.rep}</span>
          <span>生理精力: ${state.health}/100</span>
        </div>
      `;
      modal.classList.remove("hidden");
      playSoundEffect('fanfare');
    }

    function closeYearlyAppraisalModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById("yearly-appraisal-modal");
      if (modal) modal.classList.add("hidden");
      playSoundEffect('click');
    }

    // 5. 时代功勋勋章图鉴 (20项成就挑战)
    const ACHIEVEMENTS_LIST = [

      { id: "friends_everywhere", title: "🌟 莫逆满天下", desc: "与至少3位群像人物达成【莫逆之交】", check: (st) => ENSEMBLE_CHARACTERS.filter(c => c.trust >= 85).length >= 3 },

      { id: "grand_architect", title: "🏆 卓越擘画", desc: "登顶国家级示范区总指挥或协同发展总规划师", check: (st) => (st.govLevel >= 9 || st.bizLevel >= 9) }

    function checkAchievements() {
      if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
      const unlocked = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("lifesim_unlocked_achievements") || "[]");
      let newlyUnlocked = false;

      ACHIEVEMENTS_LIST.forEach(ach => {
        if (!unlocked.includes(ach.id) && ach.check(state)) {
          unlocked.push(ach.id);
          newlyUnlocked = true;
          showStatToast(`🏆 达成成就：【${ach.title}】！`, 'gold');
        }
      });

      if (newlyUnlocked) {
        localStorage.setItem("lifesim_unlocked_achievements", JSON.stringify(unlocked));
      }
    }

    function openAchievementsModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById("achievements-modal");
      if (!modal) return;

      const unlocked = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("lifesim_unlocked_achievements") || "[]");
      const countEl = document.getElementById("achieve-unlock-count");
      if (countEl) countEl.innerText = `${unlocked.length} / ${ACHIEVEMENTS_LIST.length}`;

      const list = document.getElementById("achievements-list");
      list.innerHTML = "";

      ACHIEVEMENTS_LIST.forEach(ach => {
        const isDone = unlocked.includes(ach.id);
        const card = document.createElement("div");
        card.className = isDone
          ? "p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/40 border border-amber-600/70 flex items-center justify-between transition-all"
          : "p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 text-slate-500 flex items-center justify-between opacity-60";
        card.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="text-xl ${isDone ? '' : 'grayscale'}">${ach.title.split(' ')[0]}</span>
            <div>
              <div class="font-bold text-xs ${isDone ? 'text-amber-300' : 'text-slate-400'}">${ach.title}</div>
              <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">${ach.desc}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <span class="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono font-bold ${isDone ? 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'}">
            ${isDone ? '已达成 🏆' : '未解锁 🔒'}
          </span>
        `;
        list.appendChild(card);
      });

      modal.classList.remove("hidden");
      playSoundEffect('click');
    }

    function closeAchievementsModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById("achievements-modal");
      if (modal) modal.classList.add("hidden");
      playSoundEffect('click');
    }

    // 6. 传世家风多周目继承体系 (Family Legacy)
    function applyFamilyLegacyBonus() {
      if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
      const legacyTier = localStorage.getItem("lifesim_legacy_tier");
      if (legacyTier) {
        if (legacyTier === 'noble') {
          state.iq += 8; state.clean += 5; state.capital += 30000;
          showStatToast("🌟 激活【诗礼名门】家风传承：智商+8，清廉+5，初始储蓄+3万！", "gold");
        } else if (legacyTier === 'tycoon') {
          state.capital += 100000; state.courage += 6;
          showStatToast("💰 激活【富甲一方】家风传承：初始资金额外+10万，魄力+6！", "gold");
        } else if (legacyTier === 'hero') {
          state.rep += 15; state.courage += 10; state.clean += 10;
          showStatToast("🏛️ 激活【功勋传世】家风传承：初始声誉+15，魄力+10，清廉+10！", "gold");
        }
      }
    }

    function saveEndgameLegacy() {
      if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
      let tier = "common";
      if (state.govLevel >= 7 || state.bizLevel >= 7) {
        tier = "hero";
      } else if (state.capital >= 5000000) {
        tier = "tycoon";
      } else if (state.rep >= 70 && state.clean >= 80) {
        tier = "noble";
      }
      localStorage.setItem("lifesim_legacy_tier", tier);
    }

        let currentEdition = "domestic"; // "domestic" | "global"

    function applyGameEdition(edition) {
      currentEdition = edition;
      localStorage.setItem("lifesim_edition", edition);
      const conf = EDITION_CONFIGS[edition];

      // Update ladder in TRACK_CONFIGS
      if (TRACK_CONFIGS && TRACK_CONFIGS.gov) {
        TRACK_CONFIGS.gov.ladder = conf.govLadder;
        TRACK_CONFIGS.gov.title = conf.trackName;
      }

      // Update Header Button
      const btn = document.getElementById("btn-edition-toggle");
      const iconEl = document.getElementById("edition-icon");
      const textEl = document.getElementById("edition-text");
      if (iconEl) iconEl.innerText = conf.icon;
      if (textEl) textEl.innerText = conf.badge;
      if (btn) {
        btn.className = edition === "domestic"
          ? "text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-950 hover:bg-emerald-900 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-700 font-bold transition-all shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1"
          : "text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-indigo-950 hover:bg-indigo-900 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-700 font-bold transition-all shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1";
      }

      // Update Disclaimer Banner if present
      const banner = document.getElementById("edition-disclaimer-banner");
      if (banner) {
        banner.innerText = conf.disclaimer;
      }

      // Refresh UI if game is active
      if (state && state.track === 'gov') {
        const ladderIdx = Math.min(conf.govLadder.length - 1, state.govLevel || 0);
        state.rankName = conf.govLadder[ladderIdx].rank;
        state.jobTitle = conf.govLadder[ladderIdx].job;
        updateHeaderUI();
      }
    }

    function toggleGameEdition() {
      const next = currentEdition === "domestic" ? "global" : "domestic";
      applyGameEdition(next);
      playSoundEffect('click');
      showStatToast(`已切换为：【${EDITION_CONFIGS[next].name}】`, 'gold');
    }

        function toggleAudioMute() {
      audioEnabled = !audioEnabled;
      const btn = document.getElementById("btn-sound-toggle");
      if (btn) {
        btn.innerText = audioEnabled ? "🔊" : "🔇";
        btn.className = audioEnabled 
          ? "text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400 font-mono font-bold"
          : "text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-red-950 hover:bg-red-900 text-slate-400 border border-red-800 font-mono font-bold";
      }
      if (audioEnabled) playSoundEffect('click');
    }

    const _webPcmCache = {};
    let _webMasterBus = null;
    let _lastWebTonalMs = 0;
    let _webHarmonicStep = 0;
    const _WEB_PENTATONIC = [261.63, 293.66, 329.63, 392.00, 440.00, 523.25, 587.33, 659.25, 783.99, 880.00];

    function _getWebHarmFreq(deg, oct = 1) {
      const idx = ((Math.round(deg) % _WEB_PENTATONIC.length) + _WEB_PENTATONIC.length) % _WEB_PENTATONIC.length;
      return _WEB_PENTATONIC[idx] * oct;
    }

    function _getWebMasterBus(ctx) {
      if (_webMasterBus && _webMasterBus._ctx === ctx) return _webMasterBus;
      try {
        const inGain = ctx.createGain();
        inGain.gain.value = 0.86;
        inGain._ctx = ctx;

        // 丝绸柔化低通滤波器（6400Hz），滤除毛刺感，使所有音色温润细腻
        const silkLp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        silkLp.type = 'lowpass';
        silkLp.frequency.value = 6400;
        silkLp.Q.value = 0.5;
        inGain.connect(silkLp);

        const dry = ctx.createGain();
        dry.gain.value = 0.78;
        silkLp.connect(dry);
        dry.connect(ctx.destination);

        if (typeof ctx.createConvolver === 'function') {
          const sr = ctx.sampleRate || 44100;
          const len = Math.floor(sr * 2.1);
          const ir = ctx.createBuffer(2, len, sr);
          for (let c = 0; c < 2; c++) {
            const d = ir.getChannelData(c);
            let lp = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
              const sec = i / sr;
              lp = lp * 0.76 + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.24;
              d[i] = lp * Math.pow(1 - i / len, 2.2) * Math.exp(-sec * 1.65) * 0.36;
            }
          }
          const conv = ctx.createConvolver();
          conv.buffer = ir;
          const wet = ctx.createGain();
          wet.gain.value = 0.36;
          silkLp.connect(conv);
          conv.connect(wet);
          wet.connect(ctx.destination);
        }
        _webMasterBus = inGain;
        return inGain;
      } catch (e) {
        return ctx.destination;
      }
    }

    function _getWebPcmBuffer(ctx, key, dur, fillFn) {
      const sr = ctx.sampleRate || 44100;
      const k = key + '_' + sr;
      if (_webPcmCache[k]) return _webPcmCache[k];
      const len = Math.max(64, Math.floor(sr * dur));
      const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, sr);
      fillFn(buf.getChannelData(0), len, sr);
      _webPcmCache[k] = buf;
      return buf;
    }

    function _getWebKSString(ctx, freq, dur, isGuqin) {
      const f = Math.round(freq * 10) / 10;
      return _getWebPcmBuffer(ctx, `ks_${isGuqin ? 'q' : 'z'}_${f}`, dur, (out, len, sr) => {
        const period = Math.max(8, Math.round(sr / f));
        const ring = new Float32Array(period);
        const pickPos = isGuqin ? Math.floor(period * 0.32) : Math.max(2, Math.floor(period * 0.18));
        for (let i = 0; i < period; i++) {
          const p = i / period;
          const tri = Math.sin(Math.PI * p) * (p < 0.32 ? (p / 0.32) : (1.0 - (p - 0.32) / 0.68));
          const rand = (Math.sin(i * 12.9898 + f) * 43758.5453 % 1) * 2 - 1;
          ring[i] = tri * 0.76 + rand * (isGuqin ? 0.12 : 0.20);
        }
        for (let i = period - 1; i >= pickPos; i--) ring[i] -= 0.38 * ring[i - pickPos];
        const passes = isGuqin ? 4 : 2;
        for (let s = 0; s < passes; s++) {
          let prev = ring[period - 1];
          for (let i = 0; i < period; i++) {
            const c = ring[i];
            ring[i] = 0.25 * prev + 0.5 * c + 0.25 * ring[(i + 1) % period];
            prev = c;
          }
        }
        const loss = isGuqin ? 0.9945 : 0.9968;
        const blend = isGuqin ? 0.48 : 0.34;
        let b1 = 0, b2 = 0, ptr = 0;
        const attSamples = Math.max(48, Math.floor(sr * (isGuqin ? 0.0045 : 0.0028)));
        for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
          const next = (ptr + 1) % period;
          const cur = ring[ptr];
          ring[ptr] = loss * ((1 - blend) * cur + blend * ring[next]);
          ptr = next;
          b1 = 0.96 * b1 + cur * 0.04;
          b2 = 0.85 * b2 + (cur - b1) * 0.15;
          const env = i < attSamples ? (0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(Math.PI * (i / attSamples))) : Math.exp(-i / (sr * (dur * 0.62)));
          out[i] = (cur * 0.74 + b2 * 0.34) * env;
        }
      });
    }

    function playSoundEffect(type) {
      if (!audioEnabled) return;
      try {
        const ctx = getAudioCtx();
        if (!ctx) return;
        const now = ctx.currentTime;
        const bus = _getWebMasterBus(ctx);
        const nowMs = Date.now();
        const isFoley = (type === 'click' || type === 'paper' || type === 'stamp');
        let schedDelay = 0;
        let degShift = 0;
        if (!isFoley) {
          if (nowMs - _lastWebTonalMs < 240) {
            _webHarmonicStep = (_webHarmonicStep + 1) % 3;
            schedDelay = _webHarmonicStep * 0.20;
            degShift = _webHarmonicStep * 2;
          } else {
            _webHarmonicStep = 0;
          }
          _lastWebTonalMs = nowMs;
        }
        const playBuf = (buf, vol, delay = 0, jitter = 0) => {
          if (!buf) return;
          const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
          src.buffer = buf;
          if (jitter && src.playbackRate) {
            src.playbackRate.setValueAtTime(1.0 + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * jitter, now + delay);
          }
          const g = ctx.createGain();
          g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, now + delay);
          src.connect(g);
          g.connect(bus);
          src.start(now + delay);
        };
        const playFluteOrPad = (freq, dur, vol, delay = 0, isFlute = false) => {
          const t0 = now + delay;
          const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
          const osc2 = ctx.createOscillator();
          const flt = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
          const g = ctx.createGain();
          osc.type = isFlute ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
          osc2.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq * (isFlute ? 1.016 : 0.988), t0);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freq, t0 + 0.045);
          osc2.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq * 2.0, t0);
          if (isFlute) {
            const lfo = ctx.createOscillator();
            const lfoG = ctx.createGain();
            lfo.frequency.value = 5.2;
            lfoG.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, t0);
            lfoG.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(freq * 0.008, t0 + dur * 0.45);
            lfo.connect(lfoG);
            lfoG.connect(osc.frequency);
            lfo.start(t0);
            lfo.stop(t0 + dur + 0.05);
          }
          flt.type = 'lowpass';
          flt.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.min(4800, freq * 3.8), t0);
          g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0);
          g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol, t0 + 0.038);
          g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + dur);
          const g2 = ctx.createGain();
          g2.gain.value = 0.25;
          osc.connect(flt);
          osc2.connect(g2);
          g2.connect(flt);
          flt.connect(g);
          g.connect(bus);
          osc.start(t0);
          osc2.start(t0);
          osc.stop(t0 + dur + 0.05);
          osc2.stop(t0 + dur + 0.05);
        };

        if (type === 'stamp') {
          const buf = _getWebPcmBuffer(ctx, 'seal_soft', 0.14, (out, len, sr) => {
            let prev = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
              const t = i / sr;
              const n = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) - prev * 0.65;
              prev = n;
              const slap = t < 0.005 ? n * Math.exp(-t * 480) * 0.36 : 0;
              const d1 = Math.exp(-t * 36) * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 108 * t) * 0.66;
              const d2 = Math.exp(-t * 78) * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 224 * t) * 0.28;
              out[i] = Math.tanh((slap + d1 + d2) * 1.15) * 0.76;
            }
          });
          playBuf(buf, 0.30, 0, 0.015);
          playFluteOrPad(_getWebHarmFreq(0, 0.5), 1.25, 0.06, 0.025, false);
          const app = document.getElementById("app-container");
          if (app) {
            app.classList.remove("shake-slam");
            void app.offsetWidth;
            app.classList.add("shake-slam");
          }
        } else if (type === 'phone') {
          const f1 = _getWebHarmFreq(5 + degShift, 2.0);
          const f2 = _getWebHarmFreq(7 + degShift, 2.0);
          const b1 = _getWebPcmBuffer(ctx, 'phone_harm_' + Math.round(f1), 0.18, (out, len, sr) => {
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
              const t = i / sr;
              const att = t < 0.003 ? t / 0.003 : 1.0;
              out[i] = att * Math.exp(-t * 16) * (0.58 * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f1 * t) + 0.32 * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f2 * t));
            }
          });
          for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) playBuf(b1, 0.13, schedDelay + i * 0.055, 0.008);
        } else if (type === 'paper') {
          const buf = _getWebPcmBuffer(ctx, 'paper_silk', 0.085, (out, len, sr) => {
            let p1 = 0, p2 = 0, lp = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
              const t = i / sr, nt = t / 0.085;
              const snap = t < 0.008 ? Math.sin(Math.PI * (t / 0.008)) * 0.24 : 0;
              const env = Math.pow(Math.sin(Math.PI * nt), 1.45) * Math.exp(-nt * 1.25);
              const r = Math.random();
              const imp = r < 0.16 ? (r < 0.08 ? 1 : -1) : 0;
              const diff = imp - 1.28 * p1 + 0.42 * p2;
              p2 = p1; p1 = imp;
              lp = 0.65 * lp + 0.35 * diff;
              out[i] = snap * diff * 0.28 + lp * env * 0.64;
            }
          });
          playBuf(buf, 0.22, 0, 0.025);
        } else if (type === 'fanfare') {
          // 同调雅乐合鸣：笙簧五度和音 + 古筝五声清泉琶音 + 竹笛高音泛音
          playFluteOrPad(_getWebHarmFreq(0 + degShift, 0.5), 2.1, 0.08, schedDelay, false);
          [0, 1, 2, 3, 5].forEach((deg, idx) => {
            const freq = _getWebHarmFreq(deg + degShift, 1.0);
            playBuf(_getWebKSString(ctx, freq, 1.35, false), (0.16 - idx * 0.015), schedDelay + idx * 0.072, 0.002);
          });
          playFluteOrPad(_getWebHarmFreq(5 + degShift, 1.0), 1.5, 0.08, schedDelay + 0.28, true);
        } else if (type === 'alert') {
          // 沉吟低回：同调古琴低音五度散音
          playFluteOrPad(_getWebHarmFreq(0 + degShift, 0.5), 1.4, 0.08, schedDelay, false);
          playBuf(_getWebKSString(ctx, _getWebHarmFreq(1 + degShift, 0.5), 1.35, true), 0.16, schedDelay + 0.02, 0.002);
          playBuf(_getWebKSString(ctx, _getWebHarmFreq(0 + degShift, 0.5), 1.65, true), 0.15, schedDelay + 0.18, 0.002);
        } else if (type === 'coin' || type === 'like') {
          const f1 = _getWebHarmFreq(5 + degShift, 2.0);
          const f2 = _getWebHarmFreq(7 + degShift, 2.0);
          const buf = _getWebPcmBuffer(ctx, 'coin_harm_' + Math.round(f1), 0.36, (out, len, sr) => {
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
              const t = i / sr, att = t < 0.003 ? (0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(Math.PI * (t / 0.003))) : 1;
              const m1 = 0.55 * Math.exp(-t * 10) * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f1 * t);
              const m2 = 0.35 * Math.exp(-t * 16) * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f2 * t);
              out[i] = (m1 + m2) * att * 0.68;
            }
          });
          playBuf(buf, 0.14, schedDelay, 0.006);
          playBuf(buf, 0.11, schedDelay + 0.07, 0.008);
        } else if (type === 'school_ambience') {
          // 学堂书声与上下课钟声
          const bellF = _getWebHarmFreq(4, 1.0);
          playBuf(_getWebKSString(ctx, bellF, 1.6, false), 0.11, 0, 0.002);
          playFluteOrPad(_getWebHarmFreq(2, 1.0), 1.4, 0.05, 0.18, true);
        } else if (type === 'factory_ambience') {
          // 国营工厂车间交接班蒸汽汽笛与机杼律动
          playFluteOrPad(_getWebHarmFreq(0, 0.5), 1.6, 0.07, 0, false);
          playFluteOrPad(_getWebHarmFreq(3, 0.5), 1.6, 0.05, 0.04, false);
        } else if (type === 'click') {
          const buf = _getWebPcmBuffer(ctx, 'wood_click_soft', 0.042, (out, len, sr) => {
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
              const t = i / sr;
              const pulse = t < 0.0012 ? Math.sin(Math.PI * (t / 0.0012)) * 0.18 : 0;
              const m1 = Math.exp(-t * 210) * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 640 * t) * 0.62;
              const m2 = Math.exp(-t * 420) * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 1420 * t) * 0.28;
              out[i] = (pulse + m1 + m2) * 0.78;
            }
          });
          playBuf(buf, 0.16, 0, 0.02);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Audio play error:", e);
      }
    }

      lifetimeMaxRank: 1,
      rankName: "鱼贩个体户",
      jobTitle: "码头海鲜水产批发摊",
      rep: 45,
      iq: 78,
      eq: 68,
      courage: 72,
      clean: 90,
      resilience: 70,
      capital: 24000,
      health: 95,            // 生理健康精力 (0~100)
      lifespanCeiling: 74,   // 动态寿命上限 (52~86)
      overworkYears: 0,
      wellnessYears: 0,
      wellnessStreak: 0,
      auditCrisisCount: 0,
      debtCrisisCount: 0,
      healthCrisisCount: 0,
      chronicleFilter: "all", // 编年史筛选分类
      chronicleHistory: [],  // 结构化年谱历史
      strategy: "balanced",
      isPlaying: false,
      speed: 1.0,
      timerId: null,
      sp: 35,
      activeShield: false,
      insightBuff: false,
      isParallelTrack: false,
      isInReservePool: false,
      pendingPhoneCrisis: null,
      tags: {},              // 全局跨年龄因果蝴蝶效应标签库
      handledEvents: {}      // 防重字典
    };

    function clampCoreStats(s = state) {
      if (!s) return s;
      ['health', 'clean', 'iq', 'eq', 'courage', 'rep', 'resilience'].forEach(k => {
        if (typeof s[k] === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(s[k])) {
          s[k] = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(s[k])));
        }
      });
      return s;
    }

    function syncRankProgression(s = state) {
      if (!s) return s;
      const rawRank = Math.max(Number(s.govLevel) || 1, Number(s.rankIndex) || 1, Number(s.rankLevel) || 1);
      const clampedRank = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(rawRank)));
      s.govLevel = clampedRank;
      s.rankIndex = clampedRank;
      s.rankLevel = clampedRank;
      s.lifetimeMaxRank = Math.max(clampedRank, Math.min(5, Math.round(Number(s.lifetimeMaxRank) || clampedRank)));
      return s;
    }

    function computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(s = state) {
      if (!s) return 72;
      const base = 66;
      const healthMod = Math.round(((s.health ?? 70) - 55) * 0.28);
      const wellnessMod = Math.min(7, Math.floor((s.wellnessYears || 0) * 0.65));
      const overworkPenalty = Math.min(11, Math.floor((s.overworkYears || 0) * 0.85));
      const crisisPenalty = (s.healthCrisisCount || 0) * 4 + (s.auditCrisisCount || 0) * 2;
      const ceiling = Math.max(52, Math.min(86, base + healthMod + wellnessMod - overworkPenalty - crisisPenalty));
      s.lifespanCeiling = ceiling;
      return ceiling;
    }

    function migrateSaveData(raw) {
      if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return null;
      const s = Object.assign({}, state, raw);
      const legacyMap = { overwork: 'aggressive', upright: 'clean', social: 'network', health: 'wellness' };
      if (legacyMap[s.strategy]) s.strategy = legacyMap[s.strategy];
      if (!['balanced', 'aggressive', 'clean', 'network', 'wellness'].includes(s.strategy)) s.strategy = 'balanced';
      clampCoreStats(s);
      syncRankProgression(s);
      computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(s);
      s.version = SAVE_VERSION;
      return s;
    }

    const STRATEGIES = {
      balanced: {
        name: "🛡️ 稳健中庸履职 (默认)",
        badgeClass: "bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border-amber-800",
        yearlyBuff: () => {
          state.wellnessStreak = 0;
          state.iq += 2; state.eq += 2; state.clean += 1; state.capital += 24000; state.rep += 2;
          state.health = Math.max(10, state.health - 2);
          clampCoreStats(state);
          computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(state);
        }
      },
      aggressive: {
        name: "🚀 激进开拓狂飙 (+50%战功暴击)",
        badgeClass: "bg-red-950 text-red-300 border-red-800",
        yearlyBuff: () => {
          state.wellnessStreak = 0;
          state.overworkYears = (state.overworkYears || 0) + 1;
          state.iq += 3; state.courage += 4; state.clean -= 3; state.capital += 38000; state.rep += 4;
          state.health = Math.max(10, state.health - 5);
          clampCoreStats(state);
          computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(state);
        }
      },
      clean: {
        name: "⚔️ 清正铁面防线 (+50%防暗箭)",
        badgeClass: "bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-800",
        yearlyBuff: () => {
          state.wellnessStreak = 0;
          state.clean += 4; state.courage += 2; state.capital += 18000; state.rep += 3;
          state.health = Math.max(10, state.health - 2);
          clampCoreStats(state);
          computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(state);
        }
      },
      network: {
        name: "🤝 卓越沟通协同 (+50%多跨协作支持)",
        badgeClass: "bg-purple-950 text-purple-300 border-purple-800",
        yearlyBuff: () => {
          state.wellnessStreak = 0;
          state.eq += 4; state.clean -= 1; state.capital += 22000; state.rep += 3;
          state.health = Math.max(10, state.health - 3);
          clampCoreStats(state);
          computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(state);
        }
      },
      wellness: {
        name: "🌿 养生调理 (身心健康优先)",
        badgeClass: "bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-200 border-emerald-700",
        yearlyBuff: () => {
          state.wellnessYears = (state.wellnessYears || 0) + 1;
          state.wellnessStreak = (state.wellnessStreak || 0) + 1;
          const hGain = (state.health >= 72 || state.wellnessStreak >= 3) ? 2 : 6;
          state.health = Math.min(100, state.health + hGain);
          if (state.clean < 65) state.clean += 1;
          if (state.wellnessStreak >= 2) state.rep = Math.max(0, state.rep - 2);
          state.capital += 14000;
          clampCoreStats(state);
          computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(state);
        }
      }
    };
