    function openFateWheelModal() {
      pauseTimeline();
      document.getElementById("fate-wheel-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function selectCareerTrack(trackKey) {
      selectedTrackKey = trackKey;
      const names = {
        biz: "🌊 商海弄潮",
        med: "🩺 大医精诚",
        tech: "🔬 硬核智造",
        agri: "🌾 沃野千里",
        art: "🎨 文化造梦",
        law: "⚖️ 律政天平"
      };
      const badge = document.getElementById("selected-track-badge");
      if (badge) badge.innerText = names[trackKey] || trackKey;

      ['biz', 'med', 'tech', 'agri', 'art', 'law'].forEach(k => {
        const card = document.getElementById(`track-card-${k}`);
        if (!card) return;
        if (k === trackKey) {
          card.className = "p-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-amber-500 bg-amber-950/40 cursor-pointer transition-all text-center shadow-lg";
        } else {
          card.className = "p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 hover:border-slate-700 cursor-pointer transition-all text-center";
        }
      });
    }

    function selectFamilyOrigin(originKey) {
      selectedOriginKey = originKey;
      const originObj = FAMILY_ORIGINS[originKey] || FAMILY_ORIGINS.peasant;
      const badge = document.getElementById("selected-origin-badge");
      if (badge) badge.innerText = originObj.name;

      Object.keys(FAMILY_ORIGINS).forEach(k => {
        const card = document.getElementById(`origin-card-${k}`);
        if (!card) return;
        if (k === originKey) {
          card.className = "p-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-amber-500 bg-amber-950/40 cursor-pointer transition-all shadow-lg";
        } else {
          card.className = "p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 hover:border-slate-700 cursor-pointer transition-all";
        }
      });
    }

    function selectRegionalSetting(regionKey) {
      selectedRegionKey = regionKey;
      const regObj = REGIONAL_SETTINGS[regionKey] || REGIONAL_SETTINGS.coastal;
      const badge = document.getElementById("selected-region-badge");
      if (badge) badge.innerText = regObj.name;

      Object.keys(REGIONAL_SETTINGS).forEach(k => {
        const card = document.getElementById(`region-card-${k}`);
        if (!card) return;
        if (k === regionKey) {
          card.className = "p-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-sky-500 bg-sky-950/40 cursor-pointer transition-all shadow-lg";
        } else {
          card.className = "p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 hover:border-slate-700 cursor-pointer transition-all";
        }
      });
    }

    function randomPickFate() {
      const tracks = ['biz', 'med', 'tech', 'agri', 'art', 'law'];
      const origins = Object.keys(FAMILY_ORIGINS);
      const regions = Object.keys(REGIONAL_SETTINGS);

      selectCareerTrack(tracks[Math.floor(Math.random() * tracks.length)]);
      selectFamilyOrigin(origins[Math.floor(Math.random() * origins.length)]);
      selectRegionalSetting(regions[Math.floor(Math.random() * regions.length)]);
    }

    function randomPickTrack() {
      randomPickFate();
    }

    function confirmTrackAndProceed() {
      document.getElementById("fate-wheel-modal").classList.add("hidden");
      openTalentModal();
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 天赋摇号抽卡逻辑
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function openTalentModal() {
      pauseTimeline();
      rollRandomTalents();
      document.getElementById("talent-lottery-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function rollRandomTalents() {
      const shuffled = [...TALENT_POOL].sort(() => 0.5 - Math.random());
      activeTalents = shuffled.slice(0, 3);

      const box = document.getElementById("lottery-talents-box");
      box.innerHTML = "";
      activeTalents.forEach(t => {
        const card = document.createElement("div");
        card.className = `p-2.5 rounded-xl border ${t.color} text-xs transition-all`;
        card.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between font-bold">
            <span>${t.name}</span>
            <span class="text-xs px-1 py-0.2 rounded bg-black/40 font-mono">${t.rarity}</span>
          </div>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-1 leading-relaxed">${t.desc}</p>
        `;
        box.appendChild(card);
      });

      const conf = TRACK_CONFIGS[selectedTrackKey];
      state.track = selectedTrackKey;
      state.originKey = selectedOriginKey;
      state.regionKey = selectedRegionKey;
      state.name = conf.name;
      state.age = conf.startAge;
      state.year = conf.startYear;
      state.rankName = conf.startRank;
      state.jobTitle = conf.startJob;

      state.iq = Math.floor(68 + Math.random() * 20);
      state.eq = Math.floor(65 + Math.random() * 20);
      state.courage = Math.floor(65 + Math.random() * 20);
      state.clean = Math.floor(75 + Math.random() * 20);
      state.capital = Math.floor(20000 + Math.random() * 15000);
      state.rep = 45;

      // 施加出身背景修正
      const originData = FAMILY_ORIGINS[selectedOriginKey] || FAMILY_ORIGINS.peasant;
      originData.apply(state);

      // 施加天命词条修正
      activeTalents.forEach(t => {
        if (t.id === "family") state.eq += 20;
        if (t.id === "writer") state.iq += 25;
        if (t.id === "iron") state.courage += 30;
        if (t.id === "clean_heart") state.clean += 25;
        if (t.id === "popular") state.rep += 20;
        if (t.id === "rash") { state.courage += 15; state.eq -= 15; }
      });

      document.getElementById("lottery-stats-preview").innerHTML = `
        <span>智商: ${state.iq}</span>
        <span>情商: ${state.eq}</span>
        <span>魄力: ${state.courage}</span>
        <span>清廉: ${state.clean}</span>
        <span>储蓄: ${(state.capital/10000).toFixed(1)}万</span>
      `;
    }

    function confirmTalentsAndStart() {
      document.getElementById("talent-lottery-modal").classList.add("hidden");
      initOnboardingTasks();
      applyFamilyLegacyBonus();
      const conf = TRACK_CONFIGS[state.track];
      state.handledEvents = {};
      state.tags = {};
      state.isParallelTrack = false;
      state.isInReservePool = false;
      state.pendingPhoneCrisis = null;

      const originData = FAMILY_ORIGINS[state.originKey] || FAMILY_ORIGINS.peasant;
      const regionData = REGIONAL_SETTINGS[state.regionKey] || REGIONAL_SETTINGS.coastal;

      // 绑定身份与属性标签
      document.getElementById("ui-avatar").innerText = conf.avatar;
      document.getElementById("ui-name").innerText = conf.name;
      document.getElementById("ui-track-tag").innerText = conf.title;
      const originBadge = document.getElementById("ui-origin-tag");
      const regionBadge = document.getElementById("ui-region-tag");
      if (originBadge) originBadge.innerText = originData.name;
      if (regionBadge) regionBadge.innerText = regionData.name;

      document.getElementById("ui-rep-label").innerText = conf.repLabel;
      conf.attrLabels.forEach((lbl, i) => {
        document.getElementById(`attr-${i+1}-name`).innerText = lbl;
      });

      const talentBar = document.getElementById("ui-active-talents");
      talentBar.innerHTML = "";
      activeTalents.forEach(t => {
        const badge = document.createElement("span");
        badge.className = `px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border text-xs font-bold shrink-0 ${t.color}`;
        badge.innerText = t.name;
        talentBar.appendChild(badge);
      });

      document.getElementById("chronicle-list").innerHTML = "";
      addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【人生启航】${state.name}诞生于【${originData.fullName}】，扎根【${regionData.fullName}】，迈入【${conf.title}】，携天命词条【${activeTalents.map(t=>t.name).join('、')}】开启三十载波澜壮阔风云人生！`, "命格筑基");

      renderContacts();
      renderDeskCard();
      updateEpochNewspaper();
      updateHeaderUI();
      pauseTimeline();
      showStatToast("🎮 已进入手动掌控模式，点击【推进新一岁】开启征程！", "good");
    }

    // UI状态与进度更新

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 数值浮动飘字与即时变化监听引擎 (Floating Stat Toasts)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function getStatLabels() {
      const conf = TRACK_CONFIGS[state.track];
      if (state.track === 'biz') {
        return { iq: "商业嗅觉", eq: "江湖人情", courage: "豪赌魄力", clean: "信誉征信", capital: "现金储备", rep: "商誉江湖" };
      } else if (state.track === 'med') {
        return { iq: "医学造诣", eq: "医患沟通", courage: "临机魄力", clean: "医德医风", capital: "科研经费", rep: "医德学界" };
      }
      return { iq: "大局心智", eq: "组织情商", courage: "铁腕魄力", clean: "合规操守", capital: "个人存款", rep: "组织声望" };
    }

    function showStatToast(text, type = 'info') {
      const container = document.getElementById("stat-toast-container");
      if (!container) return;
      const el = document.createElement("div");
      let colorClass = "bg-slate-900/95 text-sky-300 border-sky-600/70";
      if (type === 'good') colorClass = "bg-emerald-950/95 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-600 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(16,185,129,0.3)]";
      else if (type === 'bad') colorClass = "bg-rose-950/95 text-rose-300 border-rose-600 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(244,63,94,0.3)]";
      else if (type === 'gold') colorClass = "bg-amber-950/95 text-amber-300 border-amber-600 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(245,158,11,0.3)]";

      el.className = `stat-toast-anim px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold font-mono border flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-lg ${colorClass}`;
      el.innerHTML = `<span>⚡</span><span>${text}</span>`;
      container.appendChild(el);
      setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 3500);
    }

    function trackStatChanges(actionFn) {
      const before = {
        iq: state.iq,
        eq: state.eq,
        courage: state.courage,
        clean: state.clean,
        capital: state.capital,
        rep: state.rep,
        health: state.health
      };
      actionFn();
      const after = {
        iq: state.iq,
        eq: state.eq,
        courage: state.courage,
        clean: state.clean,
        capital: state.capital,
        rep: state.rep,
        health: state.health
      };

      const diffs = [];
      const labels = getStatLabels();
      if (after.health !== before.health) {
        const d = after.health - before.health;
        diffs.push({ text: `${d > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d} 生理精力`, type: d > 0 ? 'good' : 'bad' });
      }
      if (after.clean !== before.clean) {
        const d = after.clean - before.clean;
        diffs.push({ text: `${d > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d} ${labels.clean}`, type: d > 0 ? 'good' : 'bad' });
      }
      if (after.rep !== before.rep) {
        const d = after.rep - before.rep;
        diffs.push({ text: `${d > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d} ${labels.rep}`, type: d > 0 ? 'gold' : 'bad' });
      }
      if (after.iq !== before.iq) {
        const d = after.iq - before.iq;
        diffs.push({ text: `${d > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d} ${labels.iq}`, type: d > 0 ? 'good' : 'bad' });
      }
      if (after.eq !== before.eq) {
        const d = after.eq - before.eq;
        diffs.push({ text: `${d > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d} ${labels.eq}`, type: d > 0 ? 'good' : 'bad' });
      }
      if (after.courage !== before.courage) {
        const d = after.courage - before.courage;
        diffs.push({ text: `${d > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d} ${labels.courage}`, type: d > 0 ? 'good' : 'bad' });
      }
      if (after.capital !== before.capital) {
        const d = after.capital - before.capital;
        const wan = (d / 10000).toFixed(1);
        diffs.push({ text: `${d > 0 ? '+' : ''}${wan}万 ${labels.capital}`, type: d > 0 ? 'gold' : 'bad' });
      }

      diffs.slice(0, 3).forEach((item, idx) => {
        setTimeout(() => showStatToast(item.text, item.type), idx * 240);
      });
    }

    function updateHeaderUI() {
      const conf = TRACK_CONFIGS[state.track];
      const originData = FAMILY_ORIGINS[state.originKey] || FAMILY_ORIGINS.peasant;
      const regionData = REGIONAL_SETTINGS[state.regionKey] || REGIONAL_SETTINGS.coastal;

      const originBadge = document.getElementById("ui-origin-tag");
      const regionBadge = document.getElementById("ui-region-tag");
      if (originBadge) originBadge.innerText = originData.name;
      if (regionBadge) regionBadge.innerText = regionData.name;

      document.getElementById("ui-job-title").innerText = state.jobTitle;
      document.getElementById("ui-rank-name").innerText = state.rankName;
      document.getElementById("ui-age-year").innerText = `${state.age}岁 · ${state.year}年`;
      document.getElementById("ui-rep").innerText = state.rep;
      document.getElementById("banner-year").innerText = state.year;
      document.getElementById("banner-age").innerText = state.age;

      document.getElementById("attr-1-val").innerText = state.iq;
      document.getElementById("attr-2-val").innerText = state.eq;
      document.getElementById("attr-3-val").innerText = state.courage;
      document.getElementById("attr-4-val").innerText = state.clean;
      document.getElementById("attr-5-val").innerText = (state.capital / 10000).toFixed(1) + '万';

      const startA = conf.startAge;
      const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(5, Math.round(((state.age - startA) / (60 - startA)) * 100)));
      document.getElementById("ui-progress-bar").style.width = `${pct}%`;

      const healthEl = document.getElementById("ui-health-val");
      const healthStatusEl = document.getElementById("ui-health-status");
      if (healthEl) healthEl.innerText = state.health;
      if (healthStatusEl) {
        if (state.health >= 80) {
          healthStatusEl.innerText = "充沛";
          healthStatusEl.className = "px-1 py-0.2 rounded text-xs bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800";
        } else if (state.health >= 40) {
          healthStatusEl.innerText = "亚健康";
          healthStatusEl.className = "px-1 py-0.2 rounded text-xs bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800";
        } else {
          healthStatusEl.innerText = "高危濒危";
          healthStatusEl.className = "px-1 py-0.2 rounded text-xs bg-rose-950 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800 animate-pulse";
        }
      }

      const deadEl = document.getElementById("ui-deadline-label");
      const stageEl = document.getElementById("ui-timeline-stage");

      const curConf = TRACK_CONFIGS[state.track];
      if (curConf && curConf.ladder) {
        const nextStep = curConf.ladder.find(step => step.deadline && state.age <= step.deadline);
        if (nextStep) {
          deadEl.innerText = `下阶段目标：${nextStep.deadline}岁前冲刺【${nextStep.rank}】！`;
          stageEl.innerText = nextStep.job;
        } else {
          deadEl.innerText = `当前行业巅峰身位：${state.jobTitle}`;
          stageEl.innerText = "一代行业传奇";
        }
      } else {
        deadEl.innerText = `当前行业身位：${state.jobTitle}`;
        stageEl.innerText = state.age >= 50 ? "行业功成名就" : "产业开拓狂飙";
      }

      const advBtnText = document.getElementById("btn-advance-text");
      if (advBtnText) {
        if (state.age >= 60) {
          advBtnText.innerText = "🏆 岁月圆满 · 终局大考盖棺定论 ➔";
        } else {
          advBtnText.innerText = `推进新一岁 · 迈向 ${state.age + 1} 岁 ➔`;
        }
      }
    }

    // 时代早报头条更新
    function updateEpochNewspaper() {
      const news = EPOCH_CHRONICLES[state.year] || {
        headline: `【时代长河】${state.year}年，宏观经济与社会平稳迈进`,
        body: `国家深化改革攻坚，市县抓紧重大战略项目谋划，社会各项事业全面繁荣。`
      };
      document.getElementById("news-year-stamp").innerText = `${state.year}年 · 特刊`;
      document.getElementById("news-headline").innerText = news.headline;
      document.getElementById("news-body").innerText = news.body;
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 时间自动推进引擎
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function startAutoTimeline() {
      if (state.timerId) clearInterval(state.timerId);
      const interval = Math.round(2500 / state.speed);
      state.timerId = setInterval(() => {
        if (state.isPlaying) {
          tickOneYear();
        }
      }, interval);
    }

    function pauseTimeline() {
      state.isPlaying = false;
      if (state.timerId) {
        clearInterval(state.timerId);
        state.timerId = null;
      }
      const playIcon = document.getElementById("play-pause-icon");
      if (playIcon) playIcon.innerText = "▶";
      const playText = document.getElementById("play-pause-text");
      if (playText) playText.innerText = "继续";
      const autoIcon = document.getElementById("auto-toggle-icon");
      if (autoIcon) autoIcon.innerText = "▶";
      const autoText = document.getElementById("auto-toggle-text");
      if (autoText) autoText.innerText = "自动";
      const autoBtn = document.getElementById("btn-auto-toggle");
      if (autoBtn) autoBtn.className = "py-2.5 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-xs text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 active:scale-95 transition-all shrink-0";
      const footerBtn = document.getElementById("footer-play-btn");
      if (footerBtn) footerBtn.innerText = "▶ 继续流转";
      const footerTxt = document.getElementById("footer-running-text");
      if (footerTxt) {
        footerTxt.innerText = "已暂停";
        footerTxt.className = "font-mono text-amber-400 font-bold";
      }
      const indicator = document.getElementById("time-indicator");
      if (indicator) indicator.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500";
      const autoStatus = document.getElementById("ui-auto-status");
      if (autoStatus) autoStatus.innerText = "⏸ 时间已暂停";
    }

    function resumeTimeline() {
      state.isPlaying = true;
      const playIcon = document.getElementById("play-pause-icon");
      if (playIcon) playIcon.innerText = "⏸";
      const playText = document.getElementById("play-pause-text");
      if (playText) playText.innerText = "暂停";
      const autoIcon = document.getElementById("auto-toggle-icon");
      if (autoIcon) autoIcon.innerText = "⏸";
      const autoText = document.getElementById("auto-toggle-text");
      if (autoText) autoText.innerText = "暂停";
      const autoBtn = document.getElementById("btn-auto-toggle");
      if (autoBtn) autoBtn.className = "py-2.5 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-900/80 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-xs text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-600 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 active:scale-95 transition-all shrink-0";
      const footerBtn = document.getElementById("footer-play-btn");
      if (footerBtn) footerBtn.innerText = "⏸ 暂停时间";
      const footerTxt = document.getElementById("footer-running-text");
      if (footerTxt) {
        footerTxt.innerText = "时间自动推进中";
        footerTxt.className = "font-mono text-emerald-400 font-bold";
      }
      const indicator = document.getElementById("time-indicator");
      if (indicator) indicator.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse";
      const autoStatus = document.getElementById("ui-auto-status");
      if (autoStatus) autoStatus.innerText = "⏳ 岁月自动流淌中...";
      startAutoTimeline();
    }

    function togglePlayPause() {
      if (state.isPlaying) pauseTimeline();
      else resumeTimeline();
    }

    function toggleSpeed() {
      if (state.speed === 1.0) {
        state.speed = 2.0;
        document.getElementById("btn-speed").innerText = "⏩ 1x";
        document.getElementById("time-speed-label").innerText = "2.0x 极速流转";
      } else {
        state.speed = 1.0;
        document.getElementById("btn-speed").innerText = "⏩ 2x";
        document.getElementById("time-speed-label").innerText = "1.0x 自动流转";
      }
      startAutoTimeline();
    }

    
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 小游戏经济循环系统：各赛道职级年度薪酬分红结算
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function calculateAnnualIncome() {
      const baseIncomes = {
        biz: [24000, 60000, 180000, 500000, 1500000, 5000000],
        med: [18000, 45000, 120000, 280000, 600000, 1200000],
        tech: [30000, 80000, 220000, 550000, 1200000, 3000000],
        agri: [15000, 40000, 100000, 250000, 600000, 1500000],
        art: [20000, 50000, 150000, 400000, 1000000, 2500000],
        law: [22000, 60000, 160000, 450000, 1100000, 2800000]
      };
      const list = baseIncomes[state.track] || baseIncomes.biz;
      const rankIdx = Math.min(list.length - 1, Math.max(0, state.rankLevel || 0));
      return list[rankIdx];
    }

    function cycleGameSpeed() {
      if (state.speed === 1.0) state.speed = 2.0;
      else if (state.speed === 2.0) state.speed = 3.0;
      else state.speed = 1.0;

      const spdEl = document.getElementById("ui-speed-label");
      if (spdEl) spdEl.innerText = state.speed.toFixed(1) + "x";

      if (state.isPlaying) {
        startAutoTimeline();
      }
      showStatToast(`⚡ 推演速度切换为 ${state.speed.toFixed(1)}x`, 'gold');
    }

    function manualStepForward() {
      if (window.navigator && window.navigator.vibrate) {
        window.navigator.vibrate([15]);
      }
      playSoundEffect('click');
      tickOneYear();
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 核心流转驱动函数 (严格生命时间线)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function tickOneYear() {
      const ceiling = computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(state);
      if (state.age >= ceiling) {
        pauseTimeline();
        triggerEndgameModal();
        return;
      }
      // 1. 年度薪酬分红收益到账 (小游戏正向数值反馈)
      const yearlyIncome = calculateAnnualIncome();
      state.capital += yearlyIncome;
      playSoundEffect('coin');
      showStatToast(`💰 结算当年经营与薪资结余 +¥${yearlyIncome.toLocaleString()}`, 'good');

      // 2. 累积局内战略韬略点 (每年+15 SP)
      if (typeof state.sp === 'undefined') state.sp = 35;
      state.sp = Math.min(100, state.sp + 15);
      state.insightBuff = false; // 重置单次洞察
      updateSkillsUI();

      // 检查新官上任初考锦囊任务
      if (state.clean >= 65 && state.rep >= 45) {
        checkOnboardingTask("eval");
      }

      // 战略流派共鸣光环结算
      updateArchetypeAura();

      // 组织年度考评评估反馈 (S/A/B/C)
      evaluateYearlyAppraisal();

      // 时代勋章成就检查
      checkAchievements();

      // 1. 检查机关宴饮酒桌博弈 (26岁, 38岁)
      if ((state.age === 25 || state.age === 37) && !state.handledEvents[`banquet_${state.age}`]) {
        state.lastMajorEventAge = state.age;
        triggerBanquetMiniGame();
        return;
      }

      // 2. 检查综合质效巡检答辩环节 (33岁, 46岁)
      if ((state.age === 33 || state.age === 46) && !state.handledEvents[`insp_${state.age}`]) {
        state.lastMajorEventAge = state.age;
        triggerInspectionMiniGame();
        return;
      }

      // 3. 卓越人才与身位跨越检定
      // 3.6 六大赛道身位跨越与职业天梯阶跃 (周胜海 / 林晚意 / 顾明远 / 陆远 / 叶知秋 / 方凌)
      const TRACK_MILESTONES = {
        biz: [
          { age: 24, rank: "商行老板", job: "跨区水产冷链批发行长", text: "【商行跨越】立足码头成立全区最大冷链批发商行，年营收破千万！" },
          { age: 32, rank: "民营实业家", job: "收购国营冷冻食品厂厂长", text: "【实业突围】抵押全部身家成功改制国营冷冻厂，初显民营实业大鳄本色！" },
          { age: 40, rank: "民营集团老总", job: "城市基建控股董事长", text: "【基建巨擘】乘四万亿东风涉足城市基础建设，胜海控股全省闻名！" },
          { age: 48, rank: "拟上市公司总裁", job: "胜海实业实控人 (主板IPO在审)", text: "【资本跃升】化解银行抽贷危机，主板IPO申报获证监会受理！" },
          { age: 55, rank: "跨国巨头董事局主席", job: "高端智造跨国龙头董事长", text: "【一代传奇】完成全球化高端智造布局，成为中国民营商界一代传奇！" }
        ],
        med: [
          { age: 29, rank: "主治医师", job: "创伤微创普外主治医师", text: "【考核胜出】以全院第一名通过主治医师遴选，独立主刀三四级疑难微创手术！" },
          { age: 36, rank: "副主任医师", job: "大外科副主任 · 国自然PI", text: "【科研突围】斩获国家自然科学基金重点项目，破格晋升大外科副主任！" },
          { age: 43, rank: "主任医师 (博导)", job: "大外科大主任 · 国家课题带头人", text: "【学界巨擘】突破国家级靶向新药三期临床瓶颈，当选外科大主任！" },

          { age: 32, rank: "专精特新创始人", job: "明远微电子董事长兼首席科学家", text: "【自主攻坚】成功攻克卡脖子工业级驱动算法，荣获国家专精特新小巨人！" },
          { age: 40, rank: "具身智能独角兽CEO", job: "人形工业机器人全栈自研独角兽掌舵人", text: "【行业领跑】发布全球首款微米级力控人形工业机器人，估值突破百亿！" },

        ],
        agri: [
          { age: 25, rank: "现代农场主", job: "高山有机果蔬示范基地主理人", text: "【农创起步】荒坡试验智慧水肥果林首季大丰收，产值突破三百万！" },

        ],

          { age: 39, rank: "东方美学传媒董事长", job: "非遗文化数字化全产业链集团董事长", text: "【文化航母】打造故宫与敦煌非遗数字IP矩阵，成为文创产业领军企业！" },

          { age: 55, rank: "世界级文化艺术宗师", job: "联合国东方艺术终身成就奖大师", text: "【文脉流芳】以毕生心血筑牢中华文化软实力，当选世界级艺术宗师！" }
        ],
        law: [
          { age: 26, rank: "诉讼部金牌骨干", job: "知名大所知识产权与涉外诉讼合伙人", text: "【初试锋芒】代理首例小家电反倾销海外诉讼全胜，名震华南律政圈！" },
          { age: 33, rank: "破产重组首席律师", job: "百亿上市制造企业重整特聘破产管理人", text: "【挽救实业】化解三百亿债务危机成功重组龙头制造，保住五千职工岗位！" },
          { age: 40, rank: "红圈律所管委会主任", job: "全国顶尖综合法律事务所掌门人", text: "【律政统帅】当选红圈所主任，带领中国律师团队深度参与国际商事博弈！" },
          { age: 48, rank: "国际商事仲裁专家", job: "国际商事仲裁院首位中国特聘资深仲裁员", text: "【公义之剑】在国际仲裁法庭为中国新能源产业赢得里程碑式正义裁决！" },
          { age: 56, rank: "德高望重法治名宿", job: "法学泰斗 · 推动国家公平正义立法先驱", text: "【正义天平】推动多部国家重磅商事合规与劳动法典修订，功勋垂世！" }
        ]
      };

      const curMilestones = TRACK_MILESTONES[state.track];
      if (curMilestones) {
        const mIdx = curMilestones.findIndex(b => b.age === state.age && !state.handledEvents[`milestone_${state.track}_${b.age}`]);
        if (mIdx !== -1) {
          const m = curMilestones[mIdx];
          state.handledEvents[`milestone_${state.track}_${m.age}`] = true;
          state.rankName = m.rank;
          state.jobTitle = m.job;
          state.govLevel = Math.min(5, mIdx + 1);
          syncRankProgression(state);
          state.capital += 150000;
          state.rep = Math.min(100, state.rep + 12);
          clampCoreStats(state);
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, m.text, "人生跃迁", "history");
          playSoundEffect('fanfare');
          updateHeaderUI();
        }
      }

      // 3.8 检查职业生涯命运十字路口转轨机遇 (28岁, 35岁, 44岁重大人生跨界)
      const pivotOpp = CAREER_PIVOT_OPPORTUNITIES.find(p => p.age === state.age && !state.handledEvents[`pivot_${p.age}`]);
      if (pivotOpp) {
        state.handledEvents[`pivot_${pivotOpp.age}`] = true;
        state.lastMajorEventAge = state.age;
        triggerCareerPivotModal(pivotOpp);
        return;
      }

      // 4. 时代宏大主线关隘 (按预设历史年份或年龄触发，六大璀璨赛道全周期历史大考)
      let storyArc = null;
      if (TRACK_SPECIFIC_STORIES[state.track]) {
        storyArc = TRACK_SPECIFIC_STORIES[state.track].find(a => (a.year === state.year || (a.triggerAge && state.age >= a.triggerAge)) && !state.handledEvents[`story_${a.id}`]);
      }
      if (!storyArc) {
        storyArc = EXTENDED_STORY_ARCS.find(a => (a.year === state.year || (a.triggerAge && state.age >= a.triggerAge)) && !state.handledEvents[`story_${a.id}`]);
      }

      // 确保重大历史大考与上一次事件保持至少 2 年从容履职间距，杜绝年年连环弹窗
      const lastMajorEventAge = state.lastMajorEventAge || 20;
      if (storyArc && (state.age - lastMajorEventAge >= 2)) {
        pauseTimeline();
        state.lastMajorEventAge = state.age;
        state.handledEvents[`story_${storyArc.id}`] = true;
        openDecisionModal(storyArc);
        return;
      }

      // 5. 随机事件抽选 (严格降频：保持 75% 岁月为从容的案头主界面实干履职年，仅 25% 偶发突发事件)
      // 健康濒危（<30）才优先触发大病
      if (state.health < 30 && !state.handledEvents['swan_life_sudden_heart_attack'] && Math.random() < 0.50) {
        pauseTimeline();
        state.handledEvents['swan_life_sudden_heart_attack'] = true;
        state.healthCrisisCount = (state.healthCrisisCount || 0) + 1;
        const heartAttackEvent = BLACK_SWAN_EVENTS.find(e => e.id === 'life_sudden_heart_attack');
        if (heartAttackEvent) {
          triggerRedPhoneCall(heartAttackEvent);
          return;
        }
      }

      // 综合随机事件池抽选：必须与上一次重大弹窗保持至少 2 年从容间隔，且单次仅 20% 偶发概率
      const canTriggerRandom = (state.age - (state.lastMajorEventAge || 20) >= 2);
      if (canTriggerRandom) {
        const eventRoll = Math.random();
        if (eventRoll < 0.20) { // 仅 20% 概率触发，其余 80% 玩家留在主界面案头实干
          // 5.1 模块化故事池 (15% 几率)
          const candidateStories = MODULAR_STORY_DECK.filter(s => {
            if (state.handledEvents[`mod_${s.id}`]) return false;
            if (s.minAge && state.age < s.minAge) return false;
            if (s.maxAge && state.age > s.maxAge) return false;
            if (s.reqTrack && s.reqTrack !== state.track) return false;
            return true;
          });
          
          if (eventRoll < 0.15 && candidateStories.length > 0) {
            pauseTimeline();
            state.lastMajorEventAge = state.age;
            const pickedStory = candidateStories[Math.floor(Math.random() * candidateStories.length)];
            state.handledEvents[`mod_${pickedStory.id}`] = true;
            openDecisionModal(pickedStory);
            return;
          }

          // 5.2 突发黑天鹅或世相录 (10% 几率)
          const validSwans = BLACK_SWAN_EVENTS.filter(e => (!e.track || e.track === state.track) && !state.handledEvents['swan_' + e.id]);
          if (validSwans.length > 0 && Math.random() < 0.5) {
            pauseTimeline();
            state.lastMajorEventAge = state.age;
            state.handledEvents[`swan_${state.age}`] = true;
            const randEvent = validSwans[Math.floor(Math.random() * validSwans.length)];
            triggerRedPhoneCall(randEvent);
            return;
          } else {
            const unhandledEncounters = LIFE_ENCOUNTER_EVENTS.filter(e => !state.handledEvents['enc_' + e.id]);
            if (unhandledEncounters.length > 0) {
              pauseTimeline();
              state.lastMajorEventAge = state.age;
              const enc = unhandledEncounters[Math.floor(Math.random() * unhandledEncounters.length)];
              state.handledEvents['enc_' + enc.id] = true;
              openDecisionModal(enc);
              return;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // 6. 自然度过该年 (岁增一载)
      state.age++;
      state.year++;

      const currentStrat = STRATEGIES[state.strategy] || STRATEGIES.balanced;
      trackStatChanges(() => {
        currentStrat.yearlyBuff();
      });

      // 岁长健康自然衰减
      if (state.age > 45) {
        const decay = state.age >= 65 ? 2 : 1;
        state.health = Math.max(10, state.health - decay);
      }
      clampCoreStats(state);
      const updatedCeiling = computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(state);

      const logText = `${state.year}年，你坚守在${state.jobTitle}岗位，按【${currentStrat.name}】稳步操盘，实绩与人资平稳积累。`;
      document.getElementById("banner-desc").innerText = logText;
      addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, logText, "岁增一载", "general");
      renderDeskCard();
      updateEpochNewspaper();
      refreshMomentsFeed();
      updateHeaderUI();

      // 终局条件检查 (动态天年上限、清廉崩塌或身体彻底透支)
      if (state.age >= updatedCeiling || state.clean <= 20 || state.health <= 15) {
        pauseTimeline();
        triggerEndgameModal();
      }
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 红色机要保密电话系统 (Red Phone Hot-line)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 人生重大转轨执行引擎
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function triggerCareerPivotModal(pivotEvent) {
      pauseTimeline();
      const modal = document.getElementById("career-pivot-modal");
      if (!modal) return;
      document.getElementById("pivot-modal-title").innerText = pivotEvent.title;
      document.getElementById("pivot-modal-scene").innerText = pivotEvent.scene;

      const container = document.getElementById("pivot-options-container");
      container.innerHTML = "";

      pivotEvent.options.forEach((opt, idx) => {
        if (opt.reqTrack && opt.reqTrack !== state.track) return;

        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = "w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-amber-600/60 text-left text-xs text-amber-300 font-medium active:scale-[0.99] transition-all flex flex-col gap-1 shadow-md";
        btn.innerHTML = `
          <div class="font-bold text-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>${opt.text}</span>
            ${opt.targetTrack ? `<span class="text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono font-bold bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800">转轨至 ${TRACK_CONFIGS[opt.targetTrack].title}</span>` : ''}
          </div>
        `;
        btn.onclick = () => {
          document.getElementById("career-pivot-modal").classList.add("hidden");
          opt.action();
          state.age++;
          state.year++;
          updateHeaderUI();
          resumeTimeline();
        };
        container.appendChild(btn);
      });

      modal.classList.remove("hidden");
      playSoundEffect('fanfare');
    }

    function performCareerPivot(targetTrack, targetRank, targetJob, logMsg) {
      state.track = targetTrack;
      state.rankName = targetRank;
      state.jobTitle = targetJob;
      state.tags[`pivot_to_${targetTrack}`] = true;
      playSoundEffect('stamp');
      playSoundEffect('fanfare');

      const appContainer = document.getElementById("app-container");
      if (appContainer) {
        appContainer.classList.add("shake-slam");
        setTimeout(() => appContainer.classList.remove("shake-slam"), 400);
      }

      document.getElementById("career-pivot-modal").classList.add("hidden");
      showStatToast(`已转轨至【${TRACK_CONFIGS[targetTrack].title}】`, 'gold');
      addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, logMsg, "命运转轨", "history");

      updateHeaderUI();
      resumeTimeline();
    }

    function triggerRedPhoneCall(event) {
      pauseTimeline();
      state.pendingPhoneCrisis = event;
      const bar = document.getElementById("emergency-phone-alert-bar");
      document.getElementById("emergency-phone-summary").innerText = event.phoneTitle || "突发紧急情况，主要领导保密内线急促响铃！";
      bar.classList.remove("hidden");
      playSoundEffect('phone');
    }

    function answerRedPhone() {
      const bar = document.getElementById("emergency-phone-alert-bar");
      bar.classList.add("hidden");
      playSoundEffect('click');
      if (state.pendingPhoneCrisis) {
        openDecisionModal(state.pendingPhoneCrisis);
        state.pendingPhoneCrisis = null;
      }
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 案头公文签批系统 (带实体印章猛砸动效与浑厚重低音)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function renderDeskCard() {
      const docs = DESK_DOCUMENTS[state.track] || DESK_DOCUMENTS.gov;
      const doc = docs[(state.year + state.age) % docs.length];
      const container = document.getElementById("desk-doc-container");
      if (!container) return;

      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="desk-card p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-800/40 relative">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 mb-1">
            <span>${doc.org}</span>
            <span class="text-red-400 font-bold">${doc.secret}</span>
          </div>
          <h4 class="font-bold text-xs text-amber-200 mb-2 leading-snug">${doc.title}</h4>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-300 font-serif leading-relaxed mb-3">${doc.content}</p>
          <div class="flex justify-end">
            <button onclick="openDeskModal()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-950 hover:bg-red-900 border border-red-700 text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shadow">
              ✍️ 提笔审阅 · 盖印批办
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function triggerDeskApprovalManually() {
      switchTab('desk');
      openDeskModal();
    }

    function openDeskModal() {
      pauseTimeline();
      playSoundEffect('paper');
      const docs = DESK_DOCUMENTS[state.track] || DESK_DOCUMENTS.gov;
      const doc = docs[(state.year + state.age) % docs.length];

      // 隐藏印章容器
      document.getElementById("rubber-stamp-container").classList.add("hidden");

      document.getElementById("desk-doc-org").innerText = `报送机关：${doc.org}`;
      document.getElementById("desk-doc-num").innerText = `字号：${doc.num}`;
      document.getElementById("desk-doc-secret").innerText = doc.secret;
      document.getElementById("desk-doc-title").innerText = doc.title;
      document.getElementById("desk-doc-content").innerText = doc.content;

      const optBox = document.getElementById("desk-doc-options");
      optBox.innerHTML = "";
      doc.choices.forEach((c, idx) => {
        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = "w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#f0ebe1] hover:bg-[#e4dcce] border border-stone-400 text-left text-xs font-medium active:scale-[0.99] transition-all flex flex-col gap-1 text-stone-900 shadow-sm";
        btn.innerHTML = `
          <div class="font-bold flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>${idx + 1}. ${c.text}</span>
            <span class="text-xs px-1 py-0.2 rounded bg-red-100 text-red-800 border border-red-300 font-mono">加盖印章 ➔</span>
          </div>
          <div class="text-xs text-stone-600 font-serif">${c.effect}</div>
        `;
        btn.onclick = () => stampAndExecuteDeskApproval(c, doc);
        optBox.appendChild(btn);
      });

      document.getElementById("desk-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function stampAndExecuteDeskApproval(choice, doc) {
      playSoundEffect('stamp');
      // 禁用所有按键，展示实物盖章下沉动画
      const stampBox = document.getElementById("rubber-stamp-container");
      const stampShape = document.getElementById("stamp-shape");
      const stampText = document.getElementById("stamp-text");
      const stampDate = document.getElementById("stamp-date");

      stampText.innerText = choice.stampText || "准予执行";
      stampDate.innerText = `${state.year}.${(state.age % 12 + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;

      if (choice.stampType === 'reject') {
        stampShape.className = "rubber-stamp-reject w-24 h-16 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-1 select-none font-bold";
      } else {
        stampShape.className = "rubber-stamp-pass w-24 h-24 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-1 select-none font-bold";
      }

      stampBox.classList.remove("hidden");

      setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById("desk-modal").classList.add("hidden");
        stampBox.classList.add("hidden");
        choice.action(state);

        // 加入台账
        const histList = document.getElementById("desk-history-list");
        const record = document.createElement("div");
        record.className = "p-2 rounded bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 flex justify-between";
        record.innerHTML = `
          <span class="truncate">[${state.year}年] ${doc.title}</span>
          <span class="text-red-400 font-bold shrink-0">已盖章留痕</span>
        `;
        histList.insertBefore(record, histList.firstChild);

        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【红头批办】签批公文《${doc.title}》，已加盖红色实体公章【${choice.stampText}】，归入永久档案。`, "公文留痕");
        updateHeaderUI();
        resumeTimeline();
      }, 550);
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 组织任用四部曲沙盘检定 (视觉化实木会议桌 + 桌牌亮牌)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    let pendingPromotion = null;
    let currentPromotionStep = 1;
    let promoVerdictSuccess = false;

    function startPromotionProcess(promo) {
      pendingPromotion = promo;
      currentPromotionStep = 1;
      promoVerdictSuccess = false;

      document.getElementById("promotion-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("promo-title").innerText = `${promo.targetRank} 干部竞争性考评`;
      document.getElementById("promo-subtitle").innerText = `考察对象：${state.name}同志（拟提拔：${promo.targetJob}）`;

      renderPromotionStep();
    }

    function renderPromotionStep() {
      const stepContent = document.getElementById("promo-step-content");
      const btnNext = document.getElementById("btn-promo-next");

      [1, 2, 3, 4].forEach(s => {
        const tag = document.getElementById(`step-${s}-tag`);
        if (s === currentPromotionStep) tag.className = "text-amber-400 font-bold";
        else if (s < currentPromotionStep) tag.className = "text-emerald-400";
        else tag.className = "text-slate-600";
      });

      if (currentPromotionStep === 1) {
        const rate = Math.min(95, Math.max(40, Math.round(50 + (state.eq - 65) * 1.5)));
        const isPass = Math.random() * 100 <= rate;
        promoVerdictSuccess = isPass;

        stepContent.innerHTML = `
          <div class="font-bold text-amber-300 text-sm mb-1">【第一阶段：行业同行无记名民主推荐评议】</div>
          <p class="mb-2">考察行业协会、同行机构与项目团队骨干无记名民主推荐得票。平日严苛或做人情商不足，极易在此遭遇暗箭被“乱打分”狙击。</p>
          <div class="bg-slate-950 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-800 font-mono text-xs mb-2">
            <div>测评基准得票率：<span class="text-sky-400 font-bold">${rate}%</span> (由组织情商 ${state.eq} 决定)</div>
            <div class="mt-1">投递有效选票：<strong>120张</strong></div>
          </div>
          <div class="p-2 rounded-lg ${isPass ? 'bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-800 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-red-950/40 border border-red-800 text-red-300'} font-bold">
            ${isPass ? '🟢【民主推荐顺利过半】：赞成推荐票达 95 票，高票列为组织考察首选对象！' : '🔴【民主测评未达标】：反对票过半，未能进入下一轮考察！'}
          </div>
        `;
        btnNext.innerText = isPass ? "进入第二阶段：职业操守与诚信风控穿透审查 ➔" : "终止提拔 · 留任现职";
      } else if (currentPromotionStep === 2) {
        const hasCleanTalent = activeTalents.some(t => t.id === 'clean_heart');
        const isCleanPass = hasCleanTalent || state.clean >= 70;
        promoVerdictSuccess = promoVerdictSuccess && isCleanPass;

        stepContent.innerHTML = `
          <div class="font-bold text-amber-300 text-sm mb-1">【第二阶段：职业操守与诚信风控穿透审查】</div>
          <p class="mb-2">严查房产、近亲属经商、海外账户及历史重大签字公文，启动廉政合规'双签字'核验。</p>
          <div class="bg-slate-950 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-800 font-mono text-xs mb-2">
            <div>廉政清白指数：<span class="text-emerald-400 font-bold">${state.clean} / 100</span></div>
            <div>历史审计印记：<span class="text-slate-300">${state.tags['chem_park_signed'] ? '⚠️ 曾有重化工项目容缺审批留痕' : '台账一清二白'}</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="p-2 rounded-lg ${isCleanPass ? 'bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-800 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-red-950/40 border border-red-800 text-red-300'} font-bold">
            ${isCleanPass ? '🟢【档案核查一清二白】：财产申报完整合规，出具清廉作风与合规治理优秀审查报告！' : '🔴【个人事项核查存疑】：发现未申报往来款项，依规暂缓任用核查！'}
          </div>
        `;
        btnNext.innerText = isCleanPass ? "进入第三阶段：国家级专家评审团终极闭门表决 ➔" : "接受核查 · 暂缓任用";
      } else if (currentPromotionStep === 3) {
        // 第三步：实木会议桌 + 立体桌牌举牌沙盘
        const hasFamilyTalent = activeTalents.some(t => t.id === "family");
        const members = [
          { title: "评审委员会主席", role: "两院院士 · 终审主持", bias: () => state.rep > 55 ? 'yes' : 'pass' },
          { title: "行业战略首席专家", role: "领军实体实干派", bias: () => state.courage > 65 ? 'yes' : 'no' },
          { title: "学术技术委员会主任", role: "前沿理论创新把关", bias: () => state.eq > 65 ? 'yes' : 'pass' },
          { title: "合规风控首席", role: "职业道德与诚信审查", bias: () => state.clean >= 75 ? 'yes' : 'no' },
          { title: "领军人才评审专员", role: "青年新锐与梯队考核", bias: () => hasFamilyTalent || state.age <= 38 ? 'yes' : 'pass' },
          { title: "行业协会资深代表", role: "行业口碑与公信力关", bias: () => state.rep > 50 ? 'yes' : 'pass' },
          { title: "产学研投融资顾问", role: "产业落地与经济效益关", bias: () => state.iq > 65 ? 'yes' : 'pass' }
        ];

        let yes = 0;
        let htmlSeats = "";
        members.forEach(m => {
          const res = m.bias();
          if (res === 'yes') yes++;
          htmlSeats += `
            <div class="p-1.5 rounded bg-stone-900 border border-amber-900/60 flex flex-col items-center text-center shadow-md">
              <div class="wood-nametag px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-bold tracking-wider mb-1 w-full truncate">
                ${m.title}
              </div>
              <div class="text-xs text-stone-400 mb-1">${m.role}</div>
              <div class="text-xs font-bold ${res==='yes'?'text-emerald-400 animate-pulse':res==='no'?'text-red-400':'text-stone-400'}">
                ${res==='yes'?'🟢 举手表决【赞成】':res==='no'?'🔴 按灭【反对】':'⚪ 托腮【弃权】'}
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        });

        const isVotePass = yes >= 4;
        promoVerdictSuccess = promoVerdictSuccess && isVotePass;

        stepContent.innerHTML = `
          <div class="font-bold text-amber-300 text-sm mb-1">【第三阶段：国家级评审委员会终极闭门票决】</div>
          <p class="mb-2">由两院院士、行业泰斗与权威机构 7 位学术商业权威闭门评审，需获得至少 4 票赞成：</p>
          <div class="wood-table-bg p-2 rounded-xl grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 mb-2">
            ${htmlSeats}
          </div>
          <div class="p-2 rounded-lg ${isVotePass ? 'bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-800 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-red-950/40 border border-red-800 text-red-300'} font-bold text-center">
            ${isVotePass ? `🎉【常委会高票通过】：获赞成票 ${yes}/7 票！正式通过拟任决议！` : `🔴【未达过半数】：赞成票仅 ${yes}/7 票，未能跨过常委会门槛！`}
          </div>
        `;
        btnNext.innerText = isVotePass ? "进入第四阶段：5个工作日任前公示 ➔" : "服从组织决议 · 留任原职";
      } else if (currentPromotionStep === 4) {
        const isPublicPass = promoVerdictSuccess;

        stepContent.innerHTML = `
          <div class="font-bold text-amber-300 text-sm mb-1">【第四阶段：全网公示与正式荣耀授牌】</div>
          <p class="mb-2">主流媒体与官方行业权威平台向全球公示卓越领航者认证，接受全社会监督与祝贺。</p>
          <div class="p-2.5 rounded-lg ${isPublicPass ? 'bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-800 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-red-950/40 border border-red-800 text-red-300'} font-bold">
            ${isPublicPass ? '🌟【全网公示期满零异议 · 权威机构正式颁发终身荣誉与聘书！】' : '⚠️【任用中止】：因前期考核受阻，本次考察程序终止。'}
          </div>
        `;
        btnNext.innerText = isPublicPass ? `就任【${pendingPromotion.targetJob}】` : "服从评审决议，返回岗位蓄力深耕";
      }
    }

    function advancePromotionStep() {
      if (!promoVerdictSuccess && currentPromotionStep < 4) {
        finishPromotionProcess(false);
        return;
      }

      if (currentPromotionStep < 4) {
        currentPromotionStep++;
        renderPromotionStep();
      } else {
        finishPromotionProcess(promoVerdictSuccess);
      }
    }

    function finishPromotionProcess(success) {
      document.getElementById("promotion-modal").classList.add("hidden");

      if (success && pendingPromotion) {
        playSoundEffect('fanfare');
        state.rankName = pendingPromotion.targetRank;
        state.jobTitle = pendingPromotion.targetJob;
        state.rep += 35;
        if (pendingPromotion.isReservePool) {
          state.isInReservePool = true;
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【破格晋升】顺利领衔【${pendingPromotion.targetJob}】，正式列入行业领军骨干重点培养梯队！`, "荣誉晋升");
        } else {
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【程序通关】经严密专家委员会审定，正式履新【${pendingPromotion.targetJob}】！`, "职级晋升");
        }
      } else {
        playSoundEffect('alert');
        if (pendingPromotion && pendingPromotion.is35Split) {
          state.isParallelTrack = true;
          state.rankName = "资深业务顾问";
          state.jobTitle = "战略发展委员会资深特聘顾问";
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【稳健蓄力】高级职级评审竞争激烈遗憾暂缓，转任【资深技术业务顾问】，深耕细作积攒硬核专业资历。`, "深耕蓄力");
        } else {
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "本次竞争性考察选拔受阻，留任原职继续厚积薄发。", "厚积薄发");
        }
      }

      state.age++;
      state.year++;
      updateHeaderUI();
      resumeTimeline();
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 行业高端峰会与第三方合规飞检环节 (带因果关系标记)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function triggerBanquetMiniGame() {
      pauseTimeline();
      playSoundEffect('alert');
      state.handledEvents[`banquet_${state.age}`] = true;
      document.getElementById("banquet-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function selectBanquetChoice(type) {
      document.getElementById("banquet-modal").classList.add("hidden");
      if (type === 'seat_corner') {
        state.eq += 15;
        state.rep += 10;
        state.tags['banquet_humble'] = true;
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【务实交心】在全国领袖峰会晚宴上谦逊务实，与上下游伙伴结成深厚战略同盟！", "行业社交");
      } else if (type === 'seat_bold') {
        state.courage += 10;
        state.rep += 15;
        state.tags['banquet_drank_bold'] = true;
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【全场焦点】在行业领袖峰会晚宴上发表震撼前瞻演讲，赢得头部投资机构追捧！", "峰会领袖");
      } else {
        state.iq += 10;
        state.clean += 5;
        state.tags['banquet_tea'] = true;
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【恪守底线】在晚宴上以茶代酒严控合规，以严谨技术赢得海内外联合研发订单！", "严谨务实");
      }
      state.age++;
      state.year++;
      updateHeaderUI();
      resumeTimeline();
    }

    function triggerInspectionMiniGame() {
      pauseTimeline();
      playSoundEffect('alert');
      state.handledEvents[`insp_${state.age}`] = true;

      // 动态因果提示
      const promptEl = document.getElementById("inspection-prompt-text");
      if (state.tags['chem_park_signed']) {
        promptEl.innerText = '国家联合审计与环保飞检专班抽调原始案卷："请就当年的技术改造环评报告与供应链合规台账进行现场质询答辩！"';
      } else {
        promptEl.innerText = '权威第三方独立质量与合规审计专家组调取档案质询："关于核心工程的工艺技术标准与第三方验收，请出示完整证据链证明其无可挑剔的合规性！"';
      }

      document.getElementById("inspection-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function selectInspectionChoice(type) {
      document.getElementById("inspection-modal").classList.add("hidden");
      if (type === 'show_records') {
        state.clean += 20;
        state.rep += 20;
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【权威质检验收】出示无懈可击的全生命周期质量存证，专家组全票出具'全优合规标杆'评定结论！", "合规认证");
      } else {
        state.clean += 15;
        state.iq += 15;
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, "【穿透审计通过】经受住联合专项质量与合规审核专家组全流程严谨质检，查无实据，考核评定优秀！", "经受体检");
      }
      state.age++;
      state.year++;
      updateHeaderUI();
      resumeTimeline();
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 重大剧情与黑天鹅掷骰检定
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    let activeDecisionEvent = null;

    function openDecisionModal(evt) {
      activeDecisionEvent = evt;
      document.getElementById("decision-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dice-roll-box").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("btn-continue-life").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dec-options-box").classList.remove("hidden");

      document.getElementById("dec-year-tag").innerText = `${state.year}年 · ${state.age}岁`;
      document.getElementById("dec-title").innerText = evt.title;
      
      const appContainer = document.getElementById("app-container");
      const hintEl = document.getElementById("dec-stage-hint");
      if (evt.isBlackSwan) {
        if (state.activeShield) {
          state.activeShield = false;
          playSoundEffect('fanfare');
          showStatToast("🛡️【铁壁防线】生效！成功筑盾抵消本次黑天鹅重大冲击！", "gold");
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【防线化险】提前构筑的铁壁防线生效，无损抵御了突发黑天鹅大考：${evt.title}！`, "神盾化险", "crisis");
          document.getElementById("decision-modal").classList.add("hidden");
          resumeTimeline();
          return;
        }
        appContainer.classList.add("danger-pulse");
        playSoundEffect('alert');
        hintEl.innerText = "⚠️ 突发黑天鹅极端危机 · 祸福相依生死立断";
        hintEl.className = "text-xs font-bold text-red-400 mb-3 animate-pulse";
      } else if (evt.id && evt.id.startsWith("enc_")) {
        hintEl.innerText = "🏮 流年世相微抉择 · 时代浮沉一瞬";
        hintEl.className = "text-xs font-bold text-amber-400 mb-3";
      } else {
        hintEl.innerText = "📜 重大历史关隘抉择 · 时代风云大考";
        hintEl.className = "text-xs text-slate-400 mb-3";
      }

      document.getElementById("dec-scene").innerText = evt.scene;

      const optBox = document.getElementById("dec-options-box");
      optBox.innerHTML = "";
      evt.choices.forEach((choice, idx) => {
        let reqVal = choice.reqVal || 70;
        let curVal = state[choice.statReq] || 60;
        let rate = Math.min(95, Math.max(30, Math.round(50 + (curVal - reqVal) * 1.5)));
        if (state.insightBuff) {
          rate = Math.min(98, rate + 30);
        }

        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = "w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-left text-xs text-sky-300 font-medium active:scale-[0.99] transition-all flex flex-col gap-1";
        btn.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span class="font-bold text-slate-100">${idx + 1}. ${choice.text}</span>
            <span class="text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono font-bold ${rate>=70?'bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800':'bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800'}">成功率 ${rate}%</span>
          </div>
        `;
        btn.onclick = () => executeDiceChoice(choice, rate);
        optBox.appendChild(btn);
      });

      // 检查隐藏绝妙破局选项 (Master Choice / 神之一手 - 深度策略曲线)
      const isMajorEvent = !evt.id || !evt.id.startsWith("enc_");
      if (isMajorEvent) {
        const canMaster = (state.courage >= 80 && state.clean >= 75) || (state.iq >= 80 && state.eq >= 75);
        const masterBtn = document.createElement("button");
        if (canMaster) {
          masterBtn.className = "w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-950/80 via-slate-900 to-amber-950/80 hover:border-amber-400 border-2 border-amber-500/80 text-left text-xs text-amber-300 font-medium active:scale-[0.99] transition-all flex flex-col gap-1 shadow-lg";
          masterBtn.innerHTML = `
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span class="font-bold text-amber-200">✨【绝妙破局 · 两全其美】(神之一手)</span>
              <span class="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono font-bold bg-amber-500 text-slate-950">成功率 96%</span>
            </div>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">【统筹破局】：调动高维智勇与深厚公信，既守住根本底线，又开创性促成多方共赢！</p>
          `;
          const masterChoiceObj = {
            text: "【绝妙破局 · 两全其美】",
            statReq: "courage",
            reqVal: 70,
            successText: "神之一手！不仅完美化解了全场分歧与危机，更树立了全省瞩目的两全典范！名震一方！",
            failText: "虽然执行略显曲折，但依然力保大局稳如泰山！",
            onSuccess: (st) => {
              st.rep += 35;
              st.clean += 15;
              st.iq += 15;
              st.courage += 15;
              st.tags['master_choice_taken'] = true;
            },
            onFail: (st) => { st.rep += 15; }
          };
          masterBtn.onclick = () => executeDiceChoice(masterChoiceObj, 96);
        } else {
          masterBtn.className = "w-full p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/90 text-left text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center justify-between opacity-70 cursor-not-allowed";
          masterBtn.disabled = true;
          masterBtn.innerHTML = `
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate">
              <span>🔒</span>
              <span class="text-xs truncate">【绝妙破局 · 神之一手】(需 魄力≥80 且 清廉≥75，或 智商≥80 且 情商≥75)</span>
            </div>
            <span class="text-xs px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-mono bg-slate-900 text-slate-500 border border-slate-800 shrink-0">未达门槛</span>
          `;
        }
        optBox.appendChild(masterBtn);
      }
    }

    function executeDiceChoice(choice, rate) {
      document.getElementById("dec-options-box").classList.add("hidden");
      const rollBox = document.getElementById("dice-roll-box");
      rollBox.classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dice-roll-text").innerText = "🎲 命运之骰疯狂翻滚检定中……";
      document.getElementById("dice-roll-desc").innerText = `基准成功率 ${rate}%`;

      setTimeout(() => {
        const roll = Math.random() * 100;
        const isCritSuccess = roll <= (rate * 0.25);
        const isSuccess = roll <= rate;

        const isSwan = activeDecisionEvent && activeDecisionEvent.isBlackSwan;
        const isEnc = activeDecisionEvent && activeDecisionEvent.id && activeDecisionEvent.id.startsWith("enc_");

        if (isCritSuccess) {
          playSoundEffect('fanfare');
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-text").innerText = "🌟【天命大成功！声望翻倍！】";
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-text").className = "text-sm font-bold font-mono text-amber-300 animate-bounce";
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-desc").innerText = choice.successText;
          trackStatChanges(() => {
            if (typeof choice.onSuccess === 'function') choice.onSuccess(state);
            state.rep += 20;
          });
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【天命大成功】${choice.successText}`, "功勋卓著", "promo");
        } else if (isSuccess) {
          playSoundEffect('click');
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-text").innerText = "🟢【检定通过 · 顺利达成】";
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-text").className = "text-sm font-bold font-mono text-emerald-400";
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-desc").innerText = choice.successText;
          trackStatChanges(() => {
            if (typeof choice.onSuccess === 'function') choice.onSuccess(state);
          });
          const cat = isSwan ? "crisis" : (isEnc ? "social" : "history");
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, choice.successText, isSwan ? "涉险化解" : "克难攻坚", cat);
        } else {
          playSoundEffect('alert');
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-text").innerText = "💥【检定受阻 · 遭遇曲折】";
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-text").className = "text-sm font-bold font-mono text-red-400";
          document.getElementById("dice-roll-desc").innerText = choice.failText;
          trackStatChanges(() => {
            if (typeof choice.onFail === 'function') choice.onFail(state);
          });
          const cat = isSwan ? "crisis" : (isEnc ? "social" : "history");
          addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【考核受挫】${choice.failText}`, isSwan ? "黑天鹅重挫" : "意外曲折", cat);
        }

        updateHeaderUI();
        document.getElementById("btn-continue-life").classList.remove("hidden");
      }, 500);
    }

    function dismissDecisionResult() {
      document.getElementById("decision-modal").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("app-container").classList.remove("danger-pulse");
      activeDecisionEvent = null;
      state.age++;
      state.year++;
      updateHeaderUI();
      resumeTimeline();
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 五大多元终局盖棺定论系统 (多样化结局)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    function triggerEndgameModal() {
      clampCoreStats(state);
      syncRankProgression(state);
      const finalAge = Math.max(state.age || 60, computeDynamicLifespanCeiling(state));
      document.getElementById("endgame-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
      const titleEl = document.getElementById("end-title");
      const subEl = document.getElementById("end-subtitle");
      const rankEl = document.getElementById("end-rank");
      const jobEl = document.getElementById("end-job");
      const cleanEl = document.getElementById("end-clean");
      const repEl = document.getElementById("end-rep");
      const verdictEl = document.getElementById("end-verdict");

      // 分支 1 (C级): 依法追责 / 资不抵债 (操守失守或破产)
      if (state.clean < 55 || state.capital < 90000 || (state.auditCrisisCount || 0) >= 2) {
        state.endgameGrade = 'C';
        titleEl.innerText = "🚨【C级 · 折戟沉沙 · 晚节不保】";
        titleEl.className = "text-xl font-black text-red-500 mb-1";
        subEl.innerText = `${state.name} · 终局结案 (${finalAge}岁)`;
        rankEl.innerText = "行业终身禁入 · 严重违规失信";
        jobEl.innerText = state.jobTitle;
        cleanEl.innerText = `失守底线 (${state.clean}点)`;
        repEl.innerText = `声誉受损 (${state.rep}点)`;
        verdictEl.innerText = "未能抵御住巨额商业利益诱惑或经营失控，在重大资产运作与项目中触犯红线，最终折戟沉沙，令人深省与扼腕！";
        return;
      }

      // 分支 2 (S级): 一代宗师 (需职级>=4、声望>=83、清廉>=75、零违纪零债务零健康危机)
      if (state.lifetimeMaxRank >= 4 && state.rep >= 83 && state.clean >= 75 && (state.auditCrisisCount || 0) === 0 && (state.debtCrisisCount || 0) === 0 && (state.healthCrisisCount || 0) === 0) {
        state.endgameGrade = 'S';
        titleEl.innerText = "🏆【S级 · 一代宗师 · 功勋昭著】";
        titleEl.className = "text-xl font-black text-amber-300 mb-1";
        subEl.innerText = `${state.name} · 享年 ${finalAge} 岁`;
        rankEl.innerText = state.rankName;
        jobEl.innerText = state.jobTitle;
        cleanEl.innerText = `德艺双馨 (${state.clean}点)`;
        repEl.innerText = `${state.rep}点 (举世公认行业泰斗)`;
        verdictEl.innerText = "数十载寒暑易节，择一事终一生。在所属领域倾注毕生心血，突破多项重大核心战略关卡，培育桃李英才遍天下，被全社会尊为当之无愧的行业泰斗与时代脊梁！";
        return;
      }

      // 分支 3 (A级): 时代先锋 (高魄力、高声望、零违纪)
      if (state.lifetimeMaxRank >= 3 && state.courage >= 76 && state.rep >= 73 && state.clean >= 70 && (state.auditCrisisCount || 0) === 0) {
        state.endgameGrade = 'A';
        titleEl.innerText = "🌟【A级 · 敢为人先 · 时代先锋】";
        titleEl.className = "text-xl font-black text-sky-400 mb-1";
        subEl.innerText = `${state.name} · 享年 ${finalAge} 岁 · 行业先锋模范`;
        rankEl.innerText = state.rankName;
        jobEl.innerText = state.jobTitle;
        cleanEl.innerText = `大公无私 (${state.clean}点)`;
        repEl.innerText = `${state.rep}点 (万众敬仰 · 功德流芳)`;
        verdictEl.innerText = "在重大时代变革与关键攻坚一线中，夜以继日鏖战在最核心的岗位上，用赤诚与担当书写了名家风范！";
        return;
      }

      // 分支 4 (A级): 实业巨擘 / 行业名宿 (职级>=3、高资产或高声望、零违纪)
      if (state.lifetimeMaxRank >= 3 && (state.capital >= 460000 || state.iq >= 75 || state.eq >= 74) && state.rep >= 72 && state.clean >= 69 && (state.auditCrisisCount || 0) === 0) {
        state.endgameGrade = 'A';
        titleEl.innerText = "🚢【A级 · 时代弄潮 · 行业名宿】";
        titleEl.className = "text-xl font-black text-amber-400 mb-1";
        subEl.innerText = `${state.name} · 享年 ${finalAge} 岁 · 行业领航者`;
        rankEl.innerText = state.rankName;
        jobEl.innerText = state.jobTitle;
        cleanEl.innerText = `守正笃实 (${state.clean}点)`;
        repEl.innerText = `${state.rep}点 (享誉海内外行业典范)`;
        verdictEl.innerText = "在现代化经济与社会发展浪潮中敢为人先，以卓越专业造诣打造出享誉业界的标杆成就，成为实干兴邦的杰出代表！";
        return;
      }

      // 分支 5 (B级): 平安顺遂 (勤勉中坚)
      state.endgameGrade = 'B';
      titleEl.innerText = "🍵【B级 · 勤勉中坚 · 平安顺遂】";
      titleEl.className = "text-xl font-black text-slate-300 mb-1";
      subEl.innerText = `${state.name} · 享年 ${finalAge} 岁 · 荣休安享晚年`;
      rankEl.innerText = state.rankName;
      jobEl.innerText = "战略与学术发展特聘顾问";
      cleanEl.innerText = "遵纪守法 · 作风清白";
      repEl.innerText = `${state.rep}点 (平稳顺遂度过精彩一生)`;
      verdictEl.innerText = "三十余载兢兢业业，恪守职业操守，在专业岗位上留下了扎实稳健的行业贡献与良好口碑。晚年退居幕后担任高级特聘顾问，儿孙绕膝安享天伦之乐！";
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    // 群像对话与微信私聊 (支持分类筛选与多阶段职级动态对白)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------
    let currentContactsFilter = "all";

    function filterContacts(cat) {
      currentContactsFilter = cat;
      ['all', 'gov', 'family', 'biz', 'civ'].forEach(k => {
        const btn = document.getElementById(`c-filter-${k}`);
        if (!btn) return;
        if (k === cat) {
          btn.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800 font-bold shrink-0";
        } else {
          btn.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 border border-slate-800 hover:text-slate-200 shrink-0";
        }
      });
      renderContacts();
    }

    function getTrustTier(trust) {
      if (trust >= 85) return { text: "🌟 莫逆之交", class: "text-amber-300 border-amber-500 bg-amber-950/60" };
      if (trust >= 65) return { text: "🤝 深受信赖", class: "text-emerald-300 border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-950/60" };
      if (trust >= 45) return { text: "☕ 相谈甚欢", class: "text-sky-300 border-sky-600 bg-sky-950/60" };
      if (trust >= 25) return { text: "👋 点头之交", class: "text-slate-300 border-slate-700 bg-slate-900" };
      return { text: "⚠️ 心存戒备", class: "text-rose-400 border-rose-800 bg-rose-950/60" };
    }

    function renderContacts() {
      const container = document.getElementById("contacts-list");
      if (!container) return;
      container.innerHTML = "";

      const catBadges = {
        gov: { label: "🏛️ 公共治理", class: "bg-red-950 text-red-300 border-red-800" },
        family: { label: "👨‍👩‍👦 亲友家庭", class: "bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-800" },
        biz: { label: "💼 商海实业", class: "bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border-amber-800" },
        civ: { label: "👥 基层民意", class: "bg-sky-950 text-sky-300 border-sky-800" },
        tech: { label: "🧪 科研科技", class: "bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-800" }
      };

      ENSEMBLE_CHARACTERS.forEach((npc, index) => {
        if (currentContactsFilter !== "all" && npc.category !== currentContactsFilter) {
          return;
        }

        const badgeInfo = catBadges[npc.category] || { label: "社会群像", class: "bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border-slate-700" };
        const tier = getTrustTier(npc.trust);
        const card = document.createElement("div");
        card.className = "bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-3 rounded-xl flex flex-col gap-2 hover:border-slate-700 transition-all shadow-sm";
        card.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center text-xl shrink-0 border border-slate-700">
                ${npc.avatar}
              </div>
              <div class="truncate">
                <div class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                  <span>${npc.name}</span>
                  <span class="text-xs px-1 py-0.2 rounded border font-normal ${badgeInfo.class}">${badgeInfo.label}</span>
                  <span class="text-xs px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded border font-mono font-bold ${tier.class}">${tier.text}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5 truncate">${npc.role}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="text-right shrink-0">
              <div class="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-amber-300">${npc.trust}/100</div>
              <div class="w-16 bg-slate-800 h-1 rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-1">
                <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-600 to-amber-400 h-full rounded-full" style="width: ${Math.min(100, npc.trust)}%"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- 深度互动动作栏 (密谈 / 求助 / 馈赠) -->
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-1.5 border-t border-slate-800/80 text-xs">
            <button onclick="openChatModal(${index})" class="flex-1 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-sky-300 font-bold border border-slate-700 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
              <span>💬</span> 密谈交心
            </button>
            <button onclick="seekCharacterAssistance(${index})" class="flex-1 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-300 font-bold border border-slate-700 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
              <span>🤝</span> 紧急相助
            </button>
            <button onclick="giftCharacter(${index})" class="flex-1 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-300 font-bold border border-slate-700 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
              <span>🎁</span> 馈赠心意
            </button>
          </div>
        `;
        container.appendChild(card);
      });
    }

    function seekCharacterAssistance(idx) {
      const npc = ENSEMBLE_CHARACTERS[idx];
      if (!npc) return;
      if (npc.trust < 45) {
        showStatToast(`与【${npc.name}】关系尚浅，需好感≥45！`, 'bad');
        return;
      }

      npc.trust -= 15;
      playSoundEffect('fanfare');

      if (npc.category === 'gov') {
        state.clean += 15;
        state.rep += 20;
        showStatToast(`【${npc.name}】提供关键政策统筹指导，声誉+20！`, 'gold');
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【协同支持】与${npc.name}进行深度专业会商，获得宝贵政策合规指导与资源统筹！`, "机制协同", "social");
      } else if (npc.category === 'biz') {
        state.capital += 80000;
        showStatToast(`【${npc.name}】调拨商业资金周转，存款+8万！`, 'gold');
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【商界纾困】${npc.name}在关键节点调拨八万元资金为实体周转解围！`, "商业过桥", "social");
      } else if (npc.category === 'family') {
        state.health = Math.min(100, state.health + 20);
        showStatToast(`【${npc.name}】至亲温暖照料，精力+20！`, 'good');
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【家和万事兴】与至亲${npc.name}长谈倾诉，身心重焕生机！`, "亲情治愈", "social");
      } else {
        state.iq += 15;
        state.rep += 15;
        showStatToast(`【${npc.name}】提供民间基石支持，大局+15！`, 'good');
        addChronicleItem(state.age, state.year, `【民意归心】${npc.name}率领团队/街坊在关键关隘提供宝贵支持！`, "群众坚盾", "social");
      }

      renderContacts();
      updateHeaderUI();
    }

    function giftCharacter(idx) {
      checkOnboardingTask("social");
      const npc = ENSEMBLE_CHARACTERS[idx];
      if (!npc) return;
      if (state.capital < 2000) {
        showStatToast("个人存款不足2000元，囊中羞涩！", "bad");
        return;
      }

      state.capital -= 2000;
      npc.trust = Math.min(100, npc.trust + 10);
      playSoundEffect('like');
      showStatToast(`馈赠家乡特产，${npc.name}信任+10！`, 'good');
      renderContacts();
      updateHeaderUI();
    }

    let activeNpcIndex = 0;
    function openChatModal(npcIndex) {
      pauseTimeline();
      activeNpcIndex = npcIndex;
      const npc = ENSEMBLE_CHARACTERS[npcIndex];
      document.getElementById("chat-avatar").innerText = npc.avatar;
      document.getElementById("chat-name").innerText = npc.name;
      document.getElementById("chat-role").innerText = npc.role;

      // 随主角年龄与职级演化3阶段动态对白 (青年起步 <35岁 / 壮年攻坚 35-47岁 / 晚年沉淀 48+岁)
      let dlgIdx = 0;
      if (state.age >= 48) {
        dlgIdx = npc.dialogues[2] ? 2 : (npc.dialogues[1] ? 1 : 0);
      } else if (state.age >= 35) {
        dlgIdx = npc.dialogues[1] ? 1 : 0;
      }
      const dlg = npc.dialogues[dlgIdx] || npc.dialogues[0];
      const flow = document.getElementById("chat-dialogue-flow");
      flow.innerHTML = `
        <div class="flex gap-2 items-start">
          <span class="text-xl shrink-0 mt-0.5">${npc.avatar}</span>
          <div class="bg-slate-800 p-2.5 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 max-w-[85%] leading-relaxed font-serif shadow-sm">
            ${dlg.text}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      const optBox = document.getElementById("chat-options-box");
      optBox.innerHTML = "";
      dlg.options.forEach((opt, idx) => {
        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = "w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-left text-xs text-sky-300 font-medium active:scale-[0.99] transition-all flex items-center justify-between";
        btn.innerHTML = `<span>${idx + 1}. ${opt.text}</span><span class="text-slate-500 text-xs">回复 →</span>`;
        btn.onclick = () => selectChatReply(opt);
        optBox.appendChild(btn);
      });

      document.getElementById("chat-modal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    function selectChatReply(opt) {
      const npc = ENSEMBLE_CHARACTERS[activeNpcIndex];
      const flow = document.getElementById("chat-dialogue-flow");

      const userBubble = document.createElement("div");
      userBubble.className = "flex gap-2 items-start justify-end";
      userBubble.innerHTML = `
        <div class="bg-red-950/80 p-2.5 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none text-red-100 border border-red-800 max-w-[85%] leading-relaxed font-serif shadow-sm text-right">
          ${opt.text}
        </div>
        <span class="text-xl shrink-0 mt-0.5">🏛️</span>
      `;
      flow.appendChild(userBubble);

      setTimeout(() => {
        const replyBubble = document.createElement("div");
        replyBubble.className = "flex gap-2 items-start";
        replyBubble.innerHTML = `
          <span class="text-xl shrink-0 mt-0.5">${npc.avatar}</span>
          <div class="bg-slate-800 p-2.5 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-none text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 max-w-[85%] leading-relaxed font-serif shadow-sm">
            ${opt.reply}
          </div>
        `;
        flow.appendChild(replyBubble);
        replyBubble.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });

        if (opt.trustDelta) npc.trust = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, npc.trust + opt.trustDelta));
        if (opt.repDelta) state.rep += opt.repDelta;
        if (opt.cleanDelta) state.clean += opt.cleanDelta;
        if (opt.eqDelta) state.eq += opt.eqDelta;
        if (opt.courageDelta) state.courage += opt.courageDelta;
        if (opt.iqDelta) state.iq += opt.iqDelta;
        if (opt.capitalDelta) state.capital += opt.capitalDelta;
        if (opt.tag) state.tags[opt.tag] = true;

        renderContacts();
        updateHeaderUI();

        document.getElementById("chat-options-box").innerHTML = `
          <button onclick="closeChatModal()" class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl red-btn text-xs font-bold">
            记入心间 · 结束私聊并继续人生 →
          </button>
        `;
      }, 300);
    }

    function closeChatModal() {
      document.getElementById("chat-modal").classList.add("hidden");
      resumeTimeline();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 华夏三千年世家大系 · 核心运行与宗祠长卷引擎 (ClanCoreEngine)
    // =========================================================================

      'medical_herb': { name: '🌿 悬壶济世 · 杏林春暖', shortName: '悬壶济世', icon: '🌿', desc: '杏林春暖，历代大疫免疫加倍，族人长寿天花板上扬' },
      'iron_craft': { name: '⚒️ 百工机巧 · 经纬乾坤', shortName: '百工机巧', icon: '⚒️', desc: '规矩尺度，水利机械与硬核科技发明具备祖传匠心' }
    };

    let currentSetupClan = {
      surname: '陈',
      province: '河南',
      hallName: '颍川堂',
      vocationId: 'study_scholar',
      motto: '诗书继世长，清白传家久。'
    };

    let currentClanSubTab = 'genealogy';

    function getClanProfile() {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem("lifesim_clan_profile");
        if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
      } catch (e) {}
      return null;
    }

    function saveClanProfile(p) {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("lifesim_clan_profile", JSON.stringify(p));
      } catch (e) {}
    }
