/**
 * test_fission_monetization.js
 * Comprehensive Verification Suite for:
 * 1. 🔥 全服每日同命挑战 (Daily Seed Challenge) - Determinism, Theming & Leaderboard Sync
 * 2. 📜 宣纸长卷海报一键生成与朋友圈分享 (Canvas to TempFilePath & ShowShareImageMenu)
 * 3. 💰 高价值激励视频场景 (【天机重演·逆天改命】 & 【特区内参·绝密行情情报】)
 */

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const assert = require('assert');

// Mock browser / wechat minigame environment
const noop = () => {};
const drawCalls = [];
const ctx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => {
    if (k === 'measureText') return (text) => ({ width: (text ? text.length * 10 : 10) });
    if (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') {
      return () => ({
        addColorStop: noop
      });
    }
    if (k === 'createPattern') return () => ({});
    if (t[k] !== undefined) return t[k];
    return (...args) => {
      drawCalls.push({ method: k, args });
    };
  },
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});

const mockCanvas = {
  width: 750,
  height: 1334,
  getContext: () => ctx,
  toTempFilePathSync: () => 'wxfile://tmp_life_trajectory_poster_20261014.png',
  toTempFilePath: (opts) => {
    if (opts && typeof opts.success === 'function') {
      opts.success({ tempFilePath: 'wxfile://tmp_life_trajectory_poster_20261014.png' });
    }
    if (opts && typeof opts.complete === 'function') {
      opts.complete();
    }
  }
};

let storageStore = {};
let postedMessages = [];
let shareMenuCalledWith = null;
let savedImageFilePath = null;
let toastShown = null;

global.wx = {
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 812, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 44, bottom: 778 } }),
  createCanvas: () => ({ ...mockCanvas }),
  createOffscreenCanvas: (opts) => ({ ...mockCanvas, width: (opts && opts.width) || 750, height: (opts && opts.height) || 1334 }),
  createWebAudioContext: () => null,
  getStorageSync: k => storageStore[k],
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => { storageStore[k] = v; },
  removeStorageSync: k => { delete storageStore[k]; },
  vibrateShort: noop,
  vibrateLong: noop,
  onTouchStart: noop,
  onTouchMove: noop,
  onTouchEnd: noop,
  onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop,
  onHide: noop,
  onWindowResize: noop,
  onAudioInterruptionBegin: noop,
  onAudioInterruptionEnd: noop,
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 48, right: 365, bottom: 80, left: 278, width: 87, height: 32 }),
  getSystemInfo: noop,
  getAccountInfoSync: () => ({ miniProgram: { envVersion: 'develop' } }),
  reportEvent: noop,
  showShareMenu: noop,
  onShareAppMessage: noop,
  onShareTimeline: noop,
  getLaunchOptionsSync: () => ({ query: {} }),
  getUpdateManager: () => ({ onCheckForUpdate: noop, onUpdateReady: noop, onUpdateFailed: noop }),
  getOpenDataContext: () => ({
    postMessage: (msg) => {
      postedMessages.push(msg);
    }
  }),
  showShareImageMenu: (opts) => {
    shareMenuCalledWith = opts;
    if (opts && typeof opts.success === 'function') opts.success();
  },
  saveImageToPhotosAlbum: (opts) => {
    savedImageFilePath = opts && opts.filePath;
    if (opts && typeof opts.success === 'function') opts.success();
  },
  showToast: (opts) => {
    toastShown = opts;
  },
  createRewardedVideoAd: (opts) => {
    let onLoadCb = noop;
    let onErrorCb = noop;
    let onCloseCb = noop;
    return {
      load: () => Promise.resolve(),
      show: () => {
        if (onCloseCb) onCloseCb({ isEnded: true });
        return Promise.resolve();
      },
      onLoad: fn => { onLoadCb = fn; },
      onError: fn => { onErrorCb = fn; },
      onClose: fn => { onCloseCb = fn; },
      offClose: noop
    };
  }
};

global.requestAnimationFrame = noop;
global.canvas = mockCanvas;
global.Image = function () {};
global.document = undefined;

// Load game.js
const gamePath = path.resolve(__dirname, 'wechat-minigame/game.js');
console.log('Loading game engine for fission & monetization verification from:', gamePath);
const engine = require(gamePath);
const state = global.state;

let passedTests = 0;
let totalTests = 0;

function runTest(name, fn) {
  totalTests++;
  try {
    fn();
    console.log(`  ✅ [PASS] ${name}`);
    passedTests++;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`  ❌ [FAIL] ${name}`);
    console.error('    Error:', err.message);
    if (err.stack) console.error(err.stack);
  }
}

console.log('\n======================================================');
console.log('🧪 SUITE 1: 全服每日同命挑战 (Daily Seed Challenge)');
console.log('======================================================');

runTest('1.1 确定性种子解析 (getDailyChallengeSeed)', () => {
  const seed1 = engine.getDailyChallengeSeed('2026-10-14');
  const seed2 = engine.getDailyChallengeSeed('20261014');
  assert.strictEqual(seed1, 20261014, 'Date with dashes should resolve to integer 20261014');
  assert.strictEqual(seed2, 20261014, 'Numeric string should resolve to integer 20261014');

  const details = engine.getDailyChallengeDetails();
  assert.ok(details.dateStr, 'Must contain date string');
  assert.ok(typeof details.seed === 'number', 'Seed must be a number');
  assert.ok(details.theme, 'Must resolve an active theme');
  assert.ok(details.theme.title, 'Theme must have a title');
});

runTest('1.2 每日挑战主题池包含公元100年永元筑基与汉魏风骨主题', () => {
  const themes = engine.DAILY_CHALLENGE_THEMES;
  assert.ok(Array.isArray(themes), 'DAILY_CHALLENGE_THEMES should be an array');
  const yongyuan = themes.find(t => t.eraYear === 118 || t.title.includes('永元筑基'));
  assert.ok(yongyuan, 'Should include 公元100年 永元筑基 themed challenge');
  assert.ok(yongyuan.classType.includes('经学') || yongyuan.classType.includes('冠冕'), 'Class type should be 经学冠冕: ' + yongyuan.classType);
});

runTest('1.3 全服同一起跑线：相同种子产出100%一致的初始特质与身世', () => {
  // Run 1
  engine.startDailyChallengeRun();
  const run1Seed = state.runSeed;
  const run1BirthYear = state.birthYear;
  const run1Class = state.birthProfile.parents.classType;
  const run1Name = state.name;
  const run1Stats = { ...state.stats };

  // Run 2
  engine.startDailyChallengeRun();
  const run2Seed = state.runSeed;
  const run2BirthYear = state.birthYear;
  const run2Class = state.birthProfile.parents.classType;
  const run2Name = state.name;
  const run2Stats = { ...state.stats };

  assert.strictEqual(run1Seed, run2Seed, 'Seeds must match');
  assert.strictEqual(run1BirthYear, run2BirthYear, 'Birth years must match');
  assert.strictEqual(run1Class, run2Class, 'Parent social class must match');
  assert.strictEqual(run1Name, run2Name, 'Names must match under deterministic PRNG');
  assert.deepStrictEqual(run1Stats, run2Stats, 'Core stats must match 100%');
  assert.strictEqual(state.isDailyChallenge, true, 'isDailyChallenge flag must be active');
  assert.strictEqual(state.age, 18, 'Must be fast-forwarded to adulthood (age 18)');
});

runTest('1.4 每日挑战终局评分与微信好友同命榜数据同步 (uploadDailyChallengeScore)', () => {
  postedMessages = [];
  state.isDailyChallenge = true;
  state.age = 60;
  state.stats = { wisdom: 95, health: 80, charm: 85, wealth: 5000000, reputation: 90 };
  state.achievements = ['gaokao_top', 'first_pot_of_gold'];
  state.familyClan = { children: [{ name: '长子' }, { name: '次女' }] };

  const score = engine.calcDailyChallengeScore(state);
  assert.ok(score > 1000, `Calculated score should reflect substantial achievement: ${score}`);

  engine.uploadDailyChallengeScore(score);
  assert.strictEqual(postedMessages.length, 1, 'Should post 1 message to OpenDataContext');
  assert.strictEqual(postedMessages[0].type, 'UPDATE_DAILY_SCORE');
  assert.strictEqual(postedMessages[0].score, score);
  assert.strictEqual(postedMessages[0].date, state.dailyChallengeDate);
});

  drawCalls.length = 0;
  let returnedPath = null;
  engine.generateLifeTrajectoryPoster((path) => {
    returnedPath = path;
  });

  assert.ok(returnedPath, 'generateLifeTrajectoryPoster must return tempFilePath');
  assert.ok(state.lastGeneratedPosterCanvas, 'state.lastGeneratedPosterCanvas must be cached');
  assert.strictEqual(state.lastGeneratedPosterCanvas.width, 750, 'Poster width should be 750px');
  assert.strictEqual(state.lastGeneratedPosterCanvas.height, 1334, 'Poster height should be 1334px');

  // Verify that stroke, fillText, and arc were called during rendering (trajectory graph, seals, sun code)
  const hasStroke = drawCalls.some(c => c.method === 'stroke');
  const hasFillText = drawCalls.some(c => c.method === 'fillText');
  const hasArc = drawCalls.some(c => c.method === 'arc');
  assert.ok(hasStroke, 'Must execute stroke calls for borders and trajectory lines');
  assert.ok(hasFillText, 'Must execute fillText for calligraphy, dates, and title');
  assert.ok(hasArc, 'Must execute arc calls for cinnabar seal / sun code');
});

runTest('2.2 一键分享海报到朋友圈 (shareLifeTrajectoryPosterToMoments)', () => {
  shareMenuCalledWith = null;
  state.lastGeneratedPosterPath = null;

  engine.shareLifeTrajectoryPosterToMoments();

  assert.ok(shareMenuCalledWith.path.includes('wxfile://'), 'Must pass tempFilePath');

});

console.log('\n======================================================');
console.log('🧪 SUITE 3: 高转化激励视频 (逆天改命 & 特区内参)');
console.log('======================================================');

runTest('3.1 【天机重演 · 逆天改命】：大失败后回滚惩罚并以+15%胜率重掷（限1次/局）', () => {
  state.usedRerollAd = false;
  state.rerollFateUsedCount = 0;
  state.stats.wealth = 75;

  state.lastDecisionChoice = {
    text: '竞标时代重大枢纽工程',
    successStat: 'wealth',
    checkStat: 'wealth',
    successDelta: 30,
    failDelta: -25,
    consequence: '大获全胜',
    failConsequence: '遭遇重大暗礁，血本无归'
  };
  state.lastChoiceBaseRate = 40;
  state.lastChoiceRollVerdict = 'fail';
  state._preRollSnapshot = {
    stats: { ...state.stats },
    age: state.age
  };

  // Simulate loss applied on failure
  state.stats.wealth -= 25;
  assert.strictEqual(state.stats.wealth, 50, 'Stat penalty applied on failure');

  // Trigger Fate Reroll
  let rerolledSuccess = false;
  engine.watchAdForFateReroll((ok) => {
    if (ok) {
      engine.rerollFailedDecision();
      rerolledSuccess = true;
    }
  });

  assert.strictEqual(rerolledSuccess, true, 'Rewarded ad onSuccess callback fired');
  assert.strictEqual(state.rerollFateUsedCount, 1, 'Reroll fate count incremented to 1');
  assert.strictEqual(state.usedRerollAd, true, 'usedRerollAd marked true');

  // Pre-roll stat snapshot should have been restored during reroll
  // (Wealth was restored to 75, then re-rolled with +15% boost = 55% success rate)
  assert.ok(state.stats.wealth >= 50, 'Stats should be at least restored/recalculated');

  // Attempt second reroll in same run
  let secondReroll = false;
  engine.watchAdForFateReroll((ok) => {
    if (ok) secondReroll = true;
  });
  assert.strictEqual(secondReroll, false, 'Second reroll in same run must be rejected (max 1/run)');
});

runTest('3.2 【特区内参 · 绝密行情情报】：解锁3年预测，保障投资保本与+12%智谋红利', () => {
  state.specialZoneIntelUnlocked = false;
  state.year = 1992;
  state.capital = 10000;
  state.heldAssets = {
    realEstateShares: 20000
  };
  state.marketTrends = {
    realEstateChange: '-15%' // 宏观大跌15%
  };

  // 1. Unlock intel via ad
  let intelUnlocked = false;
  engine.watchAdForMarketIntel((ok) => {
    if (ok) intelUnlocked = true;
  });
  assert.strictEqual(intelUnlocked, true, 'Market intel ad onSuccess callback fired');
  assert.strictEqual(state.specialZoneIntelUnlocked, true, 'Intel unlocked flag must be true');
  assert.strictEqual(state.specialZoneIntelExpireYear, 1995, 'Expires after 3 years (1992 + 3 = 1995)');

  // 2. Sell asset during down market
  // Without intel: -15% -> returns 20000 * 0.85 = 17000 (loss of 3000)
  // With intel: loss turned into +5% protected minimum -> 20000 * 1.05 = 21000!
  const prevCap = state.capital;
  engine.sellMarketAsset('realEstateShares', 20000);
  const proceedsGained = state.capital - prevCap;
  assert.strictEqual(proceedsGained, 21000, 'Proceeds must turn loss (-15%) into protected positive gain (+5% = 21000)');
  assert.strictEqual(state.heldAssets.realEstateShares, 0, 'All shares sold');

  // 3. Test expiry when year advances past expire year
  state.year = 1996;
  const hasIntelExpired = state.specialZoneIntelUnlocked && (state.year <= state.specialZoneIntelExpireYear);
  assert.strictEqual(hasIntelExpired, false, 'Intel should expire after 1995');
});

console.log('\n======================================================');
console.log(`🏁 VERIFICATION SUMMARY: ${passedTests}/${totalTests} TESTS PASSED`);
console.log('======================================================\n');

if (passedTests === totalTests) {
  process.exit(0);
} else {
  process.exit(1);
}

/**
 * 华夏世家大系 · 第一赛季汉魏风骨（公元100~300年）

const assert = require('assert');

// 1. 模拟微信小游戏环境与 Canvas 上下文
const noop = () => {};
const mockCanvasCalls = [];
const mockCtx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => {
    if (k === 'measureText') return (text) => ({ width: (text ? text.length : 0) * 12 });
    if (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') return () => ({ addColorStop: noop });
    if (k === 'createPattern') return () => ({});
    if (k === 'roundRect' || k === 'fillRect' || k === 'strokeRect' || k === 'fillText') {
      return (...args) => {
        mockCanvasCalls.push({ method: k, args });
      };
    }
    if (t[k] !== undefined) return t[k];
    return noop;
  },
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});

const canvasObj = {
  width: 750,
  height: 1334,
  getContext: () => mockCtx
};

global._storage = {};
global.wx = {
  getStorageSync: (k) => global._storage[k] || null,
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => { global._storage[k] = v; },
  removeStorageSync: (k) => { delete global._storage[k]; },
  getSystemInfoSync: () => ({ screenWidth: 375, screenHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667 }),
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 20, bottom: 647 } }),
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 24, bottom: 56, left: 280, right: 365, width: 85, height: 32 }),
  onTouchStart: noop,
  onTouchMove: noop,
  onTouchEnd: noop,
  onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop,
  onHide: noop,
  onError: noop,
  createCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createInnerAudioContext: () => ({ play: noop, stop: noop, pause: noop, destroy: noop, onEnded: noop, onError: noop }),
  vibrateShort: noop,
  vibrateLong: noop,
  getPerformance: () => ({ now: () => Date.now() }),
  triggerGC: noop,
  setPreferredFramesPerSecond: noop
};

global.canvas = canvasObj;

// 加载游戏主模块

console.log('  ✅ 0~17岁全年龄段里程碑文案纯净，无任何现代高考/进厂/大学字符！\n');

/**
 * 自动化测试套件：热更安全签名、数值裁剪防误伤、MSCP赛季内容包产线与全链路关键埋点
 * 运行方式: node test_hotupdate_security_and_pipeline.js
 */

const assert = require('assert');

console.log('====================================================');
console.log('🧪 启动自动化测试：热更防篡改、数值裁剪白名单、MSCP产线与关键埋点');
console.log('====================================================');

// 1. 模拟微信运行环境桩
global.wx = {
  getSystemInfoSync: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 812, pixelRatio: 2, platform: 'devtools' }),
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 48, bottom: 80, left: 290, right: 365, width: 75, height: 32 }),
  createCanvas: () => ({
    getContext: () => ({
      save: () => {},
      restore: () => {},
      fillRect: () => {},
      strokeRect: () => {},
      rect: () => {},
      arcTo: () => {},
      fillText: () => {},
      measureText: (txt) => ({ width: (txt || '').length * 10 }),
      beginPath: () => {},
      closePath: () => {},
      moveTo: () => {},
      lineTo: () => {},
      arc: () => {},
      fill: () => {},
      stroke: () => {},
      clip: () => {},
      translate: () => {},
      rotate: () => {},
      scale: () => {},
      createLinearGradient: () => ({ addColorStop: () => {} }),
      setLineDash: () => {}
    }),
    width: 375,
    height: 812
  }),
  onTouchStart: () => {},
  onTouchMove: () => {},
  onTouchEnd: () => {},
  getStorageSync: (k) => null,
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => {},
  removeStorageSync: (k) => {},
  onShow: () => {},
  onHide: () => {},
  onError: () => {},
  showToast: () => {},
  vibrateShort: () => {},
  request: () => {},
  reportEvent: (evt, data) => {
    lastReportedMilestone = evt;
    lastReportedData = data;
    reportedMilestones.push({ eventName: evt, data });
  }
};

let lastReportedMilestone = null;
let lastReportedData = null;
const reportedMilestones = [];

// 2. 加载模块
const contentUpdater = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/contentUpdater');
const seasonConfig = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/seasonConfig');
const clanSocial = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/clanSocialSystem');
const gameEngine = require('./wechat-minigame/game');

// ----------------------------------------------------
// 验证 1: 热更安全验签 (Hot Update Security)
// ----------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n--- 1. 验证热更安全验签防御能力 ---');

// 1.1 无签名的远程包必须被彻底拒绝
const unsignedRemote = {
  manifestVersion: '2026.11.01',
  timestamp: Date.now(),
  storiesPatch: { 2030: [{ title: '恶意注入' }] }
};
const resUnsigned = contentUpdater.verifyManifestSignature(unsignedRemote, contentUpdater.HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT, true);
assert.strictEqual(resUnsigned, false, '无签名的远程热更包必须被坚决拒之门外！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 远程无签名热更包被严格拦截并拒收');

// 1.2 篡改或伪造签名的远程包必须被拒绝
const forgedRemote = {
  manifestVersion: '2026.11.01',
  timestamp: 1729000000000,
  storiesPatch: { 2030: [{ title: '黑客修改事件' }] },
  signature: 'deadbeef_invalid'
};
const resForged = contentUpdater.verifyManifestSignature(forgedRemote, contentUpdater.HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT, true);
assert.strictEqual(resForged, false, '伪造签名的远程热更包必须被验签算法拒绝！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 伪造签名的远程包验签失败并被拦截');

// 1.3 远程包使用源码默认公开盐签名必须坚决拒收（杜绝公开盐假冒签名）
const defaultSaltSignedRemote = {
  manifestVersion: '2026.11.01',
  timestamp: Date.now(),
  storiesPatch: []
};
defaultSaltSignedRemote.signature = contentUpdater.calculateManifestChecksum(defaultSaltSignedRemote, contentUpdater.HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT);
const resDefaultSalt = contentUpdater.verifyManifestSignature(defaultSaltSignedRemote, contentUpdater.HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT, true);
assert.strictEqual(resDefaultSalt, false, '远程包使用公开默认盐自签名必须被彻底拒绝！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 远程包即便用源码默认盐自签名也被严格拦截拒收');

// 1.4 配置专属私有动态安全密钥的远程热更包验签通过
const customSecret = 'SECURE_PRODUCTION_SALT_2026_CUSTOM';
const customSignedRemote = {
  manifestVersion: '2026.11.01',
  timestamp: Date.now(),
  storiesPatch: []
};
customSignedRemote.signature = contentUpdater.calculateManifestChecksum(customSignedRemote, customSecret);
const resCustom = contentUpdater.verifyManifestSignature(customSignedRemote, customSecret, true);
assert.strictEqual(resCustom, true, '配置私有动态安全密钥的远程热更包必须合法验签放行！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 具备专属私有安全密钥的远程热更包合法验签放行');

// 1.5 微信云开发权威授信通道直接放行
const cloudVerifiedManifest = {
  manifestVersion: '2026.11.01',
  _fromCloudFunction: true,
  _cloudVerified: true,
  rewards: { promotionBonus: 25000 }
};
const resCloud = contentUpdater.verifyManifestSignature(cloudVerifiedManifest, contentUpdater.HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT, true);
assert.strictEqual(resCloud, true, '微信云开发通道经由安全信道交付，必须权威授信放行！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 微信云开发权威通道热更包直接放行通过');

// ----------------------------------------------------
// 验证 2: 数值裁剪白名单与前沿线支持至3100年
// ----------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n--- 2. 验证数值裁剪白名单与前沿线放开至3100年 ---');

const rawManifest = {
  frontierYear: 2850,
  worldChrono: { frontierYear: 2950 },
  rewards: {
    promotionBonus: 22000,
    endgameSGradeBaseScore: 1000,
    endgameAGradeBaseScore: 750,
    arbitraryAttributeDelta: 80 // 非白名单大额属性增量
  }
};

const clamped = contentUpdater.clampManifestValues(rawManifest);

// 2.1 promotionBonus 和 endgameSGradeBaseScore 不能被裁为 30
assert.strictEqual(clamped.rewards.promotionBonus, 22000, 'promotionBonus 不应被误伤裁剪为30！');
assert.strictEqual(clamped.rewards.endgameSGradeBaseScore, 1000, 'endgameSGradeBaseScore 不应被误伤裁剪为30！');
assert.strictEqual(clamped.rewards.endgameAGradeBaseScore, 750, 'endgameAGradeBaseScore 不应被误伤裁剪为30！');

// 2.2 普通属性仍应被严格裁剪在 ±30 内
assert.strictEqual(clamped.rewards.arbitraryAttributeDelta, 30, '非金额/积分的纯属性增量必须被安全裁剪至 ±30！');

// 2.3 前沿线允许扩展至3100年（而非2200年硬上限）
assert.strictEqual(clamped.frontierYear, 2850, '前沿线应被允许扩展至2850年！');
assert.strictEqual(clamped.worldChrono.frontierYear, 2950, '纪元前沿线应被允许扩展至2950年！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 数值白名单成功保护大额奖赏，前沿线成功支持至3100年且普通属性被限幅±30');

// ----------------------------------------------------
// 验证 3: 死配置激活与动态生效
// ----------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n--- 3. 验证 promotionBonus 与 endgameSGradeBaseScore 动态生效 ---');

// 3.1 验证 promotionBonus 注入
const engineState = global.state;
engineState.capital = 10000;
engineState.rep = 90;
engineState.govLevel = 2;

// 动态修改 promotionBonus 为 35000 测试生效
global.LIFESIM_DYNAMIC_CONFIG.rewards.promotionBonus = 35000;
global.triggerBreakthroughPromotion();
assert.strictEqual(engineState.capital, 10000 + 35000, 'triggerBreakthroughPromotion 必须读取并嘉奖动态 promotionBonus！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 破格提拔成功读取动态 promotionBonus: ¥35,000');

// 还原
global.LIFESIM_DYNAMIC_CONFIG.rewards.promotionBonus = 22000;

// ----------------------------------------------------
// 验证 4: 联姻防伪令牌强制核验
// ----------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n--- 4. 验证联姻防伪令牌强制核验与失败上报 ---');

// 4.1 不带 token 的庚帖必须被彻底拦截
const myTargetFamily = { familyId: 'fam_target_001', surname: '王', province: '山东', hallName: '三槐堂' };
const inviteWithoutToken = {
  fromFamId: 'fam_hacker_001',
  fromSurname: '赵',
  fromProvince: '河南',
  fromHall: '天水堂',
  ts: Date.now(),
  nonce: 'nonce_hack_01'
};
const resNoToken = clanSocial.validateMarriageInvite(inviteWithoutToken, myTargetFamily);
assert.strictEqual(lastReportedMilestone, 'marriage_verify_failed', '必须上报 marriage_verify_failed 埋点！');
assert(reportedMilestones.some(m => m.eventName === 'marriage_validation_failed'), '必须包含 marriage_validation_failed 埋点！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 无令牌联姻庚帖被拒绝并触发失败原因分布埋点');

// 4.2 篡改令牌的庚帖必须被拦截
const inviteWithBadToken = {
  ...inviteWithoutToken,
  token: 'invalid_sha_token'
};
const resBadToken = clanSocial.validateMarriageInvite(inviteWithBadToken, myTargetFamily);
assert.strictEqual(resBadToken.valid, false, '篡改令牌的联姻庚帖必须被拒！');
assert.strictEqual(resBadToken.reason, 'INVALID_SIGNATURE', '原因应为 INVALID_SIGNATURE');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 伪造令牌联姻庚帖被拒绝');

// 4.3 真实令牌正常放行
const testFamily = { familyId: 'fam_real_001', surname: '陈', province: '河南', hallName: '颍川堂', genealogyList: [1,2] };
const card = clanSocial.generateMarriageInvitationCard(testFamily);
const validInvite = {
  fromFamId: testFamily.familyId,
  fromSurname: testFamily.surname,
  fromProvince: testFamily.province,
  fromHall: testFamily.hallName,
  ts: card.inviterData.ts,
  nonce: card.inviterData.nonce,
  token: card.inviterData.token
};
const resValidToken = clanSocial.validateMarriageInvite(validInvite, myTargetFamily);
assert.strictEqual(resValidToken.valid, true, '真实合法生成的联姻庚帖必须通过！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 合法联姻庚帖验签成功');

// ----------------------------------------------------
// 验证 5: 最小赛季内容包 (MSCP) 规格与产线排期
// ----------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n--- 5. 验证最小赛季内容包 (MSCP) 规格与 7 周倒排排期 ---');

assert(seasonConfig.SEASON_CONTENT_SPEC, 'SEASON_CONTENT_SPEC 必须定义');
assert.strictEqual(seasonConfig.SEASON_CONTENT_SPEC.requirements.careerLadders.minTracks, 5, 'MSCP必须包含至少5条行当官制阶梯');
assert.strictEqual(seasonConfig.SEASON_CONTENT_SPEC.requirements.historicalFigures.minFigures, 6, 'MSCP必须包含至少6位名宿');
assert.strictEqual(seasonConfig.SEASON_CONTENT_SPEC.requirements.criticalChronoEvents.minEvents, 4, 'MSCP必须包含至少4个关隘事件');
assert.strictEqual(seasonConfig.SEASON_CONTENT_SPEC.requirements.relicsAndHeritage.minRelics, 2, 'MSCP必须包含至少2件传世物');

assert(Array.isArray(seasonConfig.SEASON_PRODUCTION_PIPELINE), '必须定义 7 周倒排产线排期');
assert.strictEqual(seasonConfig.SEASON_PRODUCTION_PIPELINE.length, 8, '倒排排期应包含 T-7 至 T-0 共 8 个里程碑');

// 校验内容包就绪状态

assert.strictEqual(seasonConfig.hasSeasonContentPack('season_2_liu_chao'), true, '第2赛季内容包必须就绪');
assert.strictEqual(seasonConfig.hasSeasonContentPack('season_3_sui_tang'), false, '第3赛季内容包目前未研发完成，必须为 false！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: MSCP 产线规范与倒排排期完整，第3赛季内容包就绪检查正确返回 false');

// ----------------------------------------------------

// ----------------------------------------------------

// 模拟家族处于第2赛季末（即将结算跨入第3赛季）
engineState.familyProfile = {

  surname: '崔',
  hallName: '博陵堂',
  fullName: '博陵崔氏',

// 打开赛季结算弹窗

assert(reportedMilestones.some(m => m.eventName === 'season_settlement_opened'), '打开结算弹窗应触发 season_settlement_opened 埋点！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 结算弹窗打开上报 season_settlement_previewed 埋点');

// 渲染弹窗
global.drawSeasonSettlementModal();

// 尝试在无内容包的情况下强行触发二次确认
engineState.seasonSettlementPendingConfirm = true;
// 再次渲染触发安全检查与清零
global.drawSeasonSettlementModal();
assert.strictEqual(engineState.seasonSettlementPendingConfirm, false, '未研发的赛季（第3季）必须自动重置二次确认状态，严禁进入终局启程！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 第3赛季未发布时二次确认被严格锁死并重置');

// 6.2 验证第1赛季结算确认弹窗取消上报
engineState.familyProfile.currentSeasonId = 'season_1_han_wei';
global.openSeasonSettlementModal('season_1_han_wei');
global.drawSeasonSettlementModal();
engineState.seasonSettlementPendingConfirm = true;
global.drawSeasonSettlementModal();
const cancelBtn = typeof gameEngine.getHitboxes === 'function' ? gameEngine.getHitboxes().find(b => b.id === 'season_settle_cancel_confirm_btn') : null;
if (cancelBtn && typeof cancelBtn.handler === 'function') {
  cancelBtn.handler();
}
assert(reportedMilestones.some(m => m.eventName === 'season_settlement_confirm_cancelled'), '取消二次确认必须触发 season_settlement_confirm_cancelled 埋点！');
console.log('✅ 测试通过: 结算确认弹窗取消上报 season_settlement_confirm_cancelled 埋点');

// ----------------------------------------------------
// 验证 7: 关键埋点链路触发检验
// ----------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n--- 7. 验证立族、云恢复、前沿线拦截埋点 ---');

// 7.1 立族展示埋点
engineState.familyProfile = null;

console.log('✅ 测试通过: 立族大典展示触发 clan_establishment_viewed 埋点');

// 7.2 前沿线拦截埋点
global.openFrontierWaitingModal();

process.exit(0);

// ==============================================================================
// 🧪 微信小游戏 (Canvas 2D) 华夏三千年世家大系交互与渲染自动化验证套件
// 验证对象：wechat-minigame/game.js 纯 Canvas 运行环境
// ==============================================================================

const assert = require('assert');

// 1. 模拟微信小游戏沙盒与 Canvas 2D 上下文
const canvasCalls = [];
const noop = () => {};
const mockCtx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => {
    if (k === 'measureText') return (text) => ({ width: (text ? text.length : 0) * 12 });
    if (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') return () => ({ addColorStop: noop });
    if (k === 'createPattern') return () => ({});
    if (k === 'fillText') return (text, x, y) => canvasCalls.push({ fn: 'fillText', text, x, y });
    if (k === 'fillRect') return (x, y, w, h) => canvasCalls.push({ fn: 'fillRect', x, y, w, h });
    if (k === 'strokeRect') return (x, y, w, h) => canvasCalls.push({ fn: 'strokeRect', x, y, w, h });
    if (k === 'arc') return (x, y, r) => canvasCalls.push({ fn: 'arc', x, y, r });
    if (t[k] !== undefined) return t[k];
    return noop;
  },
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});

// 内存版 wx 存储模拟
const mockStorage = {};
const canvasObj = {
  width: 750,
  height: 1334,
  getContext: () => mockCtx
};

global.wx = {
  getSystemInfoSync: () => ({
    screenWidth: 375,
    screenHeight: 667,
    pixelRatio: 2,
    windowWidth: 375,
    windowHeight: 667,
    statusBarHeight: 20
  }),
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 20, bottom: 647 } }),
  getStorageSync: (key) => mockStorage[key] || null,
  setStorageSync: (key, val) => { mockStorage[key] = val; },
  removeStorageSync: (key) => { delete mockStorage[key]; },
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 24, bottom: 56, left: 280, right: 365, width: 85, height: 32 }),
  createCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createOffscreenCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createWebAudioContext: () => null,
  onTouchStart: noop,
  onTouchMove: noop,
  onTouchEnd: noop,
  onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop,
  onHide: noop,
  onError: noop,
  onWindowResize: noop,
  onAudioInterruptionBegin: noop,
  onAudioInterruptionEnd: noop,
  shareAppMessage: noop,
  vibrateShort: noop,
  vibrateLong: noop,
  reportEvent: noop,
  showShareMenu: noop,
  onShareAppMessage: noop,
  onShareTimeline: noop,
  getLaunchOptionsSync: () => ({ query: {} }),
  getUpdateManager: () => ({ onCheckForUpdate: noop, onUpdateReady: noop, onUpdateFailed: noop }),
  createInnerAudioContext: () => ({
    play: noop,
    pause: noop,
    stop: noop,
    onCanplay: noop,
    onEnded: noop,
    onError: noop
  })
};

global.requestAnimationFrame = noop;
global.__DEV__ = true;

// 2. 加载小游戏引擎
console.log("================================================================================");
console.log("      微信小游戏 Canvas 2D 华夏三千年世家大系专项自动化实测");
console.log("================================================================================");

const engine = require('./wechat-minigame/game.js');
const state = global.state;
assert(state, "引擎 state 上下文加载失败");

// 确保初始世家缓存为空，测试从零立族全流程
mockStorage['lifesim_clan_profile_v1'] = null;
state.familyProfile = null;

// ==============================================================================
// 1. 验证封面标题界面：立族大典入口按钮渲染
// ==============================================================================
console.log("\n>>> [测试 1：封面标题界面 · 华夏世家立族大典按钮]");
state.gameScene = 'title';
canvasCalls.length = 0;

// 执行标题屏绘制
if (typeof global.openClanEstablishmentModal === 'function') {
  global.openClanEstablishmentModal();
  assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, 'clan_establishment', "点击立族大典应弹出 clan_establishment 模态弹窗");
  console.log("  ✅ [PASS] 成功触发立族大典弹窗，当前 activeModal: " + state.activeModal);
}

// ==============================================================================
// 2. 验证立族大典 Modal (Canvas 2D 渲染与提交)
// ==============================================================================
console.log("\n>>> [测试 2：立族大典 Canvas 2D 绘制与宗族确立]");
canvasCalls.length = 0;
global.drawClanEstablishmentModal();
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('华夏世家 · 立族大典')), "Canvas 应绘制立族大典主标题");
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('家族姓氏')), "Canvas 应绘制姓氏选择区");
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('祖籍省份')), "Canvas 应绘制祖籍省份区");
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('传世堂号')), "Canvas 应绘制堂号推荐区");
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('家业底色')), "Canvas 应绘制家业底色区");
console.log("  ✅ [PASS] 立族大典 Canvas 2D 各核心功能区渲染完整，Canvas API 调用正常 (" + canvasCalls.length + " calls)");

// 模拟选择：河南 · 陈氏 · 颍川堂 · 耕读传家
state.clanForm = {
  surnameIndex: 4, // '陈'
  provinceIndex: 0, // '河南'
  vocationId: 'study_scholar',
  hallIndex: 0 // '颍川堂'
};

// 触发立族大典提交
const famMod = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/familySystem.js');
const ep1Mod = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/epochOneEngine.js');
const ancMod = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/ancientChronicleMaster.js');

const profile = famMod.createFamilyProfile({
  surname: '陈',
  province: '河南',
  hallName: '颍川堂',
  vocationId: 'study_scholar'
});

const dec1 = [];
ep1Mod.EPOCH_ONE_HISTORICAL_EVENTS.forEach((evt, idx) => {
  dec1.push(ep1Mod.runEpochOneEvent(idx, 0, {}));
});
const rec1 = ep1Mod.settleEpochOneAncestor(profile, { name: '崇德', ziHao: '子修' }, dec1);
famMod.recordAncestorToGenealogy(profile, rec1);

const names = ['彦先', '承业', '希贤', '廷辅', '守愚', '济苍', '新民'];
ancMod.EPOCHS_TWO_TO_EIGHT_DATABASE.forEach((ep, eIdx) => {
  const epNum = ep.epochIndex;
  const decs = [];
  ep.historicalEvents.forEach((evt, evtIdx) => {
    decs.push(ancMod.runSpecificEpochEvent(epNum, evtIdx, 0, {}));
  });
  const rec = ancMod.settleEpochAncestorRecord(profile, epNum, { name: names[eIdx] || `祖${epNum}` }, decs);
  famMod.recordAncestorToGenealogy(profile, rec);
});

state.familyProfile = profile;
global.saveClanProfile(profile);
state.activeModal = null;

assert(state.familyProfile, "立族后 state.familyProfile 不能为空");
assert.strictEqual(state.familyProfile.fullName, "河南·陈氏·颍川堂", "家族全称符合合规规范");
assert.strictEqual(state.familyProfile.isImmutable, true, "家族必须不可更改 (isImmutable)");
assert(state.familyProfile.genealogyList.length >= 8, "已载入汉末奠基至当代8代先贤长卷");
console.log("  ✅ [PASS] 成功确立传世世家：【" + state.familyProfile.fullName + "】，族谱传承 " + state.familyProfile.genealogyList.length + " 世");

// ==============================================================================

assert.strictEqual(loadedProfile.surname, '陈', "还原姓氏匹配");
assert.strictEqual(loadedProfile.hallName, '颍川堂', "还原堂号匹配");

global.startCharacterCreation();

/**
 * test_p1_features.js - Comprehensive Unit & Integration Verification Suite for P1 Features
 * Tests:
 * 1. Era Rivals System (时代宿敌动态对决系统)
 * 2. Economic Hardcore Realism (时代通胀与刚性支出曲线)
 * 3. Late-Game Capital Sinks & Multi-Generational Heritage (40岁三大资金蓄水池与跨代继承)
 */

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const assert = require('assert');

// Mock browser / wechat minigame environment
const noop = () => {};
const ctx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => k === 'measureText' ? () => ({ width: 10 })
    : (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') ? () => ({ addColorStop: noop })
    : k === 'createPattern' ? () => ({})
    : (t[k] !== undefined ? t[k] : noop),
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});
const canvas = { width: 750, height: 1624, getContext: () => ctx };
let storageStore = {};
global.wx = {
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 812, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 44, bottom: 778 } }),
  createCanvas: () => canvas,
  createOffscreenCanvas: () => canvas,
  createWebAudioContext: () => null,
  getStorageSync: k => storageStore[k],
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => { storageStore[k] = v; },
  removeStorageSync: k => { delete storageStore[k]; },
  vibrateShort: noop, vibrateLong: noop, onTouchStart: noop, onTouchMove: noop, onTouchEnd: noop, onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop, onHide: noop, onWindowResize: noop, onAudioInterruptionBegin: noop, onAudioInterruptionEnd: noop,
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 48, right: 365, bottom: 80, left: 278, width: 87, height: 32 }),
  getSystemInfo: noop, getAccountInfoSync: () => ({ miniProgram: { envVersion: 'develop' } }),
  reportEvent: noop, showShareMenu: noop, onShareAppMessage: noop, onShareTimeline: noop,
  getLaunchOptionsSync: () => ({ query: {} }),
  getUpdateManager: () => ({ onCheckForUpdate: noop, onUpdateReady: noop, onUpdateFailed: noop })
};
global.requestAnimationFrame = noop;
global.canvas = canvas;
global.Image = function () {};
global.document = undefined;

// Load game.js
const gamePath = path.resolve(__dirname, 'wechat-minigame/game.js');
console.log('Loading game engine from:', gamePath);
const engine = require(gamePath);
const state = global.state;

let passedTests = 0;
let totalTests = 0;

function runTest(name, fn) {
  totalTests++;
  try {
    fn();
    console.log(`  ✅ [PASS] ${name}`);
    passedTests++;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`  ❌ [FAIL] ${name}`);
    console.error(err);
    process.exitCode = 1;
  }
}

console.log('\n======================================================');
console.log('  TEST SUITE 1: 时代宿敌动态对决系统 (Era Rivals System)');
console.log('======================================================');

runTest('1.1 ERA_RIVALS_DATABASE schema and phase coverage', () => {
  assert(engine.ERA_RIVALS_DATABASE, 'ERA_RIVALS_DATABASE must exist');
  assert.strictEqual(engine.ERA_RIVALS_DATABASE.length, 4, 'Must have 4 era rivals covering life phases');
  
  const expectedRivals = [
    { id: 'rival_zhao', name: '赵天霸', age: 25 },
    { id: 'rival_cui', name: '崔铁面', age: 33 },
    { id: 'rival_gao', name: '高博', age: 41 },
    { id: 'rival_qian', name: '钱万贯', age: 49 }
  ];

  expectedRivals.forEach((exp, idx) => {
    const rival = engine.ERA_RIVALS_DATABASE[idx];
    assert.strictEqual(rival.id, exp.id);
    assert.strictEqual(rival.name, exp.name);
    assert.strictEqual(rival.age, exp.age);
    assert(Array.isArray(rival.options) && rival.options.length >= 2, `${rival.name} must have at least 2 duel choices`);
  });
});

runTest('1.2 getCurrentEraRival age routing', () => {
  assert.strictEqual(engine.getCurrentEraRival(18), null, 'Age 18 should have no rival');
  assert.strictEqual(engine.getCurrentEraRival(25).name, '赵天霸', 'Age 25 should face 赵天霸');
  assert.strictEqual(engine.getCurrentEraRival(33).name, '崔铁面', 'Age 33 should face 崔铁面');
  assert.strictEqual(engine.getCurrentEraRival(41).name, '高博', 'Age 41 should face 高博');
  assert.strictEqual(engine.getCurrentEraRival(49).name, '钱万贯', 'Age 49 should face 钱万贯');
  assert.strictEqual(engine.getCurrentEraRival(55), null, 'Age 55 should have no rival');
});

runTest('1.3 Momentum state management & clamping', () => {
  state.rivalMomentum = undefined;
  state.playerMomentum = undefined;
  engine.ensureRivalMomentumState();
  assert.strictEqual(state.playerMomentum, 50, 'Default player momentum is 50');
  assert.strictEqual(state.rivalMomentum, 50, 'Default rival momentum is 50');

  // Verify bounded clamping [0, 100]
  state.playerMomentum = 120;
  state.rivalMomentum = -30;
  engine.ensureRivalMomentumState();
  assert.strictEqual(state.playerMomentum, 100, 'Player momentum must clamp to 100 max');
  assert.strictEqual(state.rivalMomentum, 0, 'Rival momentum must clamp to 0 min');
});

runTest('1.4 Rival showdown modal opening and execution', () => {
  state.age = 25;
  const rival = engine.getCurrentEraRival(25);
  engine.ensureRivalMomentumState();
  state.activeModal = null;
  
  const opened = engine.openRivalShowdown(rival);
  assert.strictEqual(opened, true, 'openRivalShowdown should return true on success');
  assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, 'rival_showdown', 'Active modal should be rival_showdown');
  assert(state.activeRival, 'state.activeRival must be set');
  assert.strictEqual(state.activeRival.name, '赵天霸');

  // Test choice execution (e.g. choice 0)
  const result = engine.executeRivalShowdownChoice(0);
  assert(result, 'executeRivalShowdownChoice should return resolution result');
  assert(typeof result.success === 'boolean', 'Resolution must report success boolean');
  assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, null, 'Modal should close after execution');
});

runTest('1.5 Policy auto-resolution for rival showdown', () => {
  state.age = 33;
  const rival = engine.getCurrentEraRival(33);
  engine.openRivalShowdown(rival);
  assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, 'rival_showdown');
  
  // Resolve via strategic policy
  const resolved = engine.resolveCurrentModalByPolicy('strategic');
  assert(resolved, 'resolveCurrentModalByPolicy should resolve rival_showdown');
  assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, null, 'Modal should be cleared');
});

runTest('1.6 Dynamic momentum triggers (S-Appraisal & Pact completion)', () => {
  engine.ensureRivalMomentumState();
  state.playerMomentum = 50;
  state.rivalMomentum = 50;

  // Simulate S-Appraisal impact
  engine.evaluateYearlyAppraisal();
  // Player momentum and rival momentum should exist and be clamped between 0 and 100
  assert(state.playerMomentum >= 0 && state.playerMomentum <= 100);
  assert(state.rivalMomentum >= 0 && state.rivalMomentum <= 100);
});

console.log('  TEST SUITE 2: 经济硬核拟真与时代通胀 (Economic Realism)');
console.log('======================================================');

runTest('2.1 getEraInflationScale historical curve accuracy', () => {
  const scale1970 = engine.getEraInflationScale(1970);
  const scale1985 = engine.getEraInflationScale(1985);
  const scale1992 = engine.getEraInflationScale(1992);
  const scale2000 = engine.getEraInflationScale(2000);
  const scale2010 = engine.getEraInflationScale(2010);
  const scale2025 = engine.getEraInflationScale(2025);

  assert.strictEqual(scale1970, 1.0, '1970 base scale must be 1.0 (planned economy baseline)');
  assert.strictEqual(scale1985, 1.0, '1985 base scale must be 1.0 (pre-1992 price reform)');
  assert.strictEqual(scale1992, 1.0, '1992 base scale must be 1.0');
  assert(Math.abs(scale2000 - 1.64) < 0.01, `2000 scale (${scale2000}) should be 1.64`);
  assert(Math.abs(scale2010 - 2.44) < 0.01, `2010 scale (${scale2010}) should be 2.44`);
  assert(Math.abs(scale2025 - 3.64) < 0.01, `2025 scale (${scale2025}) should be 3.64`);

  // Verify monotonicity
  assert(scale1992 <= scale2000 && scale2000 < scale2010 && scale2010 < scale2025, 'Inflation curve must be monotonically increasing post-1992');
});

runTest('2.2 Medical emergency costs scaled with inflation', () => {
  state.year = 1975;
  state.capital = 100000;
  state.health = 50;

  const capBefore1975 = state.capital;
  engine.executeHealthChoice();
  const scale1975 = engine.getEraInflationScale(1975); // 1.0
  const expectedCost1975 = Math.round(15000 * scale1975);
  assert.strictEqual(capBefore1975 - state.capital, expectedCost1975, 'Cost at 1975 should be 15,000 * 1.0');

  // Test at year 2025
  state.year = 2025;
  state.capital = 200000;
  const capBefore2025 = state.capital;
  engine.executeHealthChoice();
  const scale2025 = engine.getEraInflationScale(2025); // 3.64
  const expectedCost2025 = Math.round(15000 * scale2025);
  assert.strictEqual(capBefore2025 - state.capital, expectedCost2025, `Cost at 2025 should be scaled by ${scale2025}x`);
});

runTest('2.3 Annual living costs scale with inflation in progression', () => {
  // Verify settleAnnualCoreProgression respects era inflation
  assert(typeof engine.tickOneYear === 'function', 'tickOneYear must exist');
});

console.log('  TEST SUITE 3: 40岁后期战略资金蓄水池与跨代继承 (Capital Sinks)');

runTest('3.1 Hometown university donation & dynamic IQ cap bonus', () => {
  state.age = 45;
  state.capital = 2000000;
  state.clanIqCapBonus = 0;
  state.iq = 90;
  engine.bindReactiveStatClamping();

  const success = engine.donateToHometownUniversity(500000);
  assert.strictEqual(success, true, 'Donation should succeed when funds are sufficient');
  assert.strictEqual(state.clanIqCapBonus, 3, 'First donation level should grant +3 clan IQ cap bonus');
  assert(state.epitaphTeacherOfAges, 'Epitaph千秋师表 flag should be set');
  assert(state.tags['epitaph_teacher_of_ages'], 'Tag epitaph_teacher_of_ages should be set');

  // Verify that IQ can now be set and clamped up to 103
  engine.setStat('iq', 105);
  assert.strictEqual(engine.getStat('iq'), 103, 'IQ should clamp to 100 + 3 = 103');

  // Test insufficient funds failure
  state.capital = 100000;
  const failRes = engine.donateToHometownUniversity(500000);
  assert.strictEqual(failRes, false, 'Donation must fail if capital is insufficient');
});

runTest('3.2 Family perpetual trust & 5% annual dividend cashflow', () => {
  state.capital = 1000000;
  state.familyPerpetualTrust = 0;

  const success = engine.injectFamilyPerpetualTrust(500000);
  assert.strictEqual(success, true, 'Trust injection should succeed');
  assert.strictEqual(state.familyPerpetualTrust, 500000);
  assert.strictEqual(state.capital, 500000);

  // Annual progression dividend check: 500,000 * 5% = 25,000
  const dividend = Math.round(state.familyPerpetualTrust * 0.05);
  assert.strictEqual(dividend, 25000, '5% trust dividend should be 25,000 for 500,000 trust principal');

  // Test insufficient funds failure
  state.capital = 50000;
  const failRes = engine.injectFamilyPerpetualTrust(200000);
  assert.strictEqual(failRes, false, 'Trust injection must fail if capital < amount');
});

runTest('3.3 National mega-project consortium sponsorship', () => {
  state.capital = 2000000;
  state.heritageBonusTalents = 0;
  state.talentPoints = 0;
  const repBefore = engine.getStat('rep');

  // Tier 1: 500,000
  const res1 = engine.sponsorNationalMegaprojectConsortia(500000);
  assert.strictEqual(res1, true, 'Tier 1 sponsorship should succeed');
  assert.strictEqual(state.capital, 1500000);
  assert(state.tags['megaproject_anchor_of_state'], 'Should receive anchor tag');
  assert.strictEqual(state.heritageBonusTalents, 1, 'Tier 1 grants 1 heritage talent');
  assert.strictEqual(state.talentPoints, 2, 'Tier 1 grants 2 talent points');

  // Tier 2: 1,000,000
  const res2 = engine.sponsorNationalMegaprojectConsortia(1000000);
  assert.strictEqual(res2, true, 'Tier 2 sponsorship should succeed');
  assert.strictEqual(state.capital, 500000);
  assert(state.tags['megaproject_pillar_of_state'], 'Should receive pillar tag');
  assert.strictEqual(state.heritageBonusTalents, 4, 'Tier 2 adds 3 heritage talents (total 4)');
  assert.strictEqual(state.talentPoints, 7, 'Tier 2 adds 5 talent points (total 7)');
});

runTest('3.4 Modal open and policy resolution for late_game_money_sinks', () => {
  state.age = 38;
  const openedUnder40 = engine.openLateGameMoneySinksModal();
  assert.strictEqual(openedUnder40, false, 'Late game money sinks cannot open under age 40');

  state.age = 42;
  const openedAt42 = engine.openLateGameMoneySinksModal();
  assert.strictEqual(openedAt42, true, 'Late game money sinks opens at age 42');
  assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, 'late_game_money_sinks');

  const resolved = engine.resolveCurrentModalByPolicy('strategic');
  assert(resolved, 'resolveCurrentModalByPolicy resolves late_game_money_sinks');
  assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, null, 'Modal should close after resolution');
});

runTest('3.5 Multi-generational inheritance (85% trust + clan IQ bonus)', () => {
  state.familyPerpetualTrust = 1000000;
  state.clanIqCapBonus = 6;
  state.family = state.family || {};
  state.family.children = [{ name: '林立新', gender: 'male', age: 20 }];
  state.capital = 400000;
  
  engine.successionToNextGeneration();
  assert(state.dynasticInheritance, 'state.dynasticInheritance must be created');
  assert.strictEqual(state.dynasticInheritance.clanIqCapBonus, 6, 'clanIqCapBonus must persist in inheritance');
  assert.strictEqual(state.dynasticInheritance.familyPerpetualTrust, 850000, '85% of family perpetual trust (850,000) must be recorded');

  // Complete character creation for new generation
  engine.finishCharacterCreation();
  assert.strictEqual(state.clanIqCapBonus, 6, 'clanIqCapBonus restored in new generation');
  assert.strictEqual(state.familyPerpetualTrust, 850000, 'familyPerpetualTrust restored in new generation');
  
  // Verify IQ ceiling can reach 106 in new generation
  engine.setStat('iq', 106);
  assert.strictEqual(engine.getStat('iq'), 106, 'New generation heir can reach IQ 106');
});

console.log('\n======================================================');
console.log(`  SUMMARY: ${passedTests}/${totalTests} Tests Passed`);
console.log('======================================================\n');

if (passedTests === totalTests) {
  process.exit(0);
} else {
  process.exit(1);

/**
 * 华夏三千年世家 · 单局人生与宗祠大系双向闭环端到端验证脚本
 * 验证目标：
 * 1. 单局人生创角承袭世家姓氏、世系代数与祖荫信托启蒙金
 * 2. 单局人生结卷 (triggerEndgameModal) 真正实现墨印归宗，写入 genealogyList
 * 3. 产生动态考定谥号 (文肃公/通惠公/格致公等)、族产沉淀 (30%公积)、传世家训与宗族声望增益
 * 4. 宗族接棒 (successionToNextGeneration) 自动繁衍第 N+1 世子孙，承继祖荫信托金
 * 5. 跨两代连续生命周期模拟验证：两代先贤均在宗祠长卷中肃穆陈列，当代掌门接续家风

const noop = () => {};
const mockCtx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => {
    if (k === 'measureText') return (text) => ({ width: (text ? text.length : 0) * 12 });
    if (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') return () => ({ addColorStop: noop });
    if (k === 'createPattern') return () => ({});
    if (t[k] !== undefined) return t[k];
    return noop;
  },
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});

const canvasObj = {
  width: 750,
  height: 1334,
  getContext: () => mockCtx
};

global._storage = {};
global.wx = {
  getStorageSync: (k) => global._storage[k] || null,
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => { global._storage[k] = v; },
  removeStorageSync: (k) => { delete global._storage[k]; },
  getSystemInfoSync: () => ({ screenWidth: 375, screenHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667 }),
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 20, bottom: 647 } }),
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 24, bottom: 56, left: 280, right: 365, width: 85, height: 32 }),
  onTouchStart: noop,
  onTouchMove: noop,
  onTouchEnd: noop,
  onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop,
  onHide: noop,
  onError: noop,
  onWindowResize: noop,
  triggerGC: noop,
  createCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createOffscreenCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createWebAudioContext: () => null
};

console.log('================================================================================');

// 加载游戏主模块

// 步骤 1：确立华夏世家（河南·陈氏·颍川堂），完成汉末筑基第一代
console.log('>>> [步骤一：确立世家档案并建立初始宗祠]');
const initialProfile = familySys.createFamilyProfile('陈', '河南', '颍川堂', 'study_scholar');
initialProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney = 50000;
initialProfile.clanTrust.prestigeScore = 100;

assert.strictEqual(state.familyProfile.fullName, '河南·陈氏·颍川堂');
assert.strictEqual(state.familyProfile.surname, '陈');
console.log(`  ✅ 成功建立世家：【${state.familyProfile.fullName}】，初始族产信托：¥${state.familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney}，声望：${state.familyProfile.clanTrust.prestigeScore}分`);

// 步骤 2：第一世子孙入世创角
console.log('\n>>> [步骤二：第一局游戏创角 · 承袭世家姓氏与祖荫信托启蒙金]');
global.startCharacterCreation(false);

assert.strictEqual(state.birthProfile.surname, '陈', '身世姓氏必须锁定世家姓氏【陈】');
assert.strictEqual(state.dynasticGeneration, 1, '第一局人生为世家第 1 世');
assert.strictEqual(state.clanStarterGrant, 2500, '必须注入 5% 祖荫信托启蒙金 (2500)');
assert(state.capital >= 2500, '创角资财必须包含祖荫信托启蒙金');
console.log(`  ✅ 角色姓氏：${state.birthProfile.surname}，世系代数：第 ${state.dynasticGeneration} 世`);
console.log(`  ✅ 领受祖荫信托启蒙金 ¥${state.clanStarterGrant}，初始开局资金：¥${state.capital.toLocaleString()}`);

// 步骤 3：第一局人生履历推进与经世建功
console.log('\n>>> [步骤三：第一局人生履历推进与经世建功]');
state.name = '陈思敬';
state.age = 78;
state.year = 2024;
state.track = 'gov';

const expectedBequest = Math.floor(650000 * 0.3);
const expectedTrust = 50000 + expectedBequest;
assert.strictEqual(state.familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney, expectedTrust, '族产信托池必须如实沉淀 30% 遗产');
assert(state.familyProfile.clanTrust.prestigeScore > 100, '宗族声望必须获得先祖功绩累加');
console.log(`  ✅ 第一局结卷墨印归宗成功！`);

console.log(`    • 考定宗祠谥号：【${state.lastInscribedAncestor.posthumousTitle}】（享寿 ${state.age} 岁，${state.lastInscribedAncestor.officialTitle}）`);
console.log(`    • 族产公积沉淀：+¥${expectedBequest.toLocaleString()} 元 (宗族公积总池达 ¥${state.familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney.toLocaleString()})`);
console.log(`    • 宗族威望增进：现宗族总声望 ${state.familyProfile.clanTrust.prestigeScore} 分`);
console.log(`    • 传家家规墨印：“${state.lastInscribedAncestor.newMotto.text}”`);

// 步骤 4：宗族接棒 · 繁衍第二世子孙
console.log('\n>>> [步骤四：宗族接棒 · 续写家风至第 2 世]');
global.successionToNextGeneration();

assert.strictEqual(state.dynasticGeneration, 2, '第二局继承后代数必须自动跃迁为第 2 世');
assert.strictEqual(state.creation.surname, '陈', '第二世子孙姓氏必须为【陈】');
assert(state.dynasticInheritance.clanTrustGrant > 10000, '第二代必须从扩张后的宗族信托公积池中领受丰厚启蒙金');
console.log(`  ✅ 成功接棒开辟新世！`);
console.log(`    • 接棒宗主代数：第 ${state.dynasticGeneration} 世`);
console.log(`    • 继承宗族姓氏：【${state.creation.surname}】`);
console.log(`    • 承领祖荫公积启蒙金：¥${state.dynasticInheritance.clanTrustGrant.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`    • 承继总启动资产：¥${state.dynasticInheritance.capital.toLocaleString()}`);

// 步骤 5：第二世子孙经商兴家并在终局再次归宗
console.log('\n>>> [步骤五：第二世人生经商拓业 · 再次结卷入谱]');
state.name = '陈通海';
state.age = 82;

/**
 * 华夏世家大系 · 赛季结算确认安全机制、44px触控优化、六朝烟雨专属内容、弹窗自适应与词林指津自动化验证
 */
const assert = require('assert');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// 注入微信小游戏环境与 Canvas mock
const noop = () => {};
const canvasCalls = [];
const mockCtx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => {
    if (k === 'measureText') return (text) => ({ width: (text ? text.length : 0) * 12 });
    if (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') return () => ({ addColorStop: noop });
    if (k === 'createPattern') return () => ({});
    if (k === 'roundRect' || k === 'fillRect' || k === 'strokeRect' || k === 'fillText') {
      return (...args) => {
        canvasCalls.push({ fn: k, text: args[0], args });
      };
    }
    if (t[k] !== undefined) return t[k];
    return noop;
  },
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});

const mockCanvas = {
  getContext: () => mockCtx,
  width: 750,
  height: 1334
};

global._storage = {};
global.wx = {
  getSystemInfoSync: () => ({
    windowWidth: 375,
    windowHeight: 667,
    screenWidth: 375,
    screenHeight: 667,
    pixelRatio: 2
  }),
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 20, bottom: 647 } }),
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 24, bottom: 56, left: 280, right: 365, width: 85, height: 32 }),
  createCanvas: () => mockCanvas,
  createOffscreenCanvas: () => mockCanvas,
  createWebAudioContext: () => null,
  getStorageSync: (k) => global._storage[k] || null,
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => { global._storage[k] = v; },
  removeStorageSync: (k) => { delete global._storage[k]; },
  vibrateShort: noop,
  vibrateLong: noop,
  onTouchStart: noop,
  onTouchMove: noop,
  onTouchEnd: noop,
  onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop,
  onHide: noop,
  onError: noop,
  onWindowResize: noop
};

// 加载游戏引擎
const game = require('./wechat-minigame/game.js');
const { state } = game;

console.log('================================================================================');
console.log('   华夏世家大系 · 赛季结算二次确认、44px触控、六朝内容与词林指津专项验证');
console.log('================================================================================\n');

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 1：赛季大结卷安全防误触（打开仅预览，杜绝直接扣费结卷）
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('>>> [测试 1：赛季大结卷安全机制：打开仅预览，二次确认后方正式执行]');
const testClan = {
  fullName: '河南·陈氏·颍川堂',
  surname: '陈',
  province: '河南',
  hallName: '颍川堂',
  currentSeasonId: 'season_1_han_wei',
  clanTrust: {
    prestigeScore: 2800,
    trustFundMoney: 1000000,
    relics: [{ id: 'relic_ancient_book_scroll', name: '太行古卷' }]
  },
  genealogyList: [{}, {}, {}],
  timeline: { currentYear: 300, currentEpochIndex: 1 }
};
state.familyProfile = testClan;

// 1.1 打开结算弹窗
global.openSeasonSettlementModal('season_1_han_wei');
assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, 'season_settlement', '应激活 season_settlement 弹窗');
assert.strictEqual(state.seasonSettlementPendingConfirm, false, '打开时二次确认应为 false');
assert.strictEqual(testClan.clanTrust.trustFundMoney, 1000000, '打开弹窗绝对不能直接改动族产信托！');
assert.strictEqual(testClan.currentSeasonId, 'season_1_han_wei', '打开弹窗绝对不能提前切换赛季！');
console.log('  ✅ 成功验证：打开赛季弹窗仅生成预览，未发生任何族产改动或不可逆赛季切换');

// 1.2 绘制弹窗并检查 44px 按钮
canvasCalls.length = 0;
global.drawSeasonSettlementModal();
const hitboxes = game.getHitboxes();
const advanceBtn = hitboxes.find(b => b.id === 'season_settlement_advance_btn');
assert.ok(advanceBtn, '应存在主行动 advanceBtn');
const advH = advanceBtn.height || advanceBtn.h;
assert.ok(advH >= 44, `主行动按钮高度须 >= 44px (实际: ${advH}px)`);
console.log(`  ✅ 成功验证：启程按钮触控高度为 ${advH}px，符合 >= 44px 移动端规范`);

// 1.3 点击主行动按钮，验证触发二次终局确认弹窗（而非直接执行扣费）
advanceBtn.handler();
assert.strictEqual(state.seasonSettlementPendingConfirm, true, '点击启程后应进入二次确认状态');
assert.strictEqual(testClan.currentSeasonId, 'season_1_han_wei', '未二次确认前赛季仍未改变');
assert.strictEqual(testClan.clanTrust.trustFundMoney, 1000000, '未二次确认前族产信托仍未改动');

// 1.4 绘制二次确认遮罩与对话框
canvasCalls.length = 0;
global.drawSeasonSettlementModal();
const confirmExecuteBtn = game.getHitboxes().find(b => b.id === 'season_settle_execute_confirm_btn');
const cancelConfirmBtn = game.getHitboxes().find(b => b.id === 'season_settle_cancel_confirm_btn');
assert.ok(confirmExecuteBtn, '应存在二次确认按钮');
assert.ok(cancelConfirmBtn, '应存在返回再看按钮');
const cfExecH = confirmExecuteBtn.height || confirmExecuteBtn.h;
const cfCancelH = cancelConfirmBtn.height || cancelConfirmBtn.h;
assert.ok(cfExecH >= 44, `二次确认按钮高度须 >= 44px (实际: ${cfExecH}px)`);
assert.ok(cfCancelH >= 44, `二次取消按钮高度须 >= 44px (实际: ${cfCancelH}px)`);
console.log('  ✅ 成功验证：二次确认遮罩弹窗已呈现，包含两大 >=44px 按钮');

// 1.5 模拟点击取消，验证安全回退
cancelConfirmBtn.handler();
assert.strictEqual(state.seasonSettlementPendingConfirm, false, '点击取消后应安全回退二次确认');
assert.strictEqual(testClan.currentSeasonId, 'season_1_han_wei', '取消后赛季依然不变');
console.log('  ✅ 成功验证：点击返回可安全撤销二次确认，操作完全可控');

// 1.6 再次点击启程并执行终局确认
advanceBtn.handler();
assert.strictEqual(state.seasonSettlementPendingConfirm, true);
global.drawSeasonSettlementModal();
const execBtn = game.getHitboxes().find(b => b.id === 'season_settle_execute_confirm_btn');
execBtn.handler();
assert.strictEqual(testClan.currentSeasonId, 'season_2_liu_chao', '终局确认后赛季正式切换为 season_2_liu_chao');
assert.strictEqual(state.selectedBirthYear, 300, '新角色出生年份应设定为 300 年');
console.log('  ✅ 成功验证：唯有经过终局确认后，方正式完成大结卷并跨入第二赛季【六朝烟雨】！');

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 2：第二赛季【六朝烟雨】弱冠及冠专属内容与决断
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n>>> [测试 2：第二赛季【六朝烟雨】公元300~500年专属内容]');
state.year = 318;
state.age = 18;
state.track = 'official';

// 触发及冠告示
game.drawGaokaoNoticeModal();
const noticeAcceptBtn = game.getHitboxes().find(b => b.id === 'gaokao_notice_accept_btn');
assert.ok(noticeAcceptBtn, '应存在弱冠告示领受按钮');
noticeAcceptBtn.handler();

const decEvt = state.activeDecisionEvent;
assert.ok(decEvt, '应存在 activeDecisionEvent');

assert.strictEqual(decEvt.choices.length, 3, '六朝弱冠决断应提供玄学/庄园/北府兵三大专属选项');
const choiceTags = decEvt.choices.map(c => c.tag);

// 绘制词林指津弹窗，核验 7 大核心术语

assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('纪元 (Epoch)')), '必须解释 纪元');

assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('前沿 (Frontier)')), '必须解释 前沿');
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('世 (Generation)')), '必须解释 世');
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('信托 (Clan Trust)')), '必须解释 信托');
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('阀阅 (Noble Tier)')), '必须解释 阀阅');
assert(canvasCalls.some(c => c.fn === 'fillText' && c.text && c.text.includes('世交 (Alliance)')), '必须解释 世交');
console.log('  ✅ 成功验证：词林指津 Modal 完整阐释 纪元、赛季、前沿、世、信托、阀阅、世交 7 大概念！');

global.openDynasticChronicleModal(1);
assert.strictEqual(state.activeModal, 'dynastic_chronicle');
canvasCalls.length = 0;
global.drawDynasticChronicleModal();

assert.ok(psH >= 32, `暂歇推演按钮高度须 >= 32px (实际: ${psH}px)`);
console.log(`  ✅ 成功验证：推演弹窗自适应当前视口，暂歇按钮高度为 ${psH}px`);

assert.ok(Array.isArray(gameJson.subpackages), 'game.json 必须包含 subpackages 数组');
assert.ok(gameJson.subpackages.length >= 2, '至少配置了 futureEpoch 与 deepSpaceLore 两大分包');

assert.ok(pkgNames.includes('futureEpoch'), '配置了 futureEpoch 分包');
assert.ok(pkgNames.includes('deepSpaceLore'), '配置了 deepSpaceLore 分包');

gameJson.subpackages.forEach(sub => {
  const entryPath = path.join(__dirname, 'wechat-minigame', sub.root, 'game.js');

process.exit(0);

const noop = () => {};
const mockCanvasCalls = [];
const mockCtx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => {
    if (k === 'measureText') return (text) => ({ width: (text ? text.length : 0) * 12 });
    if (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') return () => ({ addColorStop: noop });
    if (k === 'createPattern') return () => ({});
    if (k === 'roundRect' || k === 'fillRect' || k === 'strokeRect' || k === 'fillText') {
      return (...args) => {
        mockCanvasCalls.push({ method: k, args });
      };
    }
    if (t[k] !== undefined) return t[k];
    return noop;
  },
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});

const canvasObj = {
  width: 750,
  height: 1334,
  getContext: () => mockCtx
};

global._storage = {};
global.wx = {
  getStorageSync: (k) => global._storage[k] || null,
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => { global._storage[k] = v; },
  removeStorageSync: (k) => { delete global._storage[k]; },
  getSystemInfoSync: () => ({ screenWidth: 375, screenHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667 }),
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 20, bottom: 647 } }),
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 24, bottom: 56, left: 280, right: 365, width: 85, height: 32 }),
  onTouchStart: noop,
  onTouchMove: noop,
  onTouchEnd: noop,
  onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop,
  onHide: noop,
  onError: noop,
  onWindowResize: noop,
  triggerGC: noop,
  createCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createOffscreenCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createWebAudioContext: () => null
};

/**

 * - A2 / C2: 早逝提前交接，窗口连续不丢事件
 * - C3: 五种接班模式起点参数与加成代价核验
 * - A4 / T1: 存档 v5 隔离与旧档归档防护

 * - A6 / C4: 周前沿 frontierYear 拦截校验
 */

const assert = require('assert');

// 1. 模拟微信小游戏环境与 Canvas 上下文
const noop = () => {};
const mockCanvasCalls = [];
const mockCtx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => {
    if (k === 'measureText') return (text) => ({ width: (text ? text.length : 0) * 12 });
    if (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') return () => ({ addColorStop: noop });
    if (k === 'createPattern') return () => ({});
    if (k === 'roundRect' || k === 'fillRect' || k === 'strokeRect' || k === 'fillText') {
      return (...args) => {
        mockCanvasCalls.push({ method: k, args });
      };
    }
    if (t[k] !== undefined) return t[k];
    return noop;
  },
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});

  width: 750,
  height: 1334,
  getContext: () => mockCtx
};

global._storage = {};
global.wx = {
  getStorageSync: (k) => global._storage[k] || null,
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => { global._storage[k] = v; },
  removeStorageSync: (k) => { delete global._storage[k]; },
  getSystemInfoSync: () => ({ screenWidth: 375, screenHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667 }),
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 20, bottom: 647 } }),

  onWindowResize: noop,

  getSharedCanvas: () => canvasObj
};

global.canvas = canvasObj;
global.window = global;

// 载入 game.js
require('./wechat-minigame/game.js');

function initNewGame(startingYear = 100) {
  global.startCharacterCreation(false);
  global.finishCharacterCreation();
  global.state.year = startingYear;
  global.state.age = 18;
  global.state.birthYear = startingYear - 18;
  global.state.isStarted = true;
  global.state.endgameSettled = false;
  global.state.isRetired = false;
  global.state.health = 85;
  global.state.careerTenureYears = 0;
  global.state.frontierYear = 300;
  global.state.activeModal = null;
  global.state.activeModalData = null;
}
global.initNewGame = initNewGame;

console.log('====================================================');

console.log('====================================================\n');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 1: T1 & A4 存档系统 v5 与旧档归档防护
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------

global._storage = {};

// 模拟写入一个 1972 年或 mode: modern 的旧档
const legacySaveData = {
  version: 4,
  mode: 'modern',
  year: 1972,
  age: 18,
  capital: 50000,
  track: 'gov'
};
global._storage['life_sim_grand_masterpiece_save_v4'] = JSON.stringify(legacySaveData);

// 尝试强制载入

assert.strictEqual(loaded, null, '现代旧档或非 clan_season_v1 存档必须被拒绝载入并返回 null');

const archived = JSON.parse(global._storage['lifesim_legacy_archive']);
assert.strictEqual(archived.content.year, 1972, '归档数据完整保留原旧档内容');
console.log('  ✅ 旧档安全归档隔离验证通过！');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 2: T3 & C3 五种接班模式配置与加成/代价检验
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n👉 [测试 2] 校验五种接班模式（4.3 规划规格）参数与代价...');
const candidates = global.generateSuccessorCandidates();
assert.strictEqual(candidates.length, 5, '必须生成 5 种接班人模式候选');

const heirMode = candidates.find(c => c.mode === 'lineage_heir');
const adoptedMode = candidates.find(c => c.mode === 'adopted_clan');
const fosterMode = candidates.find(c => c.mode === 'foster_son');
const retainerMode = candidates.find(c => c.mode === 'retainer');
const managerMode = candidates.find(c => c.mode === 'virtuous_manager');

assert.ok(heirMode && heirMode.startingAge === 18, '宗门嫡长起步年龄必须为 18 岁');
assert.ok(adoptedMode && adoptedMode.startingAge === 20, '过继立嗣起步年龄必须为 20 岁');
assert.ok(fosterMode && fosterMode.startingAge === 20, '收养义子起步年龄必须为 20 岁');
assert.ok(retainerMode && retainerMode.startingAge === 28, '家臣辅弼起步年龄必须为 28 岁');
assert.ok(managerMode && managerMode.startingAge === 30, '贤良掌事起步年龄必须为 30 岁');

assert.ok(heirMode.bonusText.includes('声望继承 +10%'), '宗门嫡长必须具备声望继承加成');
assert.ok(adoptedMode.bonusText.includes('族产公积 +5%'), '过继立嗣必须具备族产加成');
assert.ok(fosterMode.bonusText.includes('随机才能 +1'), '收养义子必须具备随机才能加成');
assert.ok(retainerMode.bonusText.includes('起步职级 +1'), '家臣辅弼必须具备职级加成');
assert.ok(managerMode.bonusText.includes('才能最高'), '贤良掌事必须具备最高才能');
console.log('  ✅ 五种接班模式规格完整符合规划附录 4.3 表！');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 3: T3 统一寿数函数与 50 年任期上限
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n👉 [测试 3] 校验统一寿数函数 calculateAncientLifespan 与 50 年任期上限...');
assert.strictEqual(typeof global.calculateAncientLifespan, 'function', 'calculateAncientLifespan 必须全局可用');

// 先天寿数 60-74, 波动 ±8
for (let seed = 1; seed <= 50; seed++) {
  const span = global.calculateAncientLifespan(seed, 55, 0, 0, 0);
  assert.ok(span >= 52 && span <= 82, `寿数计算结果 ${span} 必须处于 52~82 允许范围`);
}
// 高体魄增寿，重疾与过劳损寿
const healthySpan = global.calculateAncientLifespan(10, 85, 0, 9, 0);
const sicklySpan = global.calculateAncientLifespan(10, 30, 9, 0, 2);
assert.ok(healthySpan > sicklySpan, `健康养生寿数 (${healthySpan}) 必须显著高于重疾过劳 (${sicklySpan})`);
console.log(`  ✅ 寿数函数表现一致: 健康样本=${healthySpan}岁, 疾笃样本=${sicklySpan}岁`);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 4: T9 & A6 & C4 周前沿 frontierYear 拦截校验
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n👉 [测试 4] 校验周前沿 frontierYear 拦截与“岁月未至”提示...');
global.initNewGame(100);
global.state.year = 130;
global.state.age = 30;
global.state.frontierYear = 130; // 当前前沿锁定在 130 年

let toastShown = null;
global.onToastPushed = (msg, type) => { toastShown = { msg, type }; };

global.tickOneYear();
assert.ok(toastShown && toastShown.msg.includes('岁月未至'), '达到前沿年份必须触发“岁月未至”提示');
assert.strictEqual(global.state.year, 130, '到达前沿后禁止继续推延年份');
console.log('  ✅ 周前沿拦截正确：年份被严格截停在 130 年！');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 5: T8 & A5 & C5 赛季大结卷年份阈值与幂等性
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n👉 [测试 5] 校验 200 年赛季大结卷阈值 (year >= 295) 与幂等性...');
global.state.year = 220;
let modalOpened = false;
global.state.activeModal = null;
toastShown = null;
global.openSeasonSettlementModal();
assert.strictEqual(global.state.activeModal, null, '公元 295 年前禁止启封赛季大结卷');
assert.ok(toastShown && toastShown.msg.includes('须至公元295年'), '未达 295 年必须弹出岁月未至提示');

// 推至 295 年
global.state.year = 295;
global.state.familyProfile = global.state.familyProfile || {
  currentSeasonId: 'season_1_han_wei',
  clanTrust: { trustFundMoney: 80000, prestigeScore: 120 },
  genealogyList: [{ name: '先祖一' }, { name: '先祖二' }]
};
global.openSeasonSettlementModal();
assert.strictEqual(global.state.activeModal, 'season_settlement', '公元 295 年后大结卷顺利启封');

// 校验幂等性：重复打开与执行
const initialTrust = global.state.familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney;
global.openSeasonSettlementModal();
assert.strictEqual(global.state.familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney, initialTrust, '重复调用结算界面不得篡改族产公积');
console.log('  ✅ 赛季大结卷开启门槛与安全幂等校验通过！');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 6: A1 & C1 四世接力 100 -> 300 年端到端全通史仿真
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n👉 [测试 6] 运行四世接力无头仿真 (100 -> 300 AD，接班模式：宗门嫡长)...');

global.initNewGame(100);
global.state.isStarted = true;
global.state.frontierYear = 300; // 开放全赛季窗口
const fam = global.ensureClanProfileForCurrentRun();
fam.genealogyList = [];

let genCount = 1;
const historyLog = [];

while (global.state.year < 300 && genCount <= 6) {
  const currentGen = genCount;
  const startYr = global.state.year;
  const startAge = global.state.age;

  // 模拟当前任家主推进岁月直至退位或寿终
  while (!global.state.endgameSettled && !global.state.isRetired && global.state.year < 300) {
    global.tickOneYear();
    // 若弹出决策或履职弹窗，使用统一策略弹窗抉择代理器自动消解
    while (global.state.activeModal && !global.state.endgameSettled && !global.state.isRetired) {
      if (typeof global.resolveCurrentModalByPolicy === 'function') {
        global.resolveCurrentModalByPolicy('first');
      } else {
        global.state.activeModal = null;
      }
    }
  }

  const endYr = global.state.year;
  const endAge = global.state.age;
  historyLog.push({ gen: currentGen, startYr, endYr, endAge, tenure: global.state.careerTenureYears || 0 });

  if (global.state.year >= 295) {
    // 赛季大结卷达成
    break;
  }

  // 执行宗门继祧交接
  const cand = global.generateSuccessorCandidates()[0]; // 宗门嫡长 (18岁)
  global.successionToNextGeneration(cand);
  global.finishCharacterCreation();
  genCount++;
}

console.log('  📊 四世接力运行履历:');
historyLog.forEach(h => {
  console.log(`     第 ${h.gen} 世: 公元 ${h.startYr} 年(起步) -> 公元 ${h.endYr} 年(卸任/寿终行年${h.endAge}岁, 主政${h.tenure}年)`);
});

assert.ok(historyLog.length >= 3 && historyLog.length <= 5, `世代数必须在 3~5 世之间 (实际: ${historyLog.length} 世)`);
assert.ok(global.state.year >= 295, `四世接力必须成功抵达公元 295~300 年终局关隘 (实际: 公元 ${global.state.year} 年)`);

// 验证出生年公式: nextBirthYear = handoverYear - startingAge
for (let i = 1; i < historyLog.length; i++) {
  const prev = historyLog[i - 1];
  const curr = historyLog[i];
  assert.strictEqual(curr.startYr, prev.endYr, `第 ${i+1} 世起步年 (${curr.startYr}) 必须紧接上一世卸任年 (${prev.endYr})`);
}
console.log('  ✅ 四世接力年代连续、出生年递进、世数 3~5 体验指标全部达标！');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 7: A2 & C2 早逝提前交接与窗口连续性
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n👉 [测试 7] 模拟家主早逝提前交接与纪元窗口连续性...');
global.initNewGame(100);
global.state.year = 145;
global.state.age = 45;
global.state.health = 8; // 强制濒危触发早逝/疾笃提前卸任

global.tickOneYear();
assert.ok(global.state.isRetired || global.state.endgameSettled, '健康濒危时必须提前触发卸任交接');

const handoverYear = global.state.year;
const nextCand = global.generateSuccessorCandidates()[3]; // 家臣辅弼 (28岁)
global.successionToNextGeneration(nextCand);
global.finishCharacterCreation();

assert.strictEqual(global.state.year, handoverYear, `继任者接手年份 (${global.state.year}) 必须精准衔接交接年 (${handoverYear})`);
assert.strictEqual(global.state.age, 28, '家臣辅弼继任者年龄必须为 28 岁');
assert.strictEqual(global.state.birthYear, handoverYear - 28, '出生年必须等于 handoverYear - startingAge');
console.log(`  ✅ 早逝提前交接测试通过: 公元 ${handoverYear} 年无缝接手，出生年为公元 ${global.state.birthYear} 年！`);

console.log('\n====================================================');
console.log('🎉 第一赛季全部自动化回归测试用例 100% 通过！质量门准入达成！');
console.log('====================================================\n');
process.exit(0);
</file>

/**
 * 华夏三千年世家大系 · 历史史实考据、统一时间线总表、真实消耗检定与前沿门槛全量测试套件
 */

const assert = require('assert');

// 1. 模拟微信小游戏环境
const noop = () => {};
const mockCanvasCalls = [];
const mockCtx = new Proxy({}, {
  get: (t, k) => {
    if (k === 'measureText') return (text) => ({ width: (text ? text.length : 0) * 12 });
    if (k === 'createLinearGradient' || k === 'createRadialGradient') return () => ({ addColorStop: noop });
    if (k === 'createPattern') return () => ({});
    if (k === 'roundRect' || k === 'fillRect' || k === 'strokeRect' || k === 'fillText') {
      return (...args) => { mockCanvasCalls.push({ method: k, args }); };
    }
    if (t[k] !== undefined) return t[k];
    return noop;
  },
  set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }
});

const canvasObj = {
  width: 750,
  height: 1334,
  getContext: () => mockCtx
};

global._storage = {};
global.wx = {
  getStorageSync: (k) => global._storage[k] || null,
  setStorageSync: (k, v) => { global._storage[k] = v; },
  removeStorageSync: (k) => { delete global._storage[k]; },
  getSystemInfoSync: () => ({ screenWidth: 375, screenHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667 }),
  getWindowInfo: () => ({ windowWidth: 375, windowHeight: 667, pixelRatio: 2, safeArea: { top: 20, bottom: 647 } }),
  getMenuButtonBoundingClientRect: () => ({ top: 24, bottom: 56, left: 280, right: 365, width: 85, height: 32 }),
  onTouchStart: noop,
  onTouchMove: noop,
  onTouchEnd: noop,
  onTouchCancel: noop,
  onShow: noop,
  onHide: noop,
  onError: noop,
  onWindowResize: noop,
  triggerGC: noop,
  createCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createOffscreenCanvas: () => canvasObj,
  createWebAudioContext: () => null
};

// 加载游戏主脚本
require('./wechat-minigame/game.js');
const {
  state,
  checkAndTriggerHanWeiHistoricalEvent,
  ensureClanProfileForCurrentRun
} = global;

const {
  HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS,
  HAN_WEI_RELICS_DATABASE
} = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/historicalMilieuHanWei');

const {
  EPOCH_ONE_HISTORICAL_EVENTS
} = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/epochOneEngine');

const {
  FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING,
  createFamilyProfile
} = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/familySystem');

const {
  SEASONS_REGISTRY,
  CHRONO_TIMELINE_REGISTRY,
  getSeasonById,
  getSeasonByIndex,
  getSeasonByYear
} = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/seasonConfig');

const {
  FUTURE_DECADE_SCENARIOS,
  checkFrontierAccessibility,
  executeFutureDecadeDecision
} = require('./wechat-minigame/src/data/futureFrontierEngine');

console.log('================================================================================');
console.log('  华夏三千年世家大系 · 史实核验、时间线总表、真实消耗与未来前沿闭环测试');
console.log('================================================================================');

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 1：两套事件写法归一化验证
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n>>> [测试 1：两套事件写法完全归一化验证]');
assert(HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS.length >= 8, '汉魏六朝关隘事件应完整收录至少8个历史节点');
assert.strictEqual(EPOCH_ONE_HISTORICAL_EVENTS.length, HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS.length, 'epochOneEngine 应引用统一的历史节点');

for (let i = 0; i < HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS.length; i++) {
  const e1 = HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS[i];
  const e2 = EPOCH_ONE_HISTORICAL_EVENTS[i];
  assert.strictEqual(e1.id, e2.id, `事件第${i}项ID一致`);
  assert.strictEqual(e1.year, e2.year, `事件第${i}项年份一致`);
  assert.strictEqual(e1.title, e2.title, `事件第${i}项标题一致`);
  assert.strictEqual(e1.options.length, e2.options.length, `事件第${i}项选项数量一致`);
}
console.log(`  ✅ ${HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS.length}大历史关隘事件在两套引擎中 100% 共享唯一真源，彻底消除割裂`);

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 2：史实与官制措辞严谨性校验
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n>>> [测试 2：史实与堂号考证严谨性校验]');

// 2.1 208年曹操官称
const chibiEvt = HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS.find(e => e.year === 208);
assert(chibiEvt, '应存在208年赤壁前夜事件');
const opt208_officer = chibiEvt.options[0];
assert(opt208_officer.successReward.officialTitle.includes('汉丞相') || opt208_officer.successReward.officialTitle.includes('丞相'),
  '208年官衔应称汉丞相府，不可出现封魏公后的魏相府错位');
assert(!opt208_officer.successReward.officialTitle.includes('魏相府'), '绝不可出现魏相府错位');
console.log(`  ✅ 208年赤壁官衔考定为【${opt208_officer.successReward.officialTitle}】，时代错位已修复`);

// 2.2 280年晋太康平吴
const taikangEvt = HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS.find(e => e.year === 280);
assert(taikangEvt, '应存在280年太康事件');
assert(taikangEvt.title.includes('太康'), '280年平吴年号应为太康');
assert(!taikangEvt.title.includes('泰始'), '280年平吴不应出现泰始年号');
console.log(`  ✅ 280年平吴标题更正为【${taikangEvt.title}】，年号考据严谨`);

// 2.3 堂号审查
assert(!FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['李'].includes('爱莲堂'), '李氏堂号绝不可包含周敦颐之爱莲堂');
assert(FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['李'].includes('青莲堂') || FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['李'].includes('陇西堂'), '李氏堂号应包含青莲堂/陇西堂');
assert(FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['周'].includes('爱莲堂'), '周氏堂号应包含爱莲堂');
assert(FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['罗'].includes('豫章堂') && FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['罗'].includes('尊尧堂'), '罗氏应包含豫章堂与尊尧堂');
assert(FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['钟'].includes('颍川堂') && FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['钟'].includes('知音堂'), '钟氏应包含颍川堂与知音堂');
assert(FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['任'].includes('乐安堂') && FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['任'].includes('玉局堂'), '任氏应包含乐安堂与玉局堂');
assert(FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['蒋'].includes('乐安堂') && FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING['蒋'].includes('钟山堂'), '蒋氏应包含乐安堂与钟山堂');
console.log('  ✅ 李、周、罗、钟、任、蒋等各大姓氏经典堂号经族谱考据，全部修正合规精准');

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 测试 3：关隘事件真实消耗、属性检定与传世宝物匹配
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('\n>>> [测试 3：关隘事件真实消耗与传世物精准匹配]');

const clan = ensureClanProfileForCurrentRun();
clan.clanTrust = {
  trustFundMoney: 10000,
  prestigeScore: 100,
  relics: []
};
state.capital = 5000;
state.age = 25;
state.activeModal = null;
state.year = 168;
state.handledEvents = {};
state.track = 'none';

const trig168 = checkAndTriggerHanWeiHistoricalEvent();
assert(trig168, '168年应触发建宁党锢');
const choices168 = state.activeDecisionEvent.choices;
assert.strictEqual(choices168.length, 3, '应包含3个选项');

// 3.1 选项 1 检定属性与门槛验证
assert.strictEqual(choices168[0].statReq, 'courage', '大开中门应检定胆魄');
assert.strictEqual(choices168[0].reqVal, 50, '大开中门门槛应为50');

// 3.2 选项 2 真实扣除资财测试
const initialCapital = state.capital;
const initialTrust = clan.clanTrust.trustFundMoney;
const optCostChoice = choices168[1]; // 连夜密送出境，消耗 1200 钱
assert(optCostChoice.costVal === 1200, '选项应明确声明消耗 1200 钱');
optCostChoice.action();

assert.strictEqual(state.capital, initialCapital - 1200, '执行后个人资金应真实扣除 1200 钱');
console.log(`  ✅ 真实代价扣减生效：从 ¥${initialCapital} 扣减 ¥1200，现为 ¥${state.capital}`);

// 3.3 选项失败代价与惩罚验证
state.year = 184;
state.activeModal = null;
state.handledEvents = {};
checkAndTriggerHanWeiHistoricalEvent();
const choices184 = state.activeDecisionEvent.choices;
const optDefend = choices184[0];
assert(optDefend.failEffects && optDefend.failEffects.capital, '守城失败应具备惩罚效果');
optDefend.onFail();
console.log(`  ✅ 失败惩罚机制生效：守城未果时正常触发代价回响与宗门惩戒`);

// 3.4 传世物精确匹配（杜绝永远下发索引0焦尾琴）
state.year = 220;
state.activeModal = null;
state.handledEvents = {};
checkAndTriggerHanWeiHistoricalEvent();
const choices220 = state.activeDecisionEvent.choices;
const optJiupin = choices220[0]; // 上品门第，奖励 relic_jiupin_gold_tablet
optJiupin.action();

assert(clan.clanTrust.relics.length > 0, '应获得传世重宝');
const grantedRelic = clan.clanTrust.relics[clan.clanTrust.relics.length - 1];
assert.strictEqual(grantedRelic.id, 'relic_jiupin_gold_tablet', '获得的宝物应为九品金策，而非硬编码的焦尾琴');
console.log(`  ✅ 传世宝物精准发放到位：【${grantedRelic.name}】（ID: ${grantedRelic.id}）`);

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
